Result: （1）提示词交互在不含任务的读出器上以完整幅度出现：9个条件token就能大范围移动不动点比例、改变结构类别并重排模型排序；（2）所有提出的解释因素均失败：前缀长度效应非单调，四种现象学因素在扩大样本后即被推翻，注意力汇聚机制仅在2/5模型上预测对符号（随机水平）；（3）一个固定的9-token前缀驱动4个模型趋于0、2个趋于1，双向性在分布内起点上仍存在。

Conclusion: 在该读出器上，解释的基本单元是"提示词-模型"配对而非提示词本身。作者指出其反复犯下的方法论错误是：用一个具有形状的标准去衡量一个没有变化空间的量。

Abstract: That a prompt's effect is not a property of the prompt is established: prompts optimised for one model degrade on another, and rankings reorder under neutral reformatting. That evidence is about task accuracy, which cannot say whether the interaction is a fact about task machinery or about the conditional distribution itself. We ask on a readout with no task in it: the fixed-point structure of the short-window argmax map x_{t+1} = argmax_x p(x | x_{t-1}, x_t), censused from 96 starts. It is deterministic, so nothing can be helped or hurt, and it exists only at short windows -- four of six models lose it entirely by window 16 -- so everything here concerns how a model reads a fragment. Two results. First, the interaction reaches this readout at full magnitude: nine tokens of conditioning move the fixed-point fraction across most of its range, change a four-way structural class, and reorder models, while instruction tuning worth 60.5 IFEval points moves the class by zero. Second, nothing we proposed carries it. Prefix length fails: the effect is not monotone. Four phenomenological factors -- prose-versus-markup, a universal direction, bidirectionality, instruct-resistance -- were each withdrawn within one run of being proposed, dissolved by widening the sample. And the nearest mechanistic account, attention-sink dominance of early tokens, predicts the sign of the shift on 2 of 5 models -- chance -- while a length-by-content cross shows it holds on real text and fails on our probe's uniformly random input, so we are outside its regime, not against it. One fixed nine-token prefix drives four models toward 0 and two toward 1; the bidirectionality survives in-distribution starts. On this readout the unit of explanation is the prompt-model pair. The recurring error it caught in us has a name: a criterion with a shape applied to a quantity with no room to vary.

</details>


### [70] [Move by Move: Measuring and Steering How LLMs Conduct Psychotherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21325)
*Afonso Baldo,Hugo Pitorro,Areti Vassilopoulos,Anabela C. Areias,Maya D'Eon,Fabíola Costa,Ricardo Rei,Nuno M. Guerreiro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个包含十种治疗性动作（therapeutic moves）的本体论，基于MULTI-60清单构建，通过五位持证心理学家的标注活动验证，并用与专家一致性匹配的基于评判者的方法进行规模化。应用于真实咨询记录和模型引导的对话后，比较了人类临床医生与前沿模型面板的动作分布。发现模型过度使用探究（高达人类三倍）、忽视心理教育，且强烈依赖上下文锚定。将本体论作为工具集暴露给模型后，平均偏差减半，回合级对齐提升7-9个百分点，且无需微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户越来越多地转向大语言模型寻求情感支持，但人们对这些模型实际如何进行心理治疗互动知之甚少。研究者希望系统性地分析和量化模型在心理治疗场景中的行为模式，从而为改进模型的情感支持能力提供基础。

Method: 1) 构建一个包含十种治疗性动作的本体论，基于MULTI-60清单，具有紧凑且基于功能的特点；2) 通过五位持证心理学家的标注活动验证本体论；3) 使用与专家一致性匹配的基于评判者的方法进行大规模标注；4) 将本体论应用到真实咨询记录和模型引导的对话中，比较人类临床医生和前沿模型的动作分布；5) 将本体论作为工具集暴露给模型，观察行为改进。

Result: 模型过度使用探究动作（高达人类速率的三倍），忽视心理教育，并且强烈依赖上下文锚定——它们会延续人类临床医生开启的策略，但很少主动发起。将本体论作为工具集暴露后，平均偏差从人类动作分布减半，回合级与人类治疗师的对齐提升7-9个百分点，且无需任何微调。

Conclusion: 通过本体论工具的暴露，无需微调即可显著改善LLM在心理治疗互动中与人类治疗师的行为一致性，这表明可解释的、基于领域知识的工具提示是一种有效的轻量级干预方式，可以引导模型更接近专业的心理治疗实践。

Abstract: Users increasingly turn to large language models for emotional support, yet little is known about how these models actually conduct a psychotherapy interaction. We introduce an ontology of ten therapeutic moves: compact, function-based categories grounded in the MULTI-60 inventory, validated through an annotation campaign with five licensed psychologists, and scaled with a judge-based approach that matches expert agreement. Applying it to real counseling transcripts and model-led sessions, we compare the move distributions between human clinicians and a panel of frontier models. Models over-use inquiry at up to three times the human rate, neglect psychoeducation, and are strongly context-anchored: they carry forward strategies initiated by a human clinician but rarely initiate them themselves. Exposing the ontology as a set of tools roughly halves the mean deviation from the human move distribution and improves turn-level alignment with human therapist by 7-9 percentage points, without any fine-tuning.

</details>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [71] [Uncertainty propagation in auto-regressive random neural network models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20483)
*Janice Adams,Daniele Venturi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文开发了用于随机神经网络模型中不确定性传播的分析和基于粒子的方法，其中输入和网络参数都允许是随机的。利用Leaky ReLU激活函数的分段线性结构，推导了网络输出对输入和参数扰动的局部近似，该近似在激活模式保持不变时是精确的。该方法可以计算网络输出的概率密度函数和特征函数的解析表达式，以及其均值和协方差的闭式近似。

作者还将该不确定性传播框架扩展到由随机神经网络表示的自主动力系统，通过重复应用该映射定义了自回归模型，并推导了在时间和状态中传播不确定性的递归方程，明确考虑了状态与参数之间的交叉协方差。在Lorenz-63系统和Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上的数值实验证明了该方法在可预测性范围内具有准确的不确定性传播能力，且适用于高维动力系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机神经网络模型在实际应用中广泛存在（如贝叶斯神经网络、随机初始化的网络等），但当前缺乏有效的不确定性传播方法。现有方法要么依赖昂贵的蒙特卡洛采样，要么对网络结构和激活函数有过强假设，难以处理随机输入和随机参数同时存在的情况，更难以扩展到由随机神经网络驱动的动力系统。

Method: 1) 利用Leaky ReLU激活函数的分段线性结构，推导网络输出相对输入和参数扰动的局部近似；2) 当激活模式保持不变时，该近似是精确的；3) 基于此近似推导网络输出的概率密度函数、特征函数、均值和协方差的解析或闭式表达式；4) 将框架扩展到自主动力系统，重复应用随机神经网络映射形成自回归模型；5) 推导递归方程以传播状态和网络参数随时间的不确定性，显式考虑状态-参数交叉协方差。

Result: 在Lorenz-63系统和Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上的数值实验表明，该方法能够在可预测性范围内实现准确的不确定性传播，并且能够适用于高维动力系统。

Conclusion: 论文提出的框架为随机神经网络的输入-参数联合不确定性传播提供了解析和高效的计算工具，并能扩展到由随机神经网络表示的自治动力系统中，兼顾了精度和可扩展性。

Abstract: We develop analytical and particle-based methods for uncertainty propagation in random neural network models, where both the inputs and network parameters are allowed to be random. Building on the piecewise-linear structure of the Leaky ReLU activation function, we derive a local approximation of the neural network output with respect to perturbations in both its inputs and parameters. This approximation is exact for perturbations that preserve the network activation pattern, and it allows us to compute analytical expressions for the probability density function and characteristic function of the network output, together with closed-form approximations for its mean and covariance. We extend this uncertainty propagation framework to autonomous dynamical systems whose one-step evolution map is represented by a random neural network. Repeated application of this map defines an autoregressive model, for which we derive recursive equations to propagate uncertainty in both the state and network parameters over time. These equations explicitly account for the state-parameter cross-covariance that develops under successive iterations of the network. Numerical experiments on the Lorenz-63 system and the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation demonstrate accurate uncertainty propagation through the predictability horizon and the applicability of the proposed framework to high-dimensional dynamical systems.

Result: 建立了具体分数估计的极小极大下界，提出匹配的MLE阈值化估计器，上下界在常数和对数因子内匹配；证明了SEDD在均匀和掩码扩散模型下可达到近乎最优的极小极大样本复杂度（以目标分布与生成分布之间的KL散度衡量）。

Conclusion: 本文从统计极限的角度为离散扩散模型（尤其是SEDD）的分数估计提供了理论基础，证明了在均匀和掩码扩散模型下，SEDD可以实现近乎最优的样本效率，确立了离散扩散模型的统计最优性。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models have demonstrated strong performance across a range of datasets, including natural language data and graph-structured data. Among many variants, score-entropy discrete diffusion (SEDD) has achieved particularly strong empirical results. In SEDD, new samples are generated by iteratively evaluating a sequence of concrete score functions, which are learned by minimizing a score-entropy loss.
  While much of the prior theoretical literature on discrete diffusion has focused on the sampling efficiency of SEDD under the assumption of small score estimation error, recent work has begun to investigate the finite-sample properties of score estimation itself. In this work, we take a different route by investigating the fundamental statistical limits of concrete score estimation. We focus on uniform and masking discrete diffusions, two of the most widely adopted discrete diffusion models. We establish a minimax lower bound under the score-entropy loss, and propose an MLE-based thresholding estimator that matches this lower bound up to constant and polylogarithmic factors that depend on neighboring density ratios. We further show that, for any target distribution, this density ratio is naturally controlled under both uniform and masking discrete diffusion models, yielding nearly matching minimax lower and upper bounds for the aggregated score estimation error. Our results imply that, with appropriate initialization and discretization, SEDD can achieve nearly optimal minimax sample complexity, as measured by the KL divergence between the target and generated distributions.

</details>

Method: 作者将调查统计中的有效样本量概念推广到阈值估计场景。核心洞察是：阈值所需的"有效样本量"取决于聚类分数落在阈值同一侧的一致程度（而非数值相似度）。论文证明了一个闭式定律，刻画有效样本量以及实际部署系统中覆盖率散布的解析表达式。

Result: 得出三个关键结论：(1) 共形文献中现有的修正量是错误的，且可能在两个方向上出错；(2) 数据集没有单一的有效样本量，每个阈值水平对应一个不同的值；(3) 相关性损害在多次运行平均的覆盖率中不可见，但对单次部署者影响完全显现。在25,028个样本的校准集上，实测可靠性仅相当于约1,300个独立样本。

Conclusion: 校准数据的相关性会严重削弱基于分位数阈值的可靠性保证，现有修正方法不适用于阈值场景。数据集的"有效样本量"应视为阈值水平的函数而非固定常数。这一发现对共形预测、弃权系统、安全过滤等广泛应用的可靠性承诺有深远影响。

Abstract: Many machine-learning systems set a threshold at a quantile of a calibration set: conformal predictors that promise 90% coverage by drawing their cutoff at the calibration set's 90th percentile, abstention gates that decline to answer when a model's score falls below the calibration set's tenth percentile, safety filters that block any output scoring above the 99th percentile of a reference set. All of them promise that the threshold will hold at the stated rate on new data. The promise assumes the calibration examples are independent, and in modern pipelines they usually are not: they share a prompt, a document, a reasoning trace. Survey statistics has known how to discount correlated data since 1965, by counting how many independent observations a sample is worth, but only for averages. We show that a threshold needs a different count. The count depends on how often clustered scores land on the same side of the threshold, and that changes with where the threshold is set. How similar the scores are as numbers does not enter. We prove a closed-form law for the resulting effective sample size and for the spread of the coverage a deployed system actually sees.
  Three consequences follow. The correction now used in the conformal literature is the wrong quantity, and can miss in either direction. A dataset has no single effective sample size. It has one for each level the threshold is set at. And the damage is invisible in coverage averaged over many runs, and fully felt by whoever deploys once. On a released calibration set of 25,028 examples, we measure the reliability of about 1,300.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原Marketron模型的十八参数空间存在结构不可辨识性，求解器陷入次优局部极小，且经济量无法测量，需要降低模型复杂度以恢复参数可辨识性与经济解释力。

Method: 通过三类简化手段降维：去除精确尺度规范与符号对称、依据明确准则冻结非金融参数、绝热消去快速隐藏信号，从而得到九参数简化模型；采用Gauss-Newton Hessian零空间分析与流形边界分析验证无进一步简化空间；引入流动与收益创新的扩散相关性捕捉短期限偏斜；实施从物理测度到风险中性测度的分阶段校准。

Result: 九参数简化模型在SPX期权表面上以单参数集成功拟合整个曲面；首次实现流动风险市场价格的识别（虽仅弱约束其水平）；信号与记忆弛豫率在SPX表面上分离良好但两者均弱识别，将市场置于驱动的非平衡态。

Conclusion: 简化模型消除了原模型的结构不可辨识性，将主动物质（active-matter）解读从类比转化为可证伪的约束，确立了市场处于驱动非平衡态的地位，并建立了扩散相关性与短期限偏斜之间的机制联系。

Abstract: The Marketron model of \cite{HalperinItkin2025Mark} and its option pricing extension in \cite{HalperinItkinMarketron2} suffer from structural non-identifiability: an eighteen-parameter space traps solvers in suboptimal local minima and renders economic quantities unmeasurable. By removing exact scaling gauges and sign symmetries, freezing non-financial parameters by explicit criteria, and adiabatically eliminating the fast hidden signal, we derive a robust nine-parameter reduced model. A Gauss-Newton Hessian with empty null space and a manifold-boundary analysis confirm that the reduced core carries no exact symmetry and admits no further reduction. A diffusive correlation between flow and return innovations captures the short-maturity skew. A staged calibration from the physical measure to the risk-neutral measure, illustrated on SPX options, fits the whole surface with a single parameter set. The same reduction turns the wedge between the physical and pricing values of the flow block into a well-defined market price of flow risk rather than a ridge artifact, identifiable here for the first time, though a single surface constrains its level only weakly. Finally, our analysis reveals that in the Marketron model the log-price obeys a generalized Langevin equation with a closed-form, state-modulated memory kernel, and that the memory variable itself is the exact Markovian lift of this kernel. This mapping also yields a testable condition, the equality of the signal and memory relaxation rates, which on the SPX surface come out well separated, though both weakly identified, placing the fitted market tentatively in the driven, non-equilibrium regime and turning the active-matter reading from an analogy into a falsifiable constraint.

</details>

Abstract: Ethereum transactions admit a canonical structural form. Each execution trace is built into an abstract syntax tree of token transfers grouped by call-frame nesting and reduced by a convergent term rewriting system of 15 rules to a unique canonical form. The system is terminating, sound, and confluent, and the induced structural equivalence on fund flows is decidable. All five properties are mechanized in Rocq with zero admitted obligations. The canonical form makes structural questions about fund flows decidable, opening the way to strategy-family classification, bot fingerprinting, and equivalence-based attribution. In this paper, we demonstrate the canonical form on arbitrage detection: cycles emerge at fixpoint and are read off the canonical form, with no protocol-specific patterns. The pipeline depends only on the standard ERC token and WETH ABIs and no protocol-specific events, so the same binary runs unmodified on Arbitrum and BSC. We evaluate on 220 000 Ethereum blocks against Eigenphi (production MEV platform) and on 1 000 shared blocks against ArbiNet (GNN classifier). The system produces 469 801 confirmed detections and 245 497 attempted arbitrages; across all detections it agrees with Eigenphi on 83.5% and covers 81% of ArbiNet, while surfacing 60 199 exclusive confirmed detections. 99.2% of all detections are produced by the fixpoint alone and are sound by construction. Manual validation of 500 transactions finds no false positives in the confirmed tier. Forensic reanalysis of 200 Eigenphi-exclusive detections finds 63.5% have no cycle in canonical form; 9.0% have cycles the fixpoint detects but our conservative classifier does n

Abstract: We study causal moderation when treatment assignment is randomized but the moderator is not. *Zhicheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于四个关键区分（预训练与部署系统、学习分布与样本、三种记忆类型、任务能力与主体性）的最小工作模型，用于诊断关于LLM的六个常见误解，并应用于出版方AI政策作为治理案例研究，从而超越"鹦鹉-心智"二元对立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于LLM的公开讨论仍被民间理论（folk theories）所主导，如"只是自动补全"或"涌现代理"等口号各执一词，将部分特征误认为整体。缺乏一个系统化的分析框架来诊断这些误解，并指导能力评估、系统设计与治理实践。

Method: 提出一个最小工作模型，建立在四个关键区分之上：(1)预训练系统与部署系统；(2)学习到的分布与特定样本；(3)参数化记忆、上下文记忆与外部记忆；(4)任务能力与主体性。基于此模型诊断六个常见误解（下一个词预测、均值回归、训练数据复述、模型记忆、对齐、理解），并以出版方AI政策作为治理案例进行应用分析。

Result: 该框架成功诊断了六个关于LLM的误解，识别出每个误解正确之处、混淆的区分以及相应的纠正方向。在治理层面，通过分析出版方AI政策展示了政策语言如何误混淆这些区分，以及如何修正这些错误。

Conclusion: 将LLM视为话语与任务表现的模拟器（simulators of discourse and task performance），而非简单地落入"鹦鹉"或"心智"的二元对立。该模型提供了一个诊断工具包，用于定位和纠正当下民间理论所延续的系统性错误，从而为能力评估、系统设计与治理提供更精确的分析基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now embedded in scientific, educational, and governance workflows, with debates centering on their capabilities, mechanisms, and impacts. Yet these debates remain structured by persistent folk theories--intuitive, informal explanatory models that guide attitudes and actions. Deflationary slogans ("just autocomplete," "stochastic parrots," and "average of the internet") and anthropomorphic framings ("emergent agents" and "proto-minds") each capture genuine features of current systems but mistake those features for the whole. This Perspective proposes a minimal working model of LLM-based systems centered on four distinctions: between pretraining and deployed systems; between the learned distribution and particular samples; among parametric, contextual, and external memory; and between task competence and agency. The model is used to diagnose six misconceptions about LLMs: next-token prediction, regression to the mean, training-data regurgitation, model memory, alignment, and understanding. For each, the analysis identifies what the misconception gets right, which distinctions it conflates, and what follows for capability evaluation, system design, and governance. Applied to publisher AI policies as governance case studies, the framework shows both how policy language can conflate these distinctions and how such errors can be corrected. The model thereby avoids the parrot-mind binary by treating LLMs as simulators of discourse and task performance, offering a diagnostic toolkit for locating and correcting the errors these folk theories perpetuate.

</details>


### [78] [From Urban Mobility to Epidemic Dynamics: A Mixture-of-Experts Framework with Preference Alignment for Policy Scenario Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20512)
*Yun Ye,Arsalan Dezhkam,Junyuan Liu,Xinglei Wang,Tao Cheng*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出UrbanShare-MoE-PA框架，将非药物干预（NPI）政策日历转化为个体层面的每日时间分配轨迹，并通过校准的SEIR模拟器评估疫情与活动的权衡。基于新加坡911个代理的数据，发现政策日历到行为转化的方式会显著影响疫情-活动结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NPI情景分析仅关注总体流动性减少，忽略了行为重新分配对疫情风险的影响。政策分析需要一种行为层，将替代政策日历转化为合理的活动和流动性轨迹，才能进行下游疫情模拟。现有研究缺乏这种可解释的、自下而上的行为模型衔接政策时机与疫情传播。

Method: 构建UrbanShare-MoE-PA框架，包含：(1) 行为引擎将每个代理-天分解为出行份额、条件于停留的POI类别分配、条件于出行的交通方式分配；(2) 结合结构化UrbanShare基线、用于异构POI和方式响应的混合专家头、以及相位感知偏好对齐（用于日历条件滚动）；(3) 将行为输出传播到校准的行为驱动SEIR模拟器中。

Result: UrbanShare-MoE改善POI重建，UrbanShare-MoE-PA实现最低的交通方式误差和最清晰的替代日历轨迹。SEIR模拟显示：早期封锁降低感染负担，晚期封锁增加感染负担，短封锁在仅适度增加疫情负担的情况下保持最高加权活动水平。

Conclusion: 疫情-活动结论取决于政策日历如何转化为行为；代理级流动性份额建模提供了政策时机、行为与下游模拟之间可解释的桥梁。

Abstract: Non-pharmaceutical interventions (NPIs) alter epidemic risk through behavioral reallocations, not simply aggregate mobility reductions. Scenario-based NPI analysis therefore requires a behavioral layer that translates alternative policy calendars into plausible activity and mobility trajectories before downstream outcomes are simulated. We introduce UrbanShare-MoE-PA, a data-driven agent-level framework that maps factual and alternative NPI calendars to daily time-allocation trajectories and propagates them through a calibrated behavior-driven SEIR simulator. The behavioral engine decomposes each agent-day into travel share, POI-category allocation conditional on staying, and travel-mode allocation conditional on traveling. It combines a structured UrbanShare baseline, mixture-of-experts heads for heterogeneous POI and mode responses, and phase-aware preference alignment for calendar-conditioned rollouts. Using data from 911 agents in Singapore observed from March to August 2020, we evaluate factual reconstruction, four alternative lockdown calendars, and epidemic-activity trade-offs. UrbanShare-MoE improves POI reconstruction over the baseline, while UrbanShare-MoE-PA achieves the lowest travel-mode errors and the clearest alternative-calendar trajectories. In the calibrated SEIR simulation, early lockdown lowers infectious burden, late lockdown increases it, and short lockdown preserves the highest weighted activity with only a modest increase in epidemic burden relative to the original policy. These results show that epidemic-activity conclusions depend on how policy calendars are translated into behavior, and that agent-level mobility-share modeling provides an interpretable bridge between policy timing, behavior, and downstream simulation.

</details>


### [79] [ExploraTwin, a Non-Profit Research Platform for Digital Twin Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20539)
*Naveen Venkatanarayanan,Yuchen Qiu,Tianyi Peng,George Gui,Olivier Toubia*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍ExploraTwin，一个开放获取、非营利的数字孪生调查模拟研究平台，支持调查模式和面板模式两种工作流，并通过CroissantTwin标准化数据格式扩展数字孪生样本。平台在Twin-2K-500数据集上复现了19项实验，首次运行即获得99.6%的结构有效响应率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生模拟虽有前景，但现有证据表明其在特定情境部署前需要先进行测试。为降低研究者和实践者测试与部署数字孪生模拟的门槛，需要一个低摩擦的研究平台。

Method: 构建ExploraTwin平台，包含两种模式：调查模式（上传Qualtrics问卷或平台内创建、选择数字孪生样本、配置运行模拟并导出数据）和面板模式（小规模孪生小组进行开放式对话、文档标注和主持式小组讨论）；同时开发CroissantTwin标准化数据格式以支持向平台添加数字孪生样本。

Result: 通过复现Twin-2K-500数据集中的19项实验验证了调查模式工作流，平台在197,000个答案单元中首次运行即达到99.6%的结构有效响应率，表明执行保真度很高。

Conclusion: ExploraTwin作为开放研究平台，为数字孪生模拟的测试与部署提供了标准化、可访问的基础设施，其高保真度验证结果支持该平台在实际研究场景中的可用性。

Abstract: Digital twin simulations show promise, but current empirical evidence suggests that the approach should be tested before being deployed in any particular context. To lower the friction for researchers and practitioners to test and deploy digital twin simulations, this brief commentary introduces ExploraTwin (https://exploratwin.org), an open-access, non-profit research platform for digital twin survey simulations. ExploraTwin supports two modes. In survey mode, researchers can upload a Qualtrics survey file or create a survey within the platform; select an available sample of digital twins; configure and run the simulation, and export analysis-ready data. In panel mode, researchers can assemble a small group of twins for open-ended conversations, document annotation, and moderated, focus-group-style voice discussions. We also developed CroissantTwin, a standardized data format for adding samples of digital twins to the platform. We demonstrate the survey mode workflow by using the platform to replicate 19 experiments on digital twins from the Twin-2K-500 dataset. ExploraTwin's survey execution fidelity is high: 99.6% of 197,000 answer units returned a structurally valid response on the first run.

</details>


### [80] [Interaction Effects Between Learner Characteristics and Dialogue Format in TTS Dialogue-Based Lessons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20822)
*Fumie Watanabe,Tota Suko,Takashi Ishida,Yuko Kuma,Manabu Kobayashi,Shigeichi Hirasawa,Gendo Kumoi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究调查了学习者特征如何影响三种由大语言模型(LLM)和文本转语音(TTS)技术生成的对话式课程(师生对话、生生对话、师师对话)中的动机、学习成果和整体评价。基于222名高一学生的重复测量设计，使用线性混合效应模型分析，发现学习者特征(体验式学习风格和批判性思维倾向)与对话格式在ARCS动机上存在显著交互作用，表明对话格式应根据学习者特征来选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM和TTS技术的普及，对话式AI课程日益流行，但对话格式(教师-学生、学生-学生、教师-教师)与学习者个体差异(如学习风格和批判性思维倾向)之间的匹配效应尚不明确，需要实证研究来指导个性化学习设计。

Method: 对222名高一学生采用重复测量设计，让每位学生体验三种对话格式(师生、生生、师师)的TTS对话式课程；测量学习者的体验式学习风格(CE和RCE因子)和批判性思维倾向；使用线性混合效应模型分析对话格式与学习者特征对ARCS动机、学习成果和整体评价的交互效应。

Result: 结果显示：对话格式与学习者特征在ARCS动机上存在显著交互作用——CE因子在师师格式下的积极效应强于师生格式，而RCE因子在师师格式下的积极效应相对较弱；学习成果方面，CE和RCE因子与对话格式的交互作用呈边缘显著趋势；整体评价上无显著交互作用，但师师格式的整体评价显著低于师生格式，与动机上的积极效应模式相悖。所有显著交互作用的效应量均为小到中等。

Conclusion: 对话格式应根据学习者特征进行选择，以实现更好的TTS对话式学习效果。但由于效应量较小，这些发现应被视为个性化学习设计的初步证据，尚需进一步研究验证。

Abstract: This study examined how learner characteristics affect motivation, learning outcomes, and overall evaluation in three types of dialogue-based lessons---(1) teacher--student, (2) student--student, and (3) teacher--teacher---generated using a large language model (LLM) and Text-to-Speech (TTS) technology. In particular, we focused on the interaction effects between dialogue format and learners' experiential learning style (the Concrete Experience factor, CE; and the factor of active experimentation through reflective observation and abstract conceptualization, RCE) and critical thinking disposition. Using a repeated-measures design with 222 first-year high school students, we analyzed the data with linear mixed-effects models. The results showed a significant interaction between learner characteristics and dialogue format for ARCS-based motivation. Specifically, the effect of the CE factor on motivation was more strongly positive in the teacher--teacher format than in the teacher--student format, whereas the positive effect of the RCE factor was relatively weaker in the teacher--teacher format. For learning outcomes, the interactions between dialogue format and both the CE and RCE factors showed a trend toward significance. No significant interaction emerged for overall evaluation; however, the overall evaluation of the teacher--teacher format was significantly lower than that of the teacher--student format, a pattern that diverged from the positive effect observed for motivation. These results suggest that dialogue format should be selected according to learner characteristics in TTS dialogue-based lessons. Because the effect sizes of the significant interactions were all small to medium, however, the findings of this study should be regarded as preliminary evidence for the design of personalized learning.

</details>


### [81] [Fine-tuning LLMs for Tourist Trajectory Prediction using Field Experiment Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20830)
*Tatsuya Amano,Hirozumi Yamaguchi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）作为旅游目的地游客行为预测模型，通过微调让模型理解天气、疲劳等上下文相关的决策因素。在日本的566条轨迹数据上验证，微调后的Llama-3.1-8B在下一个POI预测上达到49.1%的准确率，并在雨天等欠采样场景下保持良好表现，展示了良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的移动性干预评估方法难以预测游客在多变条件下的行为，因为游客决策高度依赖上下文（如天气、疲劳），而传统模型无法泛化到未观测的场景。需要一种能够编码常识性人类行为知识并处理异构信息的解决方案。

Method: 利用大语言模型在预训练中编码的关于人类行为的常识性知识来推理上下文相关的决策，通过自然语言表示灵活整合异构信息，并对局部轨迹数据进行微调，使模型适应目的地特定的行为模式。

Result: 在日本的566条和歌山城公园轨迹上验证，微调后的Llama-3.1-8B实现了49.1%的下一个POI预测准确率，并在雨天等欠采样场景下保持强劲表现，证明其有效泛化能力。

Conclusion: LLM能够作为高保真度的行为模型用于上下文相关的游客预测，为移动性干预的反事实分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: Evaluating mobility interventions at tourist destinations requires predicting visitor behavior under varying conditions. Traditional methods struggle because tourist decisions depend heavily on context like weather and fatigue, yet models cannot generalize to unobserved scenarios. Large Language Models offer a solution by encoding commonsense knowledge about human behavior from pretraining, enabling reasoning about context-dependent decisions, while natural language representation flexibly integrates heterogeneous information. Fine-tuning on local trajectories adapts this general understanding to destination-specific patterns. We validate this approach using 566 trajectories from Wakayama Castle Park, Japan. Our fine-tuned Llama-3.1-8B achieves 49.1% next POI accuracy and maintains strong performance on undersampled scenarios like rainy days, demonstrating effective generalization. This establishes LLMs as high-fidelity behavior models for context-dependent tourist prediction, providing groundwork for counterfactual analysis of mobility interventions.

</details>


### [82] [Atom Learning Model (ALM): how a real classroom got tokenised](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21106)
*Philipp Bogdan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出原子学习模型（ALM），将学校课程分词化为1934个学习原子和4616条前置条件链接，用统一图结构同时表示题目与儿童能力，无需为任何一方拟合难度参数即可判断题目是否适合儿童。系统在两家英国中学部署七周，自动生成了6648道题，每道成本26便士。但四项测量结果出乎意料，且部署中最深的题目仅两步前置深度，核心假设未被验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统教育系统需要为题目和儿童分别拟合难度参数，成本高且难以规模化。作者试图证明：仅通过将课程结构化为原子和前置链接，就能用纯算术方式实现个性化出题，无需任何人工编写或参数拟合，从而大幅降低教育技术部署成本。

Method: 将两本中学数学教科书机器读取为1934个原子（每原子为单步可完成的学习行为），由机器生成4616条前置链接形成课程图。题目表示为原子集合及其全部下层原子，儿童能力为图上每个原子的0-1分数，题目适配度通过单一索引的算术计算得出，两侧均不拟合难度参数。以757页阅读成本£55、整体构建成本£615-£1230部署系统。

Result: 系统在两家英国中学为373名儿童在七周内生成了6648道题，每道成本26便士。四项反预期发现：成本集中在链接而非页面；语言模型对题目难度标注与实测通过率的秩相关仅为-0.0123，无法判断难度；儿童在每题耗时从3秒增至7秒时停止学习；部署中从未提供超过两步前置深度的题目，核心前提停留在“未证伪”而非“已证实”状态。

Conclusion: ALM证明了无需拟合难度参数的课程结构化出题在经济上可行且能低成本部署，但最关键的中心前提（深层前置推理的判别力）因部署深度不足而未被证实。研究揭示了四个反直觉现象，为后续在教育技术、语言模型难度感知和儿童行为动力学的进一步研究提供了方向。

Abstract: The Atom Learning Model (ALM) tokenises a school curriculum. Two secondary mathematics textbooks were read by machine into 1,934 atoms, each one thing a learner can do in a single step, ordered by 4,616 machine-written prerequisite links. Both sides of a lesson are then expressed in that one structure: a question is a set of atoms plus everything beneath them, a child's ability is a score between 0 and 1 on every atom of the same graph, and whether a question suits a child is arithmetic over one index, with no difficulty parameter fitted for either side. Nobody wrote an atom, a link or a question. Reading the 757 pages cost £55, building the whole structure cost between £615 and £1,230, and against it the system composed 6,648 questions for 373 children in two English secondary schools over seven weeks, at 26p per composed question. Four measurements went against expectation. The cost is in the links, not the pages. The composer's own difficulty label has a rank correlation of -0.0123 with measured facility, so a language model shown a question cannot say how hard it is. Children stop working when a mark takes seven seconds instead of three. And the deployment never served a question deeper than two prerequisite steps, which is exactly where the central premise becomes testable, leaving it unfalsified rather than confirmed.

</details>


### [83] [Invisible Agents, Uninformed Patients: Towards Responsible Deployment Of Autonomous AI Diagnostic Agents In Sub-Saharan Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21326)
*Percy Brown,Kweku Yamoah*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨撒哈拉以南非洲地区自主AI诊断代理的部署速度已超出治理基础设施能力，指出现有AI问责与可解释性框架多以临床医生为中心，缺乏患者在知情同意和问责方面的结构性视角。论文综合现有研究并基于坦桑尼亚、赞比亚和加纳三个部署案例，提出三项基础原则：代理感知的知情同意、作为结构性要求的人工覆盖、以及情境适配的可解释性，为低资源环境下的AI治理提供最低实践标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撒哈拉以南非洲的eHealth平台正快速部署自主AI诊断代理，但缺乏相应的治理基础设施。现有AI问责、透明度和可解释性框架主要面向临床医生，并预设了低资源环境中并不普遍存在的监管条件。文献中缺乏以患者为中心的分析，尤其是患者对自主代理认知不足所导致的结构性问责缺口。

Method: 论文通过综合现有关于知情同意、算法问责和可解释AI的研究，结合三个有记录的部署案例（坦桑尼亚计算机辅助结核检测、赞比亚糖尿病视网膜病变和结核筛查、加纳移动健康聊天机器人分诊），展示这些问责缺口的现实存在性，并据此提出三项基础原则。

Result: 研究识别出自主AI代理在撒哈拉以南非洲部署中引入的三项独特挑战，并通过三个实际部署案例证明这些问责缺口已存在于该地区的活跃部署中。

Conclusion: 论文提出"代理感知知情同意、人工覆盖作为结构性要求、情境适配可解释性"三原则组合，作为在正式AI监管尚不成熟的背景下，为开发者、卫生系统管理者和政策制定者设立的实践最低标准。

Abstract: Autonomous AI diagnostic agents, systems that analyse patient-specific clinical data and produce diagnostic outputs or triage decisions without mandatory real-time human review, are increasingly deployed across eHealth platforms in sub-Saharan Africa at a pace that has outrun the governance infrastructure needed to oversee them. While significant bodies of work address AI accountability, transparency and explainability in healthcare, existing frameworks are largely clinician-centered and assume regulatory conditions that do not uniformly exist in low-resource settings. A patient-centered analysis of the disparity in patient awareness regarding autonomous agents, which results in a structural accountability gap, is mostly missing from the literature. This paper synthesizes existing research on informed consent, algorithmic accountability, and explainable AI to highlight three distinct challenges introduced by deploying AI agents in the sub-Saharan African context. Drawing on three documented deployment cases, including computer-aided tuberculosis detection in Tanzania, diabetic retinopathy and TB screening in Zambia, and mobile health chat-bot triage in Ghana, it demonstrates that these gaps are already present in active deployments across the region. In response, the paper proposes three foundational principles; agent-aware informed consent, human override as a structural requirement and contextually adapted explainability. This triad of principles lays a practical minimum standard for developers, health system administrators and policymakers in contexts where formal AI regulation remains nascent.

</details>

### [84] [Bankruptcy Prediction via Hybrid Resampling and Stacking Ensemble Techniques with Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI)-Driven Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20343)
*Obu-Amoah Ampomah,Edmund Fosu Agyemang,Kofi Acheampong,Louis Agyekum,Enock Adu Bonsu,Eric Nyarko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出并评估了一个整合共识特征选择、混合重采样、堆叠集成和可解释人工智能的破产预测框架，用于处理严重不平衡的金融数据。基于台湾破产预测数据集，通过SVM-SMOTE、SMOTE-Tomek和SMOTE-ENN三种重采样策略，对比五种机器学习集成模型和五种深度学习模型，并构建混合堆叠集成。结果显示重采样策略显著影响模型行为，SMOTE-ENN在少数类检测上表现最佳；独立模型中GRU+SMOTE-ENN取得最佳综合性能（Recall 0.8627, G-mean 0.8517, ROC-AUC 0.9431）；堆叠模型中SMOTE-ENN+(GB+XGB+HGB+LGBM+AB)+LSTM在敏感性与特异性间取得最佳平衡。SHAP分析识别出杠杆、盈利能力、偿债能力和运营效率指标为最重要的破产风险预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融数据通常具有严重的类别不平衡问题，破产案例（少数类）较少，导致传统分类模型倾向于忽略少数类样本。现有研究往往单独处理特征选择、重采样或模型选择，缺乏一个系统性的框架同时解决数据不平衡、特征冗余和模型可解释性问题，以确保破产预警系统既准确又可靠。

Method: 该框架包含四个关键步骤：(1) 使用五种特征选择算法并通过共识保留规则将输入空间缩减至23个稳健变量；(2) 应用SVM-SMOTE、SMOTE-Tomek和SMOTE-ENN三种混合重采样技术生成平衡训练数据；(3) 构建五种机器学习集成模型（GB、XGB、HistGB、LightGBM、AdaBoost）和五种深度学习模型（RNN、LSTM、GRU、DNN、MLP）进行对比；(4) 构建混合堆叠集成，以五种机器学习模型为基学习器、各深度学习模型为元学习器，并使用SHAP进行可解释性分析。

Result: 重采样策略对模型行为有显著影响：SVM-SMOTE和SMOTE-Tomek偏向准确率和特异性，而SMOTE-ENN增强了少数类检测能力。独立模型中，GRU+SMOTE-ENN表现最佳（Recall 0.8627, G-mean 0.8517, ROC-AUC 0.9431）；堆叠集成中，SMOTE-ENN+(GB+XGB+HGB+LGBM+AB)+LSTM在敏感性与特异性之间提供了最佳平衡。SHAP分析揭示杠杆、盈利能力、偿债能力和运营效率指标是最具影响力的破产风险预测因子。

Conclusion: 研究表明，合理选择重采样策略对于在不平衡金融数据上实现少数类检测的准确性至关重要，而结合堆叠集成可以进一步提升模型性能。该框架为财务困境公司的早期预警提供了更可靠且可解释的解决方案，有助于提升金融风险管理的实践效果。

Abstract: This study develops and evaluates a bankruptcy prediction framework that integrates consensus-based feature selection, hybrid resampling, stacking ensembles, and explainable artificial intelligence to improve minority-class detection in severely imbalanced financial data. Using the Taiwanese Bankruptcy Prediction dataset from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, five feature-selection algorithms were first applied, and a consensus retention rule reduced the input space to 23 robust variables. The balanced training data were then generated using SVM-SMOTE, SMOTE-Tomek, and SMOTE-ENN. Five ensemble machine learning classifiers, namely gradient boosting, extreme gradient boosting, histogram-based gradient boosting, LightGBM, and AdaBoost, were compared with five deep learning models, including RNN, LSTM, GRU, DNN, and MLP. In addition, hybrid stacking ensembles combined the five machine learning classifiers as base learners with each deep learning model as a meta-learner. Model performance was assessed using accuracy, recall, specificity, G-mean, and ROC-AUC, while SHAP was used to explain feature contributions. The results show that resampling strategy materially shaped model behavior. SVM-SMOTE and SMOTE-Tomek favored accuracy and specificity, whereas SMOTE-ENN delivered stronger minority-class detection. Among standalone models, the GRU with SMOTE-ENN achieved the best overall predictive balance, with recall of 0.8627, G-mean of 0.8517, and ROC-AUC of 0.9431. Among stacking ensembles, SMOTE-ENN with (GB+XGB+HGB+LGBM+AB)+LSTM provided the strongest compromise between sensitivity and specificity. SHAP analysis identified leverage, profitability, solvency, and operational efficiency indicators as the most influential predictors of bankruptcy risk. These findings support more reliable and interpretable early warning systems for financially distressed firms.

</details>


### [85] [Machine Learning and ARIMA Model Averaging for Adaptive Public Health Forecasting: Comparative Evaluation and an Ontario COVID-19 Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20406)
*Yushu Zou,Ye Li,Johra Moosa,Martin Grunnill,Samir N. Patel,Venkata R. Duvvuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了ARIMA、随机森林和XGBoost三种模型在安大略省COVID-19病例预测中的表现，提出了一种非负性能加权集成方法MLAMA，该方法在不同预测周期和响应性设定下均取得最低的归一化平均绝对百分比误差，支持根据操作条件选择预测模型而非依赖单一方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共卫生预测必须应对监测数据的突发变化，避免过度外推噪声、报告伪影或暂时趋势。现有研究缺乏对模型在转折点响应性、预测周期和训练数据量三个操作维度上的系统评估，需要一种能够根据操作条件灵活选择模型的框架。

Method: 使用190周（2020年1月至2023年10月）安大略省公开COVID-19病例数据，采用滚动起点时间序列交叉验证保持时间顺序。评估ARIMA、随机森林和XGBoost三个模型，从响应性、预测周期（1-6周）、历史训练数据量三个维度进行评估，并提出MLAMA——一种权重因预测周期和响应性设定而变化的非负性能加权集成方法。

Result: ARIMA在转折点后适应迅速但长周期归一化误差增大；随机森林和XGBoost初始响应较慢但长周期误差更稳定。研究期末两周预测中，使用最近数据训练优于更长历史数据（XGBoost尤为明显）。MLAMA在大多数预测周期上取得最低的归一化平均绝对百分比误差，并在各响应性设定中排名前列。

Conclusion: 研究支持根据操作条件选择预测模型而非依赖单一普适方法。MLAMA为结合互补的统计与机器学习预测提供了实用框架，Python包正在维护中，待软件验证和可复现性测试完成后公开。

Abstract: Public health forecasts must respond to abrupt changes in surveillance data without over-extrapolating noise, reporting artifacts, or temporary trends. We evaluated autoregressive integrated moving average (ARIMA), random forest, and extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost) models using 190 weekly observations of publicly available Ontario COVID-19 case counts from January 2020 to October 2023. Rolling-origin time-series cross-validation preserved temporal order during model tuning and evaluation. Performance was assessed across three operating dimensions: responsiveness following selected turning points, forecast horizons of one to six weeks, and the amount of historical training data. We also developed Machine Learning and ARIMA Model Averaging (MLAMA), a non-negative performance-weighted ensemble with weights that vary by forecast horizon and responsiveness setting. Retrospective comparisons showed that ARIMA adapted rapidly after turning points but its normalized error increased at longer horizons. Random forest and XGBoost were less responsive initially but maintained more stable normalized error over longer horizons. For two-week forecasts at the end of the study period, training on the most recent data outperformed using longer historical periods, particularly for XGBoost. MLAMA achieved the lowest normalized mean absolute percentage error across most forecast horizons and ranked among the best-performing methods across responsiveness settings. These findings support selecting forecasting models according to operating conditions rather than relying on a single universally preferred approach. MLAMA provides a practical framework for combining complementary statistical and machine-learning forecasts. The accompanying Python package is currently maintained in a private repository while software validation and reproducibility testing are completed.

</details>


### [86] [Across-Design Uncertainty in Short Pricing Panels: Evidence from Simulated Price Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21334)
*Pedro Cadahia Delgado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了短期观测定价面板中"观测数量多但价格变动少"这一特点对推断的影响。通过校准到稀疏定价机制的合成数据生成过程，区分了给定价格轨迹下的条件不确定性，与跨不同价格轨迹的估计误差变化。发现后者占梯度提升规范估计误差方差的97.6%，而面板内重采样无法识别这一跨设计成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期观测定价面板虽包含大量观测，但仅有少量不同的价格变动。这种"多数观测、少变动"的区分具有重要推断后果，但尚未被充分研究。理解不确定性分解（条件于已实现轨迹 vs 跨轨迹变化）对于正确推断至关重要。

Method: 使用校准到稀疏定价机制的合成数据生成过程，将估计误差方差分解为两部分：条件于已实现价格轨迹的不确定性，以及由同一定价过程生成的不同轨迹间的估计误差变化。通过模拟实验评估面板内重采样方法的局限性，并引入Paule-Mandel方差成分估计来量化跨独立定价单元的设计变异性。

Result: （1）跨设计离散度满足经验关系sigma≈0.182V^(-0.271)，其中V=价格变动次数×幅度平方；（2）增加共享共同价格路径的区域可降低结果噪声但不会产生独立价格轨迹，而跨独立设计特定误差单元平均可达到标准平方根率的离散度降低；（3）Paule-Mandel方差成分估计在独立定价单元上使同质模拟的经验覆盖率从0.469提升至0.931。

Conclusion: 核心启示是应转向设计能产生独立识别变异的数据生成过程，而非仅依赖固定的被动面板。面板内重采样程序只利用一个已实现轨迹的信息，无法识别跨设计的不确定性成分，因此需要更丰富的数据生成设计来获得可靠的推断。

Abstract: Short observational pricing panels can contain many observations while offering only a small number of distinct price movements. This paper studies the inferential consequences of that distinction in a synthetic data-generating process calibrated to a sparse pricing regime. We separate uncertainty conditional on a realised price trajectory from variation in estimation error across alternative trajectories generated by the same pricing process. In the baseline simulations, the latter component accounts for 97.6% of the variance of estimation error for the gradient-boosted specification. Within-panel resampling procedures use the information of one realised trajectory and do not identify this across-design component.
  Three results organise the analysis. First, across-design dispersion is well described by the empirical relation sigma_hat approx 0.182 V^(-0.271), where V equals moves times magnitude squared. Second, adding regions sharing a common price path reduces outcome noise but does not create independent price trajectories; conversely, averaging across units with independent design-specific errors reduces dispersion at the standard square root rate. Third, a Paule-Mandel variance component estimated across independently priced units substantially increases empirical coverage in homogeneous simulations, from 0.469 to 0.931. The broader implication is a shift toward designing data-generating processes that create independent identifying variation rather than relying solely on fixed passive panels.

</details>


### [87] [From Thermal Preference Prediction to Adaptive Thermal Intervention: A Reinforcement Learning Approach Using Physiological and Environmental Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20423)
*Isibor Kennedy Ihianle,Emmanuel Manu,Ehsan Asnaashari,Mojgan Jadidi,Pedro Machado,Amrit Sagoo,Ahmad Lotfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段个性化热舒适方法，整合多模态生理与环境传感数据，并结合强化学习进行决策，以替代依赖静态设定点和群体平均舒适模型的传统HVAC系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HVAC系统依赖静态设定点和群体级舒适模型，无法捕捉个体生理差异，难以满足个性化热舒适需求，也影响建筑控制策略的响应性。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段通过多模态生理与环境传感采集个体数据；第二阶段利用强化学习进行个性化热舒适决策。

Result: 摘要中未给出具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 该两阶段个性化热舒适方法有望提升 occupant 舒适度，并为更响应式的建筑控制策略提供支持。

Abstract: Personalised thermal comfort is essential for occupant wellbeing and for the development of more responsive building-control strategies, yet conventional Heating, Ventilation, and Air Conditioning (HVAC) systems rely on static setpoints and population-level comfort models that fail to capture individual physiological variability. This paper presents a two-stage personalised thermal comfort approach integrating multimodal physiological and environmental sensing with reinforcement learning-based decision-making.

</details>


### [88] [BF1: A Causal Dyadic Sparse-Attention Retrofit for Efficient Long-Context Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20427)
*Hina Dixit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BF1，一种确定性块对齐的二元稀疏注意力模式，结合精确局部邻域、全局首块和对数间隔历史块。在Blackwell GPU上，BF16实现从2K-4K token起超越密集注意力，32K时达到10.91倍逐层prefill加速。将Qwen3-0.6B的8/28层替换为BF1后，冷启动TTFT在8K/16K/32K分别降低7.7%/11.3%/15.3%。在匹配的适应协议下，BF1在困惑度指标上排名第一（1.68639），优于静态随机图、密集继续训练和局部滑动基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使在高度优化的精确内核实现下，长上下文的密集因果注意力仍然计算昂贵，需要一种确定性、可复现且系统级有效的稀疏注意力模式。

Method: BF1采用块对齐的二元稀疏路由：精确局部邻域 + 全局第一块 + 对数间隔历史块。通过正确性门控的预训练模型改造（retrofit）、匹配的拓扑控制研究以及连接逐层稀疏度与整体延迟的系统表征来验证。

Result: 每个转换层使用O(n log n)选中的token交互，图通信深度为O(log n)。在RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell上，32K时达到10.91倍逐层prefill加速。改造Qwen3-0.6B的八个注意力层后，TTFT降低7.7%-15.3%。BF1在困惑度上排名第一（1.68639），显著优于各基线。

Conclusion: BF1被确立为一种可复现的稀疏算子、选择性改造原语，在长上下文系统中具有实际价值，涵盖数值正确性、交互规模、内核性能、部分模型推理和匹配的下一token语言建模。

Abstract: Dense causal attention remains expensive at long context even when implemented with highly optimized exact kernels. We study BF1, a deterministic block-aligned dyadic sparse-attention route that combines a small exact local neighborhood, a global first block, and logarithmically spaced historical blocks. The route is related to prior log-sparse and dilated attention patterns; our contribution is a correctness-gated pretrained-model retrofit, a matched topology-control study, and a systems characterization that connects per-layer sparsity to whole-model latency. For fixed block width, every converted layer uses O(n log n) selected token interactions and has O(log n) graph communication depth. On an NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell GPU, an optimized BF16 implementation crosses dense attention between 2K and 4K tokens and reaches a 10.91x per-layer prefill speedup at 32K. Retrofitting eight of 28 Qwen3-0.6B attention layers lowers warm whole-model time to first token by 7.7%, 11.3%, and 15.3% at 8K, 16K, and 32K, respectively, while the remaining dense layers keep the complete model asymptotically quadratic. Under a matched 1,000-step, 16.384M-token adaptation protocol, BF1 ranks first across three training seeds: mean report perplexity is 1.68639 versus 1.69154 for a matched static-random nonlocal graph, 1.69258 for dense continued training, and 1.81505 for equal-budget local sliding. At seed 1234, the packed-report paired interval places Dense-CT 0.3169-0.4055% above BF1 and static-random graph 17 0.2441-0.3642% above BF1. These results establish BF1 as a reproducible sparse operator and selective retrofit primitive with real long-context systems value. This paper evaluates numerical correctness, selected-interaction scaling, kernel performance, partial-model inference, and matched next-token language modeling.

</details>


### [89] [When Clean Data Hurts: Learning with Monotone Corruptions Beyond Binary Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20480)
*Julian Asilis,Shaddin Dughmi,Chirag Pabbaraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究单调对抗性污染（monotone adversarial corruption）对更广泛学习场景（多分类和部分二元概念类）的影响，发现此类对抗者在这些场景中极具破坏力：一个DS维度仅为2的可学习多分类问题在单调对抗者下变得完全不可学习，部分二元概念类也有类似结果。同时证明当自适应添加数量为o(n)时所有类仍可学习，并给出若干正面的可学习性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典PAC模型假设i.i.d.数据，但现实中的数据可能被对抗性污染。Larsen等人（2026）引入单调对抗污染模型并证明所有已知最优二元学习器在该设置下误差率从O(d/n)恶化为Ω(d log(n/d)/n)，Mehrotra（2026）证明该对数因子对二元分类是必要的，但多分类和部分二元概念类等更一般学习设置的后果尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过构造具体的对抗性实例来证明不可能性结果：1）构造一个DS维度仅为2的多分类问题，在自适应单调对抗者插入线性数量b=n个污染数据点后变得不可学习；2）构造部分二元概念类的类似反例。同时通过一般性论证证明当自适应添加数量为o(n)时所有类仍保持可学习性，该上界被多分类下界证明是紧的。此外，对受限预算的自适应、半自适应和盲对抗者给出正面可学习性结果。

Result: 1）多分类场景：DS维度为2的可学习问题在单调对抗下变得完全不可学习（只需插入n个点）；2）部分二元概念类存在类似的不可能性结果；3）当自适应添加o(n)个点时所有类仍可学习，且多分类下界证明该界限是紧的；4）对常数预算自适应对抗者、半自适应对抗者（仅能查看S的p比例）和盲对抗者，经典O(d_DS/n)误差率仍然可达。

Conclusion: 单调对抗者在多分类和部分概念类设置中远比二元完全分类更强大，能在极小DS维度下彻底破坏可学习性。这揭示了单调对抗污染的威胁程度高度依赖于学习任务的复杂度设定。不过，通过限制对抗者的能力（预算、信息访问）可以恢复可学习性，说明问题的关键在于对抗者的自适应性和插入数量。

Abstract: Optimal learners are tailored to exploit the i.i.d.\ data assumption underlying the classic PAC model. What if an i.i.d.\ training sample were corrupted with correctly labeled examples drawn from an otherwise unrelated, even adversarial source? This model of learning with monotone adversarial corruptions was recently introduced by Larsen et al. (2026), who demonstrated that all known optimal binary learners suffer increased error rates in this setting, from $O(d / n)$ in the PAC model to $Ω(d \log(n / d) / n)$ under monotone corruption. Mehrotra (2026) proved this logarithmic factor to be necessary for binary classification, but left open the consequences of corruption for more general learning settings, such as multiclass classification and partial binary concept classes.
  As our primary result, we demonstrate that monotone adversaries are frighteningly more powerful in each of these settings. We exhibit a learnable multiclass problem, of DS dimension only 2, that becomes altogether unlearnable under a monotone adversary, and show an analogous result for partial binary concept classes. These results are achieved by an adaptive adversary permitted to view the original i.i.d.\ training set $S$ and to insert $b < \infty$ corrupted datapoints into $S$. In the multiclass example, the adversary need only insert a linear number $b = |S| = n$ of datapoints.
  We complement these impossibility results by proving that every class remains learnable when the number of adaptive additions is $o(n)$, which our previous multiclass lower bound proves to be tight. We further observe that the classic multiclass error rate of $O(d_{\mathrm{DS}} / n)$ remains achievable against adaptive adversaries restricted to a known constant budget $b = O(1)$, against semi-adaptive adversaries viewing only a $p$-fraction of $S$ for $p \in (0, 1)$, and against oblivious adversaries that cannot view $S$.

</details>


### [90] [Approximate Homomorphisms and Convergent Representations in Transducers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20428)
*Santiago Cifuentes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究受控随机过程（特别是转换器）的最小表示在扰动下的稳定性，基于神经网络潜在表示中发现预测性结构的实验动机。作者引入近似同态和接口度量等概念，证明线性转换器和预测转换器在一定条件下具有鲁棒性，而标准转换器则缺乏这种稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近的实验发现神经网络的潜在表示中存在预测性结构（predictive-state structure），这引发了一个理论问题：这种结构是否是偶然的，还是反映了某种内在的收敛性质？作者希望通过研究转换器表示在扰动下的稳定性，为神经网络潜在表示的结构收敛性提供理论支持。

Method: 作者考虑三类转换器：标准、线性和预测转换器。引入近似同态（approximate homomorphism）的概念来捕捉局部结构相似性，并定义度量来比较诱导动力系统（称为接口）。对于标准转换器，展示不存在近似同态的负面结果；对于线性转换器，证明所有接近某有限秩接口的最小实现都存在到该接口最小实现的近似同态，误差与扰动呈线性关系；对预测转换器，在信念状态不可区分性的温和假设下，用残差度量证明类似稳定性结果。

Result: 结果表明：标准转换器缺乏结构稳定性（存在简单接口无法近似同态）；线性转换器在有限秩接口下具有稳定性（误差线性于扰动）；预测转换器在残差度量和信念状态不可区分性假设下也具有类似稳定性。这些结果识别了规范转换器表示对扰动鲁棒的条件，并表明没有额外结构限制时收敛性会失败。

Conclusion: 该研究为神经网络潜在表示的结构收敛假说提供了理论依据：在合理假设下（如线性或预测性抽象嵌入到隐藏层），现代AI模型的潜在表示会表现出结构收敛。同时，负面结果也说明这种收敛需要额外的结构约束，并非普遍成立。

Abstract: We study the stability of minimal representations of controlled stochastic processes (in particular, transducers) under perturbations. This question is motivated by recent experiments finding predictive-state structure in the latent representations of neural networks. We consider standard, linear and predictive transducers. We introduce notions of approximate homomorphism capturing local structural similarity between them, together with metrics comparing their induced dynamics (which we refer to as interfaces), and prove properties such as composability of the approximate homomorphisms. For standard transducers, we show that there exist simple interfaces for which there is no approximate homomorphism between the different implementations of the dynamics. In contrast, for every finite-rank interface $\mathcal I$, we prove that all minimal linear transducers implementing interfaces sufficiently close to $\mathcal I$ have an approximate homomorphism to the minimal implementation of $\mathcal I$, with error linear in the perturbation size. We prove an analogous stability result for predictive transducers under a residual metric using some mild hypothesis regarding the indistinguishability of the belief states. These results identify conditions under which canonical transducer representations are robust to perturbations, while showing that such convergence fails without additional structural restrictions. Under the assumption that these type of abstractions are embedded into the hidden layers of modern AI models, this gives some theoretical support to the hypothesis that their latent representations exhibit structural convergence.

</details>


### [91] [A Critical Audit of Spatiotemporal Forecasting Benchmark Datasets and Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20980)
*Kenneth Martin,Simon Heilig,Asja Fischer,Michel F. C. Haddad,Adam M. Sykulski,Moshe Eliasof*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过经典时间序列方法分析GNN基准数据集，揭示了空间感知模型（GNN）在这些数据集上的表现并不优于简单的空间无关线性模型，质疑了这些基准数据集的判别可靠性，并提出了一套统计工具和更严谨的评估建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN在多元时间序列预测中广泛使用，但评估往往局限于少数基准数据集（如Chickenpox、METR-LA等），且现有基线（如历史平均、经典机器学习方法）已显示与GNN相当的性能。作者希望深入分析这些基准数据集，找出为何空间无关的线性模型表现如此强劲，从而质疑数据集的判别能力。

Method: 作者采用经典时间序列分析方法对基准数据集进行统计分析，识别显著的空间和时间相关性，揭示一阶差分数据集引入的结构性偏差；并将分析结果应用于一个简单的混合模型，展示其方法论如何指导GNN模型开发。

Result: 研究发现空间无关的线性模型比先前报告的更具竞争力，一阶差分数据集存在结构性偏差，降低了广泛采用的数据集的判别可靠性。

Conclusion: 作者建议减少对这些基准数据集的过度依赖，倡导更严谨的统计评估，并提出其分析方法可为GNN模型开发提供新思路。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are routinely employed for short-range forecasting on multivariate time series with a spatial graph structure. Despite the availability of many alternative datasets, method innovations within this domain are predominantly assessed against a rather limited set of benchmark datasets, most notably Chickenpox, PedalMe, WikiMaths, METR-LA, and PEMS-BAY. The evaluation protocols contain baselines spanning from historical averages to classical machine learning approaches. These baselines often show competitive performance compared to GNNs. In the present work, we take a step back and analyse the benchmark datasets via classical time series methods to uncover why spatially-unaware linear models pose a stronger competitor than previously reported, casting further doubt on the discriminative reliability of the aforementioned widely adopted datasets. Our statistical analysis provides a toolset for identifying significant spatial and temporal correlations, while revealing a structural bias introduced by first-order differenced datasets. We therefore recommend reducing the over-reliance on such datasets for method comparison, and instead advocate for more rigorous statistical evaluation. By applying the results of our analysis to a simple hybrid model, we show how our methodology can lead to novel ways of developing GNN models

</details>


### [92] [Wrong-Physics Backdoors in Neural PDE Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20439)
*Hanbing Liang,Fujun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对神经PDE算子的数据投毒攻击方法——跨参数重链接（cross-parameter relinking），通过在多参数求解器档案中操纵输入-输出配对，使触发输入在错误的物理参数下产生物理上合理但参数错误的解，即"错误物理后门"（wrong-physics backdoor）。在476个攻击实验中，FNO在平流扩散和二维Navier-Stokes上达到100%的后门成功率，同时保持低干净相对L2误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经PDE算子验证方法主要依赖清洁预测误差和与参数无关的合理性检查，未能识别预测输出在正确物理规律下却针对错误物理参数的情况。这暴露了一个结构性的验证漏洞：即使输出平滑且符合求解器行为特征，也无法保证其对应预期的物理参数。

Method: 提出跨参数重链接（cross-parameter relinking）数据投毒原语：利用多参数档案中张量到参数的来源追溯失败，通过标记（stamp）替代输入并将其监督信号重链接到同一潜在样本的缓存备选参数解上，实现错误物理后门攻击。攻击在多类模型（FNO、DeepONet为主，Transformer、GRU、LSTM为辅）上进行评估，并设置清洁标签、仅标签、洗牌等对照组。

Result: 在476个攻击战役中，FNO在平流扩散和二维Navier-Stokes上后门成功率高达1.0000，同时保持低清洁相对L2误差。对照组（清洁标签、仅标签、洗牌）显示仅高攻击成功率不够；成功的攻击必须将预测推向预期的替代物理目标，同时保持有界的清洁误差。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一个结构性验证缺口：仅凭平滑性或通用求解器行为不足以验证PDE算子的正确性，除非同时验证预期物理参数的来源（provenance）。这项工作强调需要在模型验证中引入参数来源追溯机制。

Abstract: Neural PDE operators are increasingly trained on reusable solver archives, yet validation often relies on clean prediction error and parameter-agnostic plausibility checks. We introduce cross-parameter relinking, a data-poisoning primitive that makes a triggered input select a valid solution from the same PDE family under an incorrect physical parameter. We term this a wrong-physics backdoor: the output remains physically plausible but is wrong for the intended parameter. The attack exploits tensor-to-parameter provenance failures in multi-parameter archives by stamping the surrogate input and relinking its supervision to a cached alternate-parameter solution for the same latent sample. Across 476 attack campaigns, we evaluate Burgers, advection-diffusion, two-dimensional Navier-Stokes, and an elliptic Poisson case. Fourier Neural Operators and DeepONet provide the primary evidence, with Transformer, GRU, and LSTM models as support. FNO reaches a backdoor success rate of 1.0000 on both advection-diffusion and two-dimensional Navier-Stokes while retaining low clean relative L2 error. Clean-label, label-only, and shuffled controls show that high attack success alone is insufficient: successful attacks must move predictions toward the intended alternate-physics target while preserving bounded clean error. These results expose a structural validation gap: smoothness or generic solver-like behavior is insufficient unless the provenance of the intended physical parameter is also verified.

</details>


### [93] [Free-Probability Kernels for Zero-Rollout Hyperparameter Selection in Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20998)
*Sara Malacarne,Andrea Ceni,Claudio Gallicchio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自由概率的确定性核方法，用于在选择泄漏线性储备池计算（RC）的超参数时替代昂贵的滚动模拟。该方法通过自由概率计算跨滞后传播系数，在宽极限下形成确定性时间核，从而在短标记导引序列上进行核岭回归排序候选运行模式，无需实例化或滚动储备池，且所选配置可跨宽度迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储备池计算的高效性在超参数选择阶段部分丧失：递归增益、输入尺度和泄漏率决定了储备池的稳定性和时间处理机制，通常需要通过大量滚动模拟来调优。在验证滚动稀缺的场景下，这一成本尤为高昂，因此需要一种无需滚动模拟的确定性方法来选择储备池运行模式。

Method: 利用自由概率计算跨滞后传播系数，这些系数概括了储备池如何混合过去的输入。在大宽度极限下，这些系数定义了近似有限储备池特征几何的确定性时间核。通过在短标记导引序列上执行核岭回归，对候选运行模式进行排序，无需实例化或滚动储备池。

Result: 在十个合成时间基准上，零滚动选择获得0.772的平均部署分数，与穷举模拟搜索的0.774相当，同时避免了156,600次选择滚动。在小滚动预算下，该排序方法在每个测试预算上均达到最强平均性能，仅用4.8%的滚动成本即达到穷举参考。在四个ETT数据集上，五个保留候选在三个数据集上恢复穷举运行点。在多变量蜂窝流量预测中，每单元15次滚动达到462次滚动的穷举参考，并在低预算下优于随机搜索和贝叶斯优化。

Conclusion: 自由概率核可作为选择储备池运行模式的确定性替代方案，特别适用于验证滚动稀缺的场景，显著降低了超参数选择的计算成本，同时保持与穷举搜索相当的性能。

Abstract: Reservoir computing (RC) couples a fixed recurrent dynamical system with a trained lightweight readout, but this efficiency is partly lost during hyperparameter selection: the recurrent gain, input scale, and leakage rate determine the reservoir's stability and temporal processing regime and are usually tuned through many rollouts. We introduce a deterministic, pilot-informed selector for leaky linear reservoirs followed by coordinate-wise nonlinear features. Free probability yields cross-lag propagation coefficients that summarize how the reservoir mixes past inputs. In the large-width limit, these coefficients define a deterministic temporal kernel that approximates the finite-reservoir feature geometry. Kernel ridge regression on a short labelled pilot sequence therefore ranks candidate operating regimes without instantiating or rolling out a reservoir, and the selected configuration transfers across widths. Across ten synthetic temporal benchmarks, zero-rollout selection obtains a mean deployment score of $0.772$, compared with $0.774$ for exhaustive simulation-based search, while avoiding $156\,600$ selection rollouts. With a small rollout budget, the proposed ranking provides the strongest mean performance at every tested budget and reaches the exhaustive reference using $4.8\%$ of its rollout cost. On four public electricity-transformer-temperature (ETT) forecasting datasets, five retained candidates recover the exhaustive operating point on three datasets. On multivariate cellular-traffic forecasting, 15 rollouts per cell reach the 462-rollout exhaustive reference and outperform random search and Bayesian optimization at low budgets. These results position free-probability kernels as deterministic surrogates for selecting reservoir operating regimes when validation rollouts are scarce.

</details>


### [94] [Decision Tree and K-Means Analysis of Raman Spectra for Edible Oils: A Physics-Informed AI Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20440)
*Amrita Shaw,Chandrasekar S. N.,Sai Muthukumar V.,Jhinuk Gupta,Deepak L. N. Kallepalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究建立了集成拉曼光谱与机器学习的框架，用于加工食品中食用油的真伪鉴别。通过t-SNE、K-means、决策树和NNLS光谱分解等方法，纯油样品仅用4个拉曼变量即实现100%分类准确率；含食品基质样品经NNLS分解后准确率可达86.4%和85.4%，同时将特征数压缩至4-5个，数据量缩减99.44%，为边缘计算和便携式食品质量监测提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加工食品中食用油的真伪鉴别对食品质量、防欺诈和法规合规至关重要，但食品基质效应会引入光谱重叠，影响传统分析方法的效果，亟需一种兼具准确性、可解释性和高效性的鉴别方案。

Method: 采用拉曼光谱结合多种机器学习方法：(1) t-SNE和K-means聚类进行无监督分析；(2) 决策树（预剪枝和后剪枝）进行特征选择和分类；(3) 基于NNLS的物理信息人工智能（PI-AI）光谱分解，分离油类信号与纸张、马铃薯基质贡献；(4) 对纯油和炸薯片基质中的五种食用油进行系统比较。

Result: 纯油样品决策树仅用4个拉曼变量（占1866个特征空间的0.21%）即达到100%测试集准确率；含基质样品经NNLS分解后，后剪枝模型准确率达86.4%（扣除纸张）和85.4%（扣除纸张与马铃薯），重要变量分别降至5个和4个；四特征表示使数据占用减少99.44%且准确率无损失。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过物理意义明确、高度紧凑且可解释的光谱表示，可以实现准确的拉曼油类鉴别，为Frugal AI、Edge AI、便携式传感和嵌入式食品质量监测提供了有前景的基础。

Abstract: Authentication of edible oils in processed foods is important for food quality, fraud prevention, and regulatory compliance. This study establishes an integrated Raman spectroscopy and machine-learning framework that links intrinsic spectral organization, interpretable classification, and Physics-Informed Artificial Intelligence (PI-AI). Five edible oils were investigated in pure form and within a fried-potato-chip matrix using t-SNE, K-means clustering, Decision Trees, and Non-Negative Least Squares (NNLS)-based spectral decomposition. Unsupervised analyses revealed substantially stronger class organization and separability in pure oils, whereas food-matrix effects introduced pronounced spectral overlap. Decision Trees achieved 100% classification accuracy for pure oils using only four Raman variables from the original 1866-feature spectral space. These four variables, consistently identified by both pre-pruned and post-pruned models, represented only approximately 0.21% of the available spectral information while retaining perfect test-set performance. For matrix-containing samples, NNLS-based PI-AI spectral decomposition substantially improved classification by separating oil-related signatures from paper and potato contributions. Optimized post-pruned models achieved accuracies of 86.4% and 85.4% for paper-subtracted and paper-plus-potato-subtracted datasets, respectively, while reducing the number of important Raman variables to only five and four. The compact four-feature representation further reduced the data footprint by 99.44% without loss of classification accuracy. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that accurate Raman-based oil identification can be achieved through physically meaningful, highly compact, and interpretable spectral representations, providing a promising foundation for Frugal AI, Edge AI, portable sensing, and embedded food-quality monitoring.

</details>


### [95] [TRACE-C: Rank-Calibrated Relational Anomaly Detection for Multi-Stream Operational Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21251)
*Matthew Faucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE-C是一种可审计的严格先验秩校准检测器，用于对齐的多流遥测异常检测。它通过三种通道（局部归一化和、高斯copula形式依赖对比、AR(1)创新）的秩聚合来识别联合异常。在六条英国电网流上的评估显示，该方法能检测出Storm Atiyah等事件，但作者诚实地揭示了其局限：copula通道贡献有限、p值只是选择量而非事件概率、秩计数不能证明覆盖率或错误发现率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的逐流遥测异常检测无法捕捉联合异常——当每条单独的流都处于正常范围内时，多条流的组合行为仍可能是异常的。本文旨在开发一种能够检测这种"联合异常"的可审计检测器，同时保持严格的统计校准和透明度。

Method: TRACE-C采用严格先验秩校准方法：(1) 使用同期滚动中位数/MAD残差；(2) 三种窗口通道——最大归一化局部和、基于稳健z残差的高斯copula形式依赖对比、最差标准化AR(1)创新；(3) 通过Fisher方法聚合各通道秩并与早期聚合进行排序。评估使用2019年1-4月拟合、2019年7-12月开发验证、2020年冻结的保留数据。

Result: TRACE-C在2019年测试窗口中将Storm Atiyah排在首位，但通道消融显示该排名主要归因于局部通道而非copula形式通道（仅copula时排名第59）。短时频率事件（8月9日）融合检测排名143（远低于时间通道单独的40），而重建基线将其排第一。2020年无窗口被选中，作者认为这符合记录规则饱和而非平静年份；最高排名的冻结窗口被解释为Storm Ellen。

Conclusion: 论文强调三个重要的解释限制：(1) 得到的p值是选择量而非事件概率；(2) copula形式通道并非真正的copula密度，未应用概率积分或正态分数变换；(3) 经验秩计数只是诊断工具，不能作为覆盖率或错误发现率的证明。所有结果均来自可复现的机器可读报告。

Abstract: Operational telemetry can be jointly anomalous while every individual stream stays inside its familiar range. TRACE-C is an auditable strictly-prior rank-calibrated detector for aligned multi-stream telemetry: same-regime rolling median/MAD residuals feed three window channels -- a maximum normalized local sum, a Gaussian copula-form dependence contrast on robust-z residuals, and a worst standardized AR(1) innovation -- whose channel ranks are Fisher-aggregated and ranked against earlier aggregates.
  We evaluate six Great Britain grid streams with a January-April 2019 fit, July-December 2019 development evidence, and a 2020 hold-out frozen before inspection. TRACE-C ranks Storm Atiyah first among 2019 test windows, but a disclosed channel ablation attributes that rank to the local channel, not the copula-form channel: copula-only ranks Atiyah 59th. The short 9 August frequency event is ranked far lower by the fused detector (143) than by the temporal channel alone (40), and reconstruction baselines rank it first. In 2020 no window is selected, which is consistent with record-rule saturation rather than an uneventful year; the highest-ranked frozen window was later interpreted as Storm Ellen.
  Three interpretive limits carry throughout. The resulting p-values are selection quantities, not event probabilities. The copula-form channel is not a literal copula density: the method applies no probability-integral or normal-score transform. Empirical rank counts are diagnostics, not coverage or false-discovery proofs. Every table and figure in this paper is generated from committed machine-readable reports.

</details>


### [96] [Shared Physics Responses Recover Hidden Rankings in Neural Operator Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20441)
*Hanbing Liang,Fujun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需高保真参考解即可在部署阶段选择最优神经算子预测模型的方法。通过基于平方Hilbert空间损失的理论分析，证明了模型库的排序仅依赖于候选模型差异的低维空间，从而可以利用单个锚点线性化响应方程同时对所有模型进行评分。该方法在流体、反应扩散和波动动力学等多种算子库中恢复了超过99.6%的成对偏好和99.0%的最优检查点，且校正后的物理代理指标经常优于最佳候选模型，并建立了可计算的充分条件来严格认证强单调离散化的精确决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在部署阶段选择最优模型时面临核心挑战：由于没有高保真参考解（ground-truth），无法直接评估模型预测质量。传统的验证方法依赖于真实数据，但在实际部署场景中这些数据往往是不可获得的，这限制了科学代理模型（scientific surrogates）的可靠部署。

Method: 本文方法包括：(1) 理论上证明在平方Hilbert空间损失下，有限模型库的排序仅取决于候选模型之间差异的低维空间；(2) 利用单一锚点基于控制方程的线性化响应（linearized response）作为共享物理诊断指标，对所有模型同时评分；(3) 通过物理代理指标的校正来超越最佳个体候选；(4) 建立可计算的充分条件以严格认证强单调离散化下的精确决策。

Result: 在流体、反应扩散和波动动力学等多种Fourier和卷积算子库中，该方法恢复了超过99.6%的成对模型偏好和99.0%的最优检查点。此外，校正后的物理代理指标在性能上经常优于原始模型库中的最佳候选模型。

Conclusion: 通过利用局部动力学响应而非原始缺陷幅度（raw defect magnitude），该框架能够在不需要ground-truth数据的情况下，实现科学代理模型的可靠且高效的部署。这为神经算子在真实场景中的模型选择和部署提供了理论保证和实用工具。

Abstract: Selecting the optimal neural-operator prediction during deployment is challenging when high-fidelity reference solutions are unavailable. We demonstrate that under a squared Hilbert-space loss, ranking a finite model library depends strictly on the low-dimensional span of candidate differences, allowing us to score all models simultaneously using a single anchor-based linearized response of the governing equation. This shared physical diagnostic accurately recovered over 99.6\% of pairwise preferences and 99.0\% of optimal checkpoints across diverse Fourier and convolutional operator libraries for fluid, reaction-diffusion, and wave dynamics. Furthermore, the corrected physical proxy frequently outperformed the best individual candidates, and we establish computable sufficient conditions that rigorously certify exact decisions for strongly monotone discretizations. By exploiting the local dynamical response rather than raw defect magnitude, this framework enables the reliable and highly efficient deployment of scientific surrogates without requiring ground-truth data.

</details>


### [97] [Stored in Optimizer State, Valued by Later Training: A Causal Account of Subliminal Trait Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20442)
*Qinyang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个两阶段机制来解释"潜意识特质迁移"（subliminal trait transfer）：优化器状态（特别是第一矩）作为因果载体负责传输源扰动（与观察者无关），而后续训练阶段决定该扰动的行为价值（即符号和大小）。通过"训练器状态"（参数+优化器矩）的统一视角和精确的传输-估值恒等式，作者分离了这两个阶段，并通过符号预测和跨模型实验验证了这一机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅解释了潜意识特质信号如何进入梯度，但无法解释两个关键现象：(1) 源数据被移除后，信号为何仍能存活并持续产生影响；(2) 相同的源差异在后续训练中为何会产生不同符号（正或负）的行为效应。作者希望从理论上统一解释这两个问题。

Method: 将参数和优化器矩视为一个统一的"训练器状态"，推导出精确的传输-估值恒等式，将源扰动的传播（观察者无关）与未来连续性训练和行为的估值（依赖后续路径）分离。通过状态手术（state surgery）证明第一矩（first moment）是因果载体：仅移植第一矩（不移植参数）在切割时刻保持所有状态不变，但后续无源更新产生持续差异。同时使用全时域costate预测所有路径的行为符号。

Result: 实验中，相同源差异通过匹配的未来路径产生负、近零和正的Qwen效应（-0.658, +0.008, +0.658）。该排序在12个Llama-3.2-1B种子中一致复现。全时域costate预测了所有42个Qwen路径平均符号和21个Llama普通路径符号。结果跨Qwen、SmolLM2、Llama复制，并在非LoRA MNIST系统（包括CNN+AdamW和momentum SGD）上验证了完整状态递推的预测能力。

Conclusion: 潜意识特质迁移是一个两阶段机制：优化器状态（第一矩）负责传输源扰动，后续训练决定其行为价值。这一发现统一解释了信号存活和符号变化两个此前未解的问题，为理解和控制模型行为特质迁移提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Subliminal trait transfer allows a student model to acquire behavioral dispositions from teacher-generated data in which the trait is not semantically expressed. Recent work explains how such signals enter gradients, but not how they survive source removal or acquire different signs under later training. We treat parameters and optimizer moments as a single trainer state and derive an exact transport-valuation identity separating observer-independent propagation of the source perturbation from the value assigned by a future continuation and behavioral readout. State surgery identifies the first moment as a causal carrier. Transplanting it alone leaves parameters, hidden states, and outputs unchanged at the cut, yet source-free updates generate growing parameter and hidden-state differences; transplanting parameters with the first moment recovers the terminal behavioral response. Sending the same source-induced difference through matched futures produces negative, near-zero, and positive Qwen effects (-0.658, +0.008, and +0.658 seed means). This ordering recurs in all 12 Llama-3.2-1B seeds after eight updates, while state-difference norms remain nearly equal across routes. Both contrasts grow in every paired seed when the continuation extends to sixteen updates. A full-horizon costate predicts all 42 Qwen route-mean signs and all 21 resolved Llama ordinary-route signs. Observer-independent transport also replicates across Qwen, SmolLM2, and Llama, while the complete-state recurrence predicts physical, hidden, and fixed-head responses in non-LoRA MNIST systems, including CNNs trained with AdamW and momentum SGD. Together, these results identify a two-stage mechanism for subliminal trait transfer: optimizer state transports the source perturbation, and later training determines its behavioral value.

</details>


### [98] [Amortized Bandwidth Learning for Kernel Density Estimation under Logarithmic Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20445)
*Junyi Liang,Hailiang Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种摊销框架，通过学习核密度估计中样本到带宽的映射（而非用经典分析或逐样本优化方法），利用对数分数在密度估计任务分布上进行训练。通过截断重归一化的有界支撑公式和仿射标准化实现跨任务稳定学习和跨区间迁移，在多项实验中一致且显著优于Silverman规则、Sheather-Jones选择器和最小二乘交叉验证，尤其在小样本和异构样本中优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核密度估计的性能高度依赖带宽选择，但经典选择器要么依赖解析/渐近规则，要么对每个样本单独求解优化问题，缺乏高效且通用的方案。现有方法在小样本和异构任务上表现不佳，亟需一种能够跨任务泛化、不依赖具体分布族假设的带宽选择方法。

Method: 提出摊销（amortized）框架：学习从样本到带宽的映射函数，在密度估计任务分布上通过优化对数分数进行训练。采用截断重归一化的有界支撑公式以支持跨异构任务的稳定学习；利用仿射标准化使在单一参考区间上训练的带宽选择器能够迁移到任意有界区间。有限高斯混合作为通用训练机制，借助其L1逼近性质提供训练数据。

Result: 在高斯采样、多族基准和随机高斯混合训练等实验中，摊销选择器一致且显著优于Silverman规则、Sheather-Jones选择器和最小二乘交叉验证，在小样本和异构样本上增益尤其显著。在高斯混合上训练的带宽选择器能够强泛化到不同的密度结构，可直接应用于未知密度的有限样本。

Conclusion: 该摊销框架兼具广泛适用性和强经验性能，无需指定或拟合分布族即可直接为未知密度的有限样本选择带宽，适合需要将有限样本或集成转化为连续概率密度的广泛应用场景，具有很大的实用价值。

Abstract: Kernel density estimation converts finite samples into probability densities, but its performance depends critically on bandwidth selection. Classical selectors prescribe the sample-to-bandwidth rule analytically or asymptotically, or solve a new optimization for each sample. An amortized framework is proposed that instead learns this mapping across a distribution of density-estimation tasks by optimizing the logarithmic score. A truncated-and-renormalized bounded-support formulation enables stable learning across heterogeneous tasks, while affine standardization allows a selector trained on a single reference interval to transfer across bounded intervals. Experiments under Gaussian sampling, a multi-family benchmark, and randomized Gaussian-mixture training show that the amortized selector consistently and substantially outperforms Silverman's rule, the Sheather--Jones selector, and least-squares cross-validation, with especially large gains in small and heterogeneous samples. Finite Gaussian mixtures provide a generic training mechanism supported by their $L^1$ approximation property. Selectors trained in this way generalize strongly across different density structures, allowing the same trained selector to be applied directly to finite samples from unknown densities without specifying or fitting a distributional family. This combination of broad applicability and strong empirical performance makes the framework attractive for a wide range of applications in which finite samples or ensembles must be converted into continuous probability densities.

</details>


### [99] [Provable Edge-of-Stability for Adam on a One-Dimensional Quadratic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20638)
*Yiman Fong,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了未修正Adam优化器在一维二次问题上的边缘稳定性（EoS）现象，通过常曲率设置隔离优化器诱导的动力学，证明了Adam在广泛参数区间内具有向冻结稳定阈值$2(1+\beta_1)/[\eta(1-\beta_1)]$恢复的趋势，同时识别出该边缘寻求机制失效的场景（包含严格次临界周期轨道和均匀超临界收敛轨迹）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam的EoS现象已被广泛观察到，但其背后的动力学机制尚未被充分理解。作者希望在一个简洁的、不受损失几何演化干扰的设定下，给出EoS的具体动力学解释，并揭示其局限性。

Method: 在一维二次损失函数上分析未修正的Adam（uncorrected Adam），利用常曲率设置隔离优化器诱导的动力学，通过严格的数学证明刻画整个参数空间中的动力学行为，包括稳定阈值附近的恢复趋势、次临界周期轨道及均匀超临界的特殊轨迹。

Result: 在广泛参数区间内证明Adam表现出向冻结稳定阈值$2(1+\beta_1)/[\eta(1-\beta_1)]$的恢复倾向；同时识别出该机制失效的场景，包括严格次临界周期轨道和可收敛到最优解但始终保持均匀超临界的特殊调优轨迹。

Conclusion: 该研究在无损失几何演化的简洁设定下为Adam的EoS现象提供了具体的动力学解释，同时揭示了该边缘寻求机制存在的局限性，有助于深化对Adam优化器动态行为的理论理解。

Abstract: The edge-of-stability (EoS) phenomenon of Adam has been widely observed, while its underlying dynamical mechanism is not yet fully understood. We study uncorrected Adam on a one-dimensional quadratic, a clean setting where constant curvature isolates the optimizer-induced dynamics behind the EoS. We characterize the resulting dynamics across the parameter space. In broad regimes, we prove that Adam exhibits a restoring tendency toward its frozen stability threshold $2(1+β_1)/[η(1-β_1)]$. We also identify settings in which this edge-seeking mechanism breaks down, including strictly subcritical periodic orbits and specially tuned trajectories that converge to the optimum while remaining uniformly supercritical. These results give a concrete dynamical explanation for Adam's EoS in a setting free of evolving loss geometry, while also exposing its limitations.

</details>


### [100] [Mutual information and sensitivity analysis for feature selection in customer targeting: a comparative study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20447)
*Nestor Barraza,Sergio Moro,Marcelo Ferreyra,Adolfo de la Peña*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了互信息与基于数据的敏感性分析两种特征选择技术在银行电话营销案例中的表现。使用逻辑回归模型评估，敏感性分析在低假阳性率下表现更好（9个特征），而互信息在高假阳性率下稍优（13个特征）。互信息仍是有效方法，敏感性分析则以更少特征获得良好预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据驱动的知识发现项目中，特征选择是至关重要的任务。现有多种方法（如互信息和新兴的基于数据的敏感性分析），但缺乏对这两种技术在真实场景中的系统性优劣对比，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将互信息与基于数据的敏感性分析两种特征选择技术应用于银行电话营销案例，分别选出最影响营销成功的关键特征集（互信息13个特征，敏感性分析9个特征），并基于各自特征集构建逻辑回归模型进行效果评估。

Result: 敏感性分析在低假阳性率下表现更好，而互信息在高假阳性率下略优。互信息适合银行在不牺牲大量成功客户的前提下降低接触成本；敏感性分析以更少的特征获得了良好的预测效果。

Conclusion: 互信息尽管不是新技术，在特征选择上仍然是有效的方法；基于数据的敏感性分析则在特征数量更少的情况下也能取得良好预测性能，两种方法各有适用场景。

Abstract: Feature selection is a highly relevant task in a data-driven knowledge discovery project. Several techniques have been developed aiming at finding the features that influence most an outcome to predict, including mutual information and, in recent years, the data-based sensitivity analysis. The present research focus on analyzing the advantages and disadvantages of each of these two techniques, by applying both to a bank telemarketing case. Thereafter, a logistic regression model is built on the tuned set of features identified by each of the two techniques as the most influencing set of features on the success of a telemarketing contact, in a total of 13 features for mutual information and 9 features for the data-based sensitivity analysis. The latter performs better for lower values of false positives while the former is slightly better for a higher false positive ratio. Thus, mutual information becomes a better choice if bank managers intend to reduce slightly the cost of contacts without risking losing a high number of successes. Such results show that mutual information, although not recent, is still a valid method for feature selection. On the other side, the data-based sensitivity analysis selection achieved good prediction results with less features.

</details>


### [101] [Metag: A dataset to build agentic meta-reviewing capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20488)
*Anirudh Sundar,Min Chen,Divya Tadimeti,Gemma Zhang,Alice Li,Nigel Boachie Kumankumah,Pavan Uttej Ravva,Sadid Hasan,Somya Chatterjee,Pruthvi Prakash Navada,Xiao Wang,Yue Kang,Sulaiman Vesal,Larry Heck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍Metag数据集，用于加速元审稿（meta-reviewing）代理的开发，帮助识别论文在评审-反驳过程中所做的修改。每个实例包含审稿人关注点、作者提出的解决方案以及实现该变更的论文差异（diffs）。数据集包含349个高质量动作项，已公开在GitHub上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着会议投稿量的持续增长，元审稿人（meta-reviewers）需要综合审稿反馈、作者反驳和稿件修订，负担日益加重。现有工具缺乏支持元审稿人快速验证作者是否回应审稿意见的数据，因此需要构建此类数据集来加速相关方法的开发。

Method: Metag的构建流程包括：1）从OpenReview获取论文在审稿截止前的版本和录用后的版本；2）计算两个文档之间的差异（diffs）；3）邀请人类标注者将这些差异与OpenReview讨论中的行动项进行对齐。最终形成包含审稿人关注点、作者解决方案和论文差异三元组的实例。

Result: 构建了包含349个高质量行动项的数据集，每个行动项都与具体的论文差异相关联。数据集公开可获取。

Conclusion: Metag数据集将支持构建方法，使元审稿人能够快速识别作者是否回应了审稿人意见以及修改发生在论文的哪个位置，从而为同行评审过程增加透明度和可追溯性。

Abstract: AI tools increasingly support tasks across the scientific research cycle, from experiment design and manuscript preparation to peer review. At the same time, the continuing growth in conference submissions has increased the burden on meta-reviewers, who must synthesize reviewer feedback, author rebuttals, and manuscript revisions. To address this concern, this paper introduces Metag, a dataset to accelerate the development of meta-reviewing agents, specifically to identify changes made to scientific articles during the review-rebuttal process. Each instance contains a reviewer concern, the author's proposed resolution, and the manuscript diffs implementing the stated change. Metag is collected by obtaining manuscript versions from before the review deadline and after acceptance, computing differences between the two documents, and asking human annotators to align these differences with action items from OpenReview discussions. The resulting dataset consists of 349 high-quality action items tied to paper differences and will enable building methods to empower meta reviewers to quickly identify whether authors have addressed reviewer statements and where in the paper those changes have been made, resulting in additional transparency and traceability throughout peer review. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/microsoft/Metag-dataset.

</details>


### [102] [Bern2Edge: A Neurosymbolic Compiler for Edge Deployment via Bernstein Polynomial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20497)
*Malak Gamal El-Din,Yifan Zhang,Yasser Shoukry,Sitao Huang,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bern2Edge是一个端到端框架，通过知识蒸馏将预训练的前馈网络转换为基于Bernstein多项式激活的硬件高效表示，提供LUT查表（高保真）和符号规则（可解释）两种部署路径，在边缘设备上实现高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将训练、压缩和硬件综合作为独立阶段处理，导致软件训练模型与高效端到端部署之间存在鸿沟，且可解释性支持有限。

Method: 使用知识蒸馏将预训练教师前馈网络转换为Bernstein多项式激活表示，该表示支持两条部署路径：(i)高保真LUT实现，在压缩下保持模型保真度；(ii)基于Bernstein激活几何的符号规则表示，支持带显式输入空间约束的可解释推理。

Result: BNN在相同压缩约束下相比ReLU提升最高2.12个百分点准确率；系统级在AMD Xilinx KV260 FPGA上实现最高99.8%延迟降低和95.2% BRAM减少，同时保持0.5个百分点内准确率；规则路径减少最高89.0% DSP使用，代价为1.5个百分点总准确率。

Conclusion: Bern2Edge成功弥合了软件训练与硬件部署之间的差距，同时提供高保真和可解释两种部署选项，适用于资源受限的边缘设备。

Abstract: Deploying high-accuracy neural networks on resource-constrained edge devices remains challenging, as existing approaches treat training, compression, and hardware synthesis as separate stages, leaving a gap between software-trained models and efficient end-to-end deployment with limited support for interpretability. We propose Bern2Edge, an end-to-end framework that uses knowledge distillation to convert a pretrained teacher feed-forward network into hardware-efficient representations via Bernstein polynomial activations. This representation enables two deployment paths: (i) a high-fidelity LUT-based realization that preserves model fidelity under compression, and (ii) a symbolic rule-based representation derived from Bernstein activation geometry, enabling interpretable inference with explicit input-space constraints. The resulting BNNs achieve up to 2.12 percentage-point (pp) accuracy improvement over ReLU under identical compression constraints. At the system level, Bern2Edge achieves up to 99.8% latency reduction and 95.2% BRAM reduction relative to a W8A8 quantized teacher on an AMD Xilinx KV260 FPGA, while maintaining accuracy within 0.5 pp, and further deploys on a low-power Spartan-7 XC7S15 FPGA. The rule-based path reduces DSP usage by up to 89.0% at a cost of 1.5 pp in total accuracy.

</details>


### [103] [When Graph-JEPA Learns the Wrong Thing: Diagnosing and Repairing Category-Conditional Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20516)
*Gollam Rabby,Sören Auer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个关键问题：联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在标准指标（线性探针准确率0.871、有效秩18-47）上表现健康，但实际上表征不携带任何可用的实例信息（检索信息量为0比特）。作者通过图科学推理数据集（57,903篇文章）诊断出这一问题源于目标函数的最优解中存在退化解——方差分配失衡（冻结输入将86.05%方差放在子图身份上，而训练潜在变量将99.61%放在方面身份上）。修复后虽达到近乎理论极限的性能，但进一步发现目标本身具有可约性（可确定性简化的回归目标），架构的主导因素其实是学习率调度而非架构本身。作者发布了带有可约性审计和目标质量门的测试工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA类）被广泛采用，且通常用线性探针和有效秩来验证其表示质量，但这些指标可能在表征完全不携带有效实例信息时仍然"健康"。作者希望揭示这种指标饱和/误报现象，并区分其根本原因：是目标函数设计缺陷，还是数据/评估本身不支持。

Method: 使用Graph-JEPA对57,903篇科学文章构建的推理子图进行掩码预测：预测子图的某个掩码方面（aspect），用其余方面作为输入。通过检索任务（MRR和比特恢复量）作为真正的信息量度量，对比线性探针和有效秩。进行三类上界实验排除语料/掩码/池/度量问题；通过方差分配分析追踪退化根因；通过修复配置（调整损失）验证假设，并设计可约性审计和目标质量门来评估目标本身。
