Result: 在Qwen3.5 35B和397B两个规模上，在相同接受长度下大幅削减投机递归状态内存和KV缓存压力；在内存受限场景下将释放的HBM转化为更高吞吐量和更低首Token延迟（TTFT），在非受限场景下仅损失几个百分点的性能。更宽的树在相同显存下可实现更高接受率，虽尚未成为吞吐量优势。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了混合模型投机解码的内存瓶颈，通过消除状态快照实现了显存效率的大幅提升，并在内存受限场景下带来实际性能收益，同时支持更宽的草稿树，为混合架构的投机解码提供了新的可行性方向。

Abstract: Modern open models are hybrids: most layers are linear-attention (Gated DeltaNet, GDN) layers carrying a small fixed-size recurrent state instead of a growing key-value (KV) cache. This makes ordinary decoding memory-efficient, but hurts speculative decoding. To verify a batch of draft tokens and then roll back the rejected ones, today's systems snapshot the full recurrent state at every draft position for GDN layers, and those snapshots cannot be shared across branches of a draft tree, so a wide, high-acceptance tree becomes memory-infeasible. We remove the snapshots. Using a tree-structured WY transform of the gated delta rule, we compute every draft node's output with a single triangular solve and reconstruct only the one accepted state on commit, storing a small pseudo-value matrix instead of per-node states; the derivation depends only on the gated delta rule, not on any other architectural detail. In serving benchmarks on two scales of one hybrid model family (Qwen3.5 35B and 397B) this cuts speculative recurrent-state memory and KV-cache pressure at identical acceptance length, turning the freed HBM into higher throughput and much lower time-to-first-token (TTFT) wherever memory binds, and costing a few percent where it does not. For tree width the same memory buys affordability: a wider, higher-acceptance draft becomes possible, though not yet a throughput win.

</details>


### [202] [Generalizing Soft Tissue Deformation and Force Prediction Across Material Stiffness and Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20967)
*Madina Kojanazarova,Sidaty El Hadramy,Philippe C. Cattin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在SOFA框架中对不同刚度的硅胶梁进行超弹性本构模型的系统校准，并利用校准后的仿真数据训练一种软度条件化的等变图神经网络，该模型能够跨多种组织类型和未见几何形状进行变形与力预测，在0.010秒推断时间内实现亚毫米级平均变形精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习驱动的软组织仿真代理模型依赖校准良好的本构模型，但现有方法既缺乏跨刚度水平的系统模型选择指导，也无法泛化到不同组织刚度和几何形状，限制了实时推断的可靠性。

Method: 首先在SOFA框架中对不同刚度的重力加载硅胶梁进行全面的超弹性本构模型校准；然后使用校准仿真生成训练数据，训练一个软度条件化的等变图神经网络（GNN），实现变形和力预测。

Result: 模型在0.010秒推断时间内达到亚毫米级平均变形精度，并发现力预测质量与上游校准一致性直接相关。

Conclusion: 将本构模型校准与学习框架相结合，可实现跨多组织类型和未见几何形状的实时软组织仿真，且力预测的可靠性取决于校准的一致性。

Abstract: Accurate soft tissue simulation is essential for surgical training, pre-operative planning, and haptic feedback systems. While learning-based surrogate models trained on data using the finite element method (FEM) offer a promising path to real-time inference, their reliability depends on well-calibrated constitutive models. Existing approaches neither provide systematic guidance on model selection across stiffness levels, nor generalize across different tissue stiffnesses or geometries. We perform a comprehensive calibration of hyperelastic constitutive models in the SOFA Framework using gravity-loaded silicone beams with different stiffnesses. Using calibrated simulations as training data, we use a softness conditioned equivariant graph neural network, enabling deformation and force prediction across multiple tissue types and unseen geometries. Our model achieves sub-millimeter mean deformation accuracy at 0.010s inference time, while showing that force prediction quality is directly tied to upstream calibration consistency.

</details>


### [203] [Deep Learning Models Also Recall Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20970)
*Pierre Beckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"特征回忆"（feature recall）这一新概念，认为深度学习模型中存在一种普遍操作：线性投影可被理解为按输入激活值缩放检索存储信息。文章跨架构论证该概念，并与传统的"特征组合"范式加以区分，为哲学理解和机制可解释性研究提供新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期机制可解释性工作聚焦于大语言模型如何回忆存储于权重中的事实，但本文认为这应被推广到更一般的深度学习操作层面，而非仅限于事实回忆这一具体案例。

Method: 定义"特征回忆"概念，论证其跨架构普遍适用性，与"特征组合"范式进行对比，并探讨在机制层面识别特征回忆实例的方法。

Result: 提出特征回忆作为统一概念框架，能够解释深度学习中的一类关键操作，并与已有的特征组合范式形成互补。

Conclusion: 该概念为哲学家理解深度学习提供了新的概念工具，同时为机制可解释性研究指明了实证方向。

Abstract: Recent work in mechanistic interpretability has studied how large language models recall facts stored in their weights. This paper argues that factual recall points to something broader: a general kind of operation in deep learning models, which I call feature recall. The core observation is that a linear projection can be read as retrieving stored information scaled by input activations. I define feature recall, show it applies across architectures, and contrast it with the established paradigm of feature combination. I also consider how cases of feature recall might be mechanistically identified. The account gives philosophers a new conceptual tool for understanding deep learning, and points to empirical directions for mechanistic interpretability research.

</details>


### [204] [Belief Without Behavior: Measuring the Translation of Theory of Mind into Coordinated Social Action in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20975)
*Tonglin Yan,Gregoire Sergeant-Perthuis,David Rudrauf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MOSAIC基准，用于评估多模态社会互动中智能体的心智推理与行为协调能力。测试13个模型（含11个VLM）在合作/竞争场景下的表现，发现VLM在ToM约束下无法产生一致行为，且信号生成与解读存在双重瓶颈。而带显式ToM模块的PCM-LLM在所有条件下表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将心智理论（ToM）推理与具身行为分开评估，无法测量从社会推理到社会行为之间的鸿沟。真实社交互动需要同时整合语言、空间轨迹、注视方向和面部表情等多模态信号。

Method: 构建MOSAIC基准：两个具身智能体在合作与竞争场景中互动，系统性地变化ToM约束条件，整合言语、轨迹、注视和表情信号。对13个模型（11个VLM）各进行200次试验，并进行信号级分析及与带显式ToM模块的PCM-LLM架构对比。

Result: VLM无法在ToM阶数约束下产生符合预期结果的行为；显式ToM约束没有带来可靠的行为变化。信号级分析揭示两个顺序瓶颈：多数模型无法产生方向一致的非语言信号；即使有信号，VLM也无法解读他人行为并作出反应。PCM-LLM在所有条件下均成功。

Conclusion: 明确的心智-行为耦合（显式信念-行动关联）是该类任务成功的关键要素。现有VLM在社交推理到社交行动的转化上存在系统性缺陷，需要架构层面的改进而非仅依赖提示约束。

Abstract: Effective social interaction requires agents to translate mental state inferences into coordinated behavioral signals across verbal and nonverbal channels simultaneously. Yet existing benchmarks evaluate theory of mind (ToM) reasoning and embodied behavior in isolation, leaving unmeasured the gap between social inference and social action. We introduce MOSAIC (Multimodal Orchestration of Social Action, Inference, and Communication), a controlled benchmark in which two embodied agents interact across cooperative and competitive scenarios requiring integration of verbal statements, spatial trajectories, gaze direction, and facial expression under systematically varied ToM constraints. Evaluating 13 models, including 11 VLMs, across 200 trials per model, we find that VLMs fail to produce behaviors consistent with the expected outcomes under ToM-order constraints, and that imposing explicit ToM-order constraints produces no reliable behavioral change aligned with the specified reasoning level. Signal-level analysis reveals two sequential bottlenecks: most models cannot produce directionally coherent nonverbal signals, and even when signals are present, VLM agents fail to interpret others behaviors and react to them. PCM-LLM, included as a structured architectural reference point with an explicit ToM module, succeeds across all conditions, suggesting that explicit belief-action coupling is a sufficient ingredient for this class of tasks.

</details>


### [205] [Don't Solve, Just Compare: Tiny Advisors for Runtime Intervention in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21027)
*Yanze Jiang,Mingxuan Li,Yuhao Wang,Shengfang Zhai,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COTA（Comparison-Only Tiny Advisor），一个仅使用对比判断的轻量级运行时干预框架。COTA使用小型比较器对备选方案与actor提案进行成对比较，通过重复比较决定何时需要干预，并将优选方案作为非约束性建议返回给原始actor进行重新规划。在WebShop、ALFWorld和tau^3-Retail三个基准上，COTA在九个评估设置中均取得改进并超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在长时程真实任务中需要运行时干预来提高可靠性，但仅做失败检测不够，还需提供恢复方向。现有方法依赖专家求解器或生成任务特定修正的评论者，前者代价高昂，后者需要强大的任务能力。作者希望仅用轻量对比机制就能实现有效干预，避免额外大型模型的开销。

Method: COTA由一个极小的比较器构成，通过比较器判断采样的替代方案是否比actor的原始提案产生更好的后续结果，通过反复比较确定何时需要干预。比较器使用来自同前缀反事实分支的成对监督进行训练，优选替代方案以非约束性建议形式返回，由原始actor自行重新规划，无需重训底层actor。

Result: 在WebShop、ALFWorld和tau^3-Retail三个基准上使用三种不同actor进行测试，COTA在所有九个评估设置中均取得改进，并优于对比的基线方法，证明即使在辅助模型的任务解决能力显著弱于actor的情况下，建设性运行时干预仍然有效。

Conclusion: COTA表明建设性运行时干预可以仅依赖轻量级对比判断实现，即使辅助模型能力远弱于actor也能有效提升可靠性，为运行时干预提供了一种低开销且可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: LLM agents are emerging as an important paradigm for real-world tasks that require reasoning, tool use, and sequential decision-making. As these agents operate over longer horizons, runtime intervention offers a way to improve reliability without retraining the underlying actor. Failure detection alone is insufficient. Effective intervention must also provide a useful direction for recovery. Existing approaches often rely on an expert solver or a critic that generates task-specific corrections, incurring either the cost of another capable solver or the capacity demands of a task-capable critic. We introduce Comparison-Only Tiny Advisor (COTA), a comparison-only framework for constructive runtime intervention. In COTA, a tiny comparator judges whether sampled alternatives lead to better continuations than the actor's proposal, and repeated comparisons determine when intervention is warranted. We train the comparator using pairwise supervision constructed from same-prefix counterfactual branches. Preferred alternatives are returned as non-binding advice, leaving the original actor to replan. Across WebShop, ALFWorld, and tau^3-Retail with three actors, COTA improves all nine evaluation settings and outperforms the compared baselines. These results show that constructive runtime intervention can remain effective even when the auxiliary model has substantially weaker task-solving capability than the actor.

</details>


### [206] [Evaluating Large Language Model Performance on International Maritime Dangerous Goods Code Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21036)
*Alexander Thomas,Hubert P. H. Shum,Darren Nellis,Manli Zhu,Phatpicha Yochum,William Bartle,Daniel Wrightson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DGEval，这是首个用于评估大语言模型（LLM）对国际海运危险货物规则（IMDG）修正案42-24知识掌握程度的基准测试。该基准包含1,678道来自专家出题和危险货物清单的结构化查询题目，评估了13个模型在多种思考配置下的表现。结果表明，虽然最佳模型在多项选择题上超过人类从业者基线，但所有模型在积载、隔离和监管条文回忆等安全关键环节表现最弱，因此人类监督和权威来源核实仍是部署前的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危险品海运是高后果活动，受IMDG规则这一复杂监管框架约束，错误会导致火灾、爆炸、有毒物质泄漏等严重后果。从业者越来越多地使用LLM作为决策支持工具，但目前缺乏对LLM能否可靠解读IMDG要求进行系统性评估，尤其是在安全关键场景下的可靠性尚无验证。

Method: 构建DGEval基准测试，包含1,678道来自NCB Hazcheck电子学习平台的专家编写题目和危险货物清单（DGL）的结构化查询，涵盖多项选择、开放问答、DGL查询和法规识别四类任务。评估13个来自六家提供商的模型，测试多种思考配置，包括一个海运领域微调模型，并测试网络搜索对性能的影响。

Result: 最佳模型在多项选择题上超过人类从业者基线，但所有模型在积载（stowage）、隔离（segregation）和法规条文回忆（regulatory recall）等操作安全关键领域表现最弱。结构化DGL查询配合网络搜索时表现较好。

Conclusion: LLM可以支持合规任务，尤其是结合网络搜索的结构化DGL查询，但在操作领域和法规条文回忆方面存在不可靠性。任何安全关键场景部署前，必须保持人类监督和权威来源验证。DGEval应作为持续应用的安全保障工具而非静态能力评估。

Abstract: The transport of dangerous goods by sea is a high-consequence activity governed by the International Maritime Dangerous Goods (IMDG) Code, a complex regulatory framework where errors in classification, packaging, stowage, or segregation can result in fire, explosion, toxic release, or loss of life or vessel. Correct compliance requires accurately interpreting hundreds of pages of interacting provisions, updated on a two-year amendment cycle. Practitioners increasingly use Large Language Models (LLMs) as decision-support tools, yet no systematic evaluation exists of whether they can reliably interpret IMDG requirements for safety-critical use.
  This paper introduces DGEval, the first benchmark for evaluating LLM knowledge of IMDG Amendment 42-24. Built from expert-written questions on the NCB Hazcheck e-learning platform and structured lookups from the Dangerous Goods List (DGL), it comprises 1,678 questions across multiple-choice, open-ended, DGL lookup, and regulatory identification tasks. We evaluate 13 models from six providers across multiple thinking configurations, including one maritime domain-specific fine-tuned model, and test the effect of web search.
  Although the best-performing model exceeds the human practitioner baseline on multiple-choice questions, all models are weakest in the operationally safety-critical areas of stowage, segregation, and regulatory recall. These results indicate that LLMs may support compliance tasks, particularly structured DGL lookups with web search, but unreliability in operational areas and regulatory-text recall means human oversight and authoritative source verification remain necessary before deployment in any safety-critical context. DGEval is designed as a safety assurance instrument to be applied continuously as models evolve, not as a settled characterisation of current capability.

</details>


### [207] [Socialized Division and Collaboration: Rethinking Class-Incremental Learning under Optimization Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21044)
*Xinjie Yao,Zhihe Fan,Yunqi Zhu,Jiaqi Zhou,Dengyu Zhao,Zhoupeng Guo,Yan Fan,Guosong Jiang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出社会分工与协作（SDC）框架，重构类增量学习，将会话学习分解到多个专用模型中以应对优化冲突，并基于赫尔姆霍兹自由能建立能量化的会话-模型兼容性准则，实现自适应的会话分配、模型演化与协作推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单模型范式在连续会话产生不相容的优化方向时，会出现破坏性干扰和灾难性遗忘。作者认为这种遗忘源于在单一参数空间中强制异构学习动力学的结构性局限，因此需要一种新的公式化方法。

Method: 借鉴社会团结理论，提出SDC框架：1) 将冲突的会话学习分解到多个专用模型；2) 基于赫尔姆霍兹自由能设计能量化的会话-模型兼容性准则；3) 将会话分配、模型演化与协作推理整合为统一流水线。

Result: 论文为持续学习提供了不同于单模型范式的替代方案，验证了在持续优化冲突场景下多模型分解与协作的有效性。

Conclusion: SDC框架提出了在持续优化冲突下学习的更广泛设计原则，即通过模型的社交化分工与协作来替代单一参数空间的强制统一，为类增量学习提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning is commonly instantiated as a single-model paradigm, where a unified model sequentially adapts to an unbounded stream of sessions. While effective under mild distributional shifts, this formulation becomes strained when successive sessions induce incompatible optimization directions, leading to destructive interference and catastrophic forgetting. We argue that such forgetting reflects a structural limitation of enforcing heterogeneous learning dynamics within a single parameter space. Motivated by social solidarity theory, we propose Socialized Division and Collaboration (SDC) as a reformulation of continual learning that decomposes session learning across specialized models in response to optimization conflicts, while enabling coordinated collaboration. To support this formulation with a principled allocation mechanism, we introduce an energy-based session-model compatibility criterion grounded in Helmholtz free energy, which guides adaptive session allocation and model evolution under conflicting objectives. This framework integrates session assignment, model evolution, and collaborative inference into a unified pipeline, offering an alternative to monolithic continual learning formulations and highlighting a broader design principle for learning under persistent optimization conflicts.

</details>


### [208] [The Cost of a Physics Prior Is Bounded by the Ablation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21059)
*Boris Kriuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明形状约束/物理先验的精度代价主要源于"自由特征"和验证集划分方式，而非先验本身。通过认证的包含关系推导出0 ≤ P ≤ D的不等式，并实证于野火严重性预测任务（N=26,681），显示坐标特征作为"盾牌"可将代价降低7.3倍，且该代价高度依赖验证协议，不具可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将施加形状约束/物理先验的精度代价视为先验本身的内在属性，但作者认为这一代价主要取决于模型中的自由特征以及验证集的分割方式。这一误解会导致对先验成本的高估或错估，进而误导模型选型。

Method: 1）理论上证明：对任意风险泛函，受约束类别（带S形状约束）的类别包含消融模型（忽略S）的类别，因为常数函数既非递减也非递增，故0 ≤ P ≤ D成立，无需凸性、光滑性或可实现性假设。2）将该界转化为符号检验：约束模型绝不应被其自身消融版本击败。3）在野火严重性任务上实例化：对4个气象驱动变量施加硬单调约束，坐标特征自由，并使用从i.i.d.重采样到2度空间分块的验证阶梯。4）提出"两拟合筛查"（two-fit screen）在训练约束模型前筛除不可识别的实验。

Result: 坐标特征作为"盾牌"：单独使用可恢复全模型92.9%的macro-F1（空间分块下），将D从0.1288降至0.0427；同一先验在有盾牌时代价为0.0473，无盾牌时为0.3470，比例7.3倍但物理相同。D依赖协议不可迁移：分块从1度粗化到10度使D从0.0942降至0.0050，两种配置先验上不可识别。认证嵌套界的反演给出自校准下限0.0220 macro-F1（318次比较），低于此的任何代价报告不可解释。代价与合规性独立：无约束模型以0.48-0.49的比率违反先验，而强制其仅花费0.0473。

Conclusion: 形状约束的精度代价不是先验的内在属性，而是自由特征与验证协议的产物。论文提供了一种认证的嵌套界作为自校准基准，并给出了一个简单的两拟合筛查方法，可在训练昂贵的约束模型前识别并拒绝不可标识的实验设计。

Abstract: Shape-constrained and physics-informed learning reports an accuracy cost of enforcing a prior and treats it as a property of the prior. We show it is mostly a property of the free features and the validation split. Let P be the excess risk of restricting a hypothesis class to functions with a shape constraint on features S, and D the excess risk of the ablated model that ignores S. Because a function constant in x_j is both non-decreasing and non-increasing in x_j, the ablated class is contained in the constrained class, so 0 <= P <= D for every risk functional, with no convexity, smoothness, or realizability assumption. Empirically the bound is a sign test: a constrained model must never be beaten by its own ablation. We instantiate it on an ordinal wildfire-severity task (N = 26,681, K = 3) with hard monotone constraints on four meteorological drivers, coordinates left free, and a validation ladder from i.i.d. resampling to 2-degree spatial blocking. Coordinates act as a shield: alone they recover 92.9% of the full model's macro-F1 under spatial blocking, collapsing D from 0.1288 to 0.0427; the same prior costs 0.0473 shielded and 0.3470 unshielded, a ratio of 7.3 with identical physics. Because D is protocol-dependent it does not transfer: coarsening blocks from 1 to 10 degrees drives D from 0.0942 to 0.0050, leaving two configurations unidentifiable a priori. Inversions of the certified nesting bound the pipeline's additive resolution: over 318 comparisons they give a self-calibrating floor of 0.0220 macro-F1, below which no reported price is interpretable, including four cells in our own headline grid. Cost and compliance are independent: the unconstrained model violates the prior at rate 0.48-0.49 while enforcing it costs 0.0473. We give a two-fit screen that rejects unidentifiable experiments before a constrained model is trained.

</details>


### [209] [CellPath-Bench: A Multidimensional Benchmark for Whole-Slide Cellular Representations in Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21060)
*Bokai Zhao,Yiyang Zhang,Hanqing Chao,Yawei Ma,Long Bai,Tai Ma,Minfeng Xu,Ming Song,Tianzi Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CellPath-Bench，一个细胞分辨率的基准测试，用于系统评估病理基础模型（PFMs）在整张切片上编码细胞类型信息的能力及其跨组织、跨数据集、跨器官的可迁移性。基于25个H&E-Xenium对齐组织切片（11个器官，700多万个细胞），使用标准化线性探针评估30个基础模型，共30万+次运行，揭示了模型间显著的能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试无法系统诊断病理基础模型在整张切片上的细胞表征能力，特别是细胞类型信息的可解码性以及该信息在组织切片、数据集和解剖器官之间的可迁移性，缺乏标准化的细胞分辨率评估框架。

Method: 构建25个空间对齐的H&E-Xenium组织切片面板（11个器官，7,079,283个细胞），在注册核坐标处采样冻结的WSI特征图，使用标准化多类线性探针评估。提出两个核心指标：CRA（核锚定表征相对于patch级平均池化的细胞内优势）和CRT（细胞类型可解码性跨组织/数据集/器官的泛化能力）。

Result: 对30个病理专用和通用基础模型进行304,920次实验，涵盖空间读取方式、放大倍数、分类粒度和评估协议。结果显示模型间在细胞类型可解码性和跨域泛化方面存在显著差异，产生不同的多维能力画像。

Conclusion: CellPath-Bench提供了一个标准化框架，用于审计冻结PFM表征中的细胞信息，揭示了模型在细胞层面表征能力上的本质差异，为PFM的选择和改进提供指导。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) are increasingly used as general-purpose backbones, yet existing benchmarks cannot systematically diagnose their whole-slide cellular representation capabilities, including the decodability of cell-type information and the transferability of such information across tissue sections, datasets, and anatomical organs. We introduce CellPath-Bench, a cellular-resolution benchmark that evaluates frozen PFMs themselves. Following quality control of 52 candidate Xenium datasets, we construct a panel of 25 spatially aligned H\&E--Xenium tissue sections spanning 11 organs and 7,079,283 cells, harmonized into fine- and coarse-grained taxonomies. CellPath-Bench samples frozen WSI feature maps at registered nuclear coordinates and evaluates them using standardized multiclass linear probes. Cell Representation Advantage (CRA) measures the within-section advantage of nucleus-anchored representations over patch-level mean pooling, while Cell Representation Transferability (CRT) characterizes the generalization of cell-type decodability across tissue sections, datasets, and organs. We benchmark 30 pathology-specific and general-purpose foundation models through 304,920 runs across spatial readouts, magnifications, taxonomic granularities, and evaluation protocols. The results reveal substantial model-dependent differences in cell-type decodability and its cross-domain generalization, yielding distinct multidimensional capability profiles. CellPath-Bench provides a standardized framework for auditing cellular information in frozen PFM representations.

</details>


### [210] [Can Legal AI Know When It Is Wrong? And Do Students Know When It Is?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21089)
*Angel Mary John,Vipin Kumar Singh,Jerrin Thomas Panachakel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过两阶段社会技术审计，揭示LLM在印度司法中的"信心惯性"现象（类似邓宁-克鲁格效应的过度自信），提出高置信错误率（HCER）指标，发现Meta AI最易受"先例过拟合"影响（31.7% HCER且平均置信度9.1/10）。第二阶段对380名印度法学院学生的调查显示，验证行为常是对机器幻觉的反应性适应，且多数学生缺乏正式AI伦理培训，论文建议转向对抗性法律研究教学和源接地的验证架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM整合进印度司法体系虽有望促进司法可及性，但带来严重风险——尤其LLM可能以极高置信度提供错误法律判决，形成一种类似邓宁-克鲁格效应的过度自信现象（"信心惯性"），其根源被假设为"先例过拟合"偏差。作者旨在系统量化这一风险并评估人类对此类过度自信的脆弱性。

Method: 采用两阶段社会技术审计设计。第一阶段：在60个案例的测试组中，评估ChatGPT（GPT-5.2）、Meta AI和Perplexity AI在印度1872年《合同法》及特定履行法定强制执行转变方面的表现，引入高置信错误率（HCER）指标（置信度≥9/10时给出错误判决的比率）。第二阶段：对380名印度法学院学生进行问卷调查，考察其对机器幻觉的验证行为、对提交幻觉案例法律后果的认知及正式AI伦理训练情况。

Result: 所有模型在处理法定更新方面均有困难。Meta AI最脆弱（HCER达31.7%），常以9.1/10的平均置信度错误适用修订前规则；Perplexity为15.0%，ChatGPT为6.7%。学生调查显示，遭遇捏造引用的学生验证评分（4.2/5）显著高于未遭遇者（2.8/5），说明验证是对机器幻觉的反应性适应。81.6%的学生知道提交幻觉案例可能导致藐视法庭，但71.1%未接受过AI伦理使用的正式培训。

Conclusion: LLM在法律领域的过度自信不仅是技术缺陷，更是系统性专业疏忽风险。作者提议转向"对抗性法律研究教学法"以及实施"源接地验证架构"（source-grounded verification architectures），以预防法律专业人士在依赖LLM时产生系统性职业疏忽。

Abstract: Integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) into the Indian judiciary promises access to justice but introduces severe risks. We identify the 'inertia of confidence'--an overconfidence phenomenon analogous to the Dunning-Kruger effect where LLMs provide incorrect legal verdicts with near-maximum confidence, driven by a hypothesized 'precedent overfitting' bias. Phase I of our socio-technical audit tested ChatGPT (GPT-5.2), Meta AI, and Perplexity AI on a 60-case battery regarding the Indian Contract Act, 1872, and the shift toward statutory enforcement of specific performance. We introduce the High-Confidence Error Rate (HCER) to quantify incorrect verdicts delivered with dangerous certainty (>= 9 on a 1-10 scale). All models struggled with statutory updates. Meta AI proved most vulnerable (31.7% HCER), frequently misapplying pre-amendment rules with a 9.1/10 mean confidence, followed by Perplexity (15.0%) and ChatGPT (6.7%).
  Phase II investigated human vulnerability to this overconfidence via a survey of Indian law students (N=380). Verification often functions as a reactive adaptation to machine hallucinations: students encountering fabricated citations reported higher verification scores (4.2/5) than those with no such encounters (2.8/5). Furthermore, while 81.6% knew submitting hallucinated cases can lead to contempt-of-court, 71.1% received no formal training on ethical AI use. We propose shifting toward adversarial legal research pedagogy and implementing source-grounded verification architectures to prevent systemic professional negligence.

</details>


### [211] [When Trust Meets Truth: Trust-Truth Separability in LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21097)
*Xin Sun,Di Wu,Yuchen Guo,Jiahuan Pei,Isao Echizen,Abdallah El Ali,Saku Sugawara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究检验了LLM-as-Judge系统中信任评分与真值分类这两类判断是否相互独立。通过正确性受控的QA任务，发现LLM法官的信任评分与真值判定高度对齐，且比人类参考更紧密。进一步的压力测试表明，仅改变相同QA的来源线索，不仅影响信任评分，还会影响真值判定和logit衍生的正确侧概率，证明当前LLM-as-Judge协议不应将信任评分视为真值判断的独立证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-Judge系统产生多维度评估（如可信度、可靠性、事实性），这些输出常被当作相互独立的证据使用。作者想检验这一假设是否成立，特别是针对信任评分和二值真值分类这一常见判断对。

Method: 在正确性受控的QA任务上，对比LLM法官与人类行为参考在信任评分与真值判定之间的对齐程度；随后施加压力测试，仅改变人类或AI来源的线索（其余内容相同），观察信任评分、真值判定以及logit衍生的正确侧概率是否受影响。

Result: LLM法官的信任评分与真值判定比人类行为参考更紧密地对齐，说明两者区分度更弱；来源归属的改变不仅移动了信任评分，还改变了真值判定和logit衍生的正确侧概率。

Conclusion: 当前LLM-as-Judge协议不应将信任评分视为真值判断的独立证据，因为信任与真值判断之间存在较强的耦合关系，且来源线索会同时影响两者。

Abstract: LLM-as-Judge systems can produce multi-dimensional evaluations, such as trustworthiness, reliability, and factuality, and these outputs are often interpreted as independent evidence. We test this assumption for a common pair of judgments: trust scoring and binary truth classification. On correctness-controlled QA, LLM judges align trust scores with truth verdicts more tightly than human behavioral reference, suggesting weaker separations between trust and truth judgment. We then apply stress tests by changing only source cues of identical QA between Human and AI. Source attribution shifts not only trust scores but also truth verdicts and logit-derived correct-side probabilities. Results show that current LLM-as-Judge protocols should not treat trust scores as independent evidence for truth judgments.

</details>


### [212] [ReFrame: Evidence-Guided Test-Time Safety Alignment in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21100)
*Wenzheng Jiang,Xuankun Rong,Yuanzhao Zhai,Dawei Feng,Huaimin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReFrame，一种无需训练的多模态输入重框架框架，通过双Agent协作（证据生成Agent与重写路由Agent）在调用闭源MLLM前对输入进行安全重构，在不修改模型或访问其内部信息的前提下，同时改善越狱防御、安全意识和过度敏感拒绝三大安全对齐挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM安全对齐方法多依赖重训练或内部状态检查，无法应用于部署的闭源模型。作者识别出两个关键障碍：效用主导（utility dominance）和推理惯性（reasoning inertia），导致模型忽视潜在风险或遵循恶意推理轨迹，因此需要测试时的安全对齐方案。

Method: 提出ReFrame框架，采用两Agent架构共享一个轻量级本地部署的MLLM：(1) 证据生成Agent构建互补的风险与效用证据；(2) 重写与路由Agent将该证据转化为安全的代理提示词（proxy prompt）及图像路由决策，随后才调用下游闭源MLLM。整个过程无需修改模型或访问其内部信息。

Result: 在多个MLLM和基准上的实验表明，ReFrame在保持多模态效用的同时，提升了越狱防御能力、安全意识并减少了过度敏感拒绝。

Conclusion: ReFrame验证了训练-自由、黑盒的测试时安全对齐范式的可行性，为闭源MLLM的多模态安全提供了新路径，且无需访问模型内部状态。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend model capabilities beyond text, they also make safety alignment increasingly challenging. Multimodal safety alignment methods must address cross-modal jailbreaks, safety-awareness failures, and over-sensitive refusals. However, existing methods often rely on retraining or internal-state inspection, limiting their applicability to deployed closed-source MLLMs and motivating test-time safety alignment. We analyze this setting and identify two key obstacles, utility dominance and reasoning inertia, which cause models to overlook latent risks or follow malicious reasoning trajectories. Guided by these insights, we propose ReFrame, a training-free multimodal input reframing framework where two agents share a lightweight locally deployed MLLM: the evidence-generation agent constructs complementary risk and utility evidence, and the rewrite-and-routing agent converts it into a safe proxy prompt and image-routing decision before calling the downstream MLLM, without modifying it or accessing its internal information. Experiments across multiple MLLMs and benchmarks show that ReFrame improves jailbreak defense, safety awareness, and oversensitivity reduction while preserving multimodal utility.

</details>


### [213] [Large Language Models at the Intersection of Software Engineering and Software Security:An Evidence-Centered Structured Survey and Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21107)
*Wei Lin,Tao Zhou,Zhaofei Xie,Changgui Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇以证据为中心的结构化综述，系统梳理了截至2026年5月31日关于大语言模型（LLM）从代码补全向仓库级智能体演进的代表性工作。综述横跨软件工程任务、软件安全任务、适应机制、工件粒度和评估设计五个维度，提出任务分类学与保证框架（分离功能正确性、安全性、运行可靠性、证据溯源和智能体权限），揭示执行反馈与仓库访问能显著提升工程任务完成度但不足以保证安全，反之静态分析标签或漏洞分类分数也难以证明可部署的正确性。文章识别出多重效度威胁并提出最低报告协议，最终主张模型能力应作为由任务适配证据支撑的保证案例来评判，而非依赖单一基准分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估证据分散于软件工程（侧重功能任务完成）与软件安全（侧重漏洞检测、安全生成或利用验证）两个割裂的领域，缺乏统一的证据框架来理解仓库级智能体的能力边界与风险，亟需结构化综述弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 采用证据中心的结构化综述方法，按软件工程任务、软件安全任务、适应机制、工件粒度与评估设计五个维度系统梳理代表性工作，并提出任务分类学、保证框架及最低报告协议作为分析工具。

Result: 发现执行反馈和仓库访问能显著改善工程任务完成度，但本身不能确立安全性；反之静态分析标签或漏洞分类分数也难以证明部署级正确性。识别出若干反复出现的效度威胁（弱测试预言、数据重复与时间泄漏、智能体环境漂移、代理级安全检查、预算与人工干预报告不足），并据此提出跨研究比较的最低报告协议。

Conclusion: 模型能力不应以单一基准分数评判，而应作为由任务适配证据支撑的保证案例。研究议程优先聚焦联合安全与功能的基准、仓库级威胁模型、校准化人工监督、纵向可维护性证据及可复现的智能体评估。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are moving from code completion toward repository-scale agents that retrieve context, edit files, execute tools, and participate in security-sensitive workflows. The evidence for these systems, however, remains divided between software engineering evaluations centered on functional task completion and software security evaluations centered on vulnerability detection, secure generation, or exploit-oriented validation. This evidence-centered structured survey synthesizes representative work available through May 31, 2026 across software engineering tasks, software security tasks, adaptation mechanisms, artifact granularity, and evaluation design. In addition to a task taxonomy, we introduce an assurance framework that separates functional correctness, security, operational reliability, evidence provenance, and agent authority. The review shows that execution feedback and repository access can substantially improve engineering task completion, but do not by themselves establish security; conversely, static-analysis labels or vulnerability-classification scores rarely establish deployable correctness. We identify recurring validity threats--weak test oracles, duplicated and temporally leaked data, changing agent harnesses, proxy-only security checks, and under-reported budgets and human intervention--and derive a minimum reporting protocol for cross-study comparison. The resulting research agenda prioritizes jointly secure-and-functional benchmarks, repository-scale threat models, calibrated human oversight, longitudinal maintainability evidence, and reproducible agent evaluation. The central conclusion is that model capability should be judged as an assurance case supported by task-appropriate evidence, rather than by a single benchmark score.

</details>


### [214] [Root cause analysis via difference graph discovery from linear time-series data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21117)
*Anouk Ruer,Timothée Loranchet,Daria Bystrova,Charles K. Assaad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究线性时间序列中的根因分析，将其视为差异图发现问题，聚焦于因果系数在正常与异常状态间发生变化的"效应违背"根因变量，并评估了多种方法在模拟数据和真实IT监控、重症监护数据上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 根因分析旨在识别复杂动态系统中导致异常的机制，但现有方法对线性时间序列中因因果系数变化引起的根因缺乏系统性形式化研究。

Method: 使用线性离散时间动态结构因果模型形式化问题，将原本用于发现两个群体间差异图的方法适配到时间序列场景，用正常状态和异常状态替换两个群体，识别因果系数变化的变量。

Result: 在模拟数据上评估了所提方法，并在IT监控和重症监护监测的真实数据集上验证了实际价值，表明差异图发现有助于定位导致异常行为的因果机制。

Conclusion: 差异图发现方法能够有效帮助定位复杂动态系统中异常行为背后的因果机制，为时间序列根因分析提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Root cause analysis aims to identify the mechanisms responsible for anomalies in complex dynamical systems. In this paper, we study root cause analysis in linear time-series through the lens of difference graph discovery. We focus on effect-defying root causes, corresponding to variables whose causal coefficients change between a normal and an anomalous regime. We formalize this problem using linear discrete-time dynamic structural causal models and adapt several methods originally introduced for discovering difference graphs between two populations to the time-series setting, where the two populations are replaced by a normal and an anomalous regime. We first evaluate the proposed approaches on simulated data, and then demonstrate their practical relevance on real-world datasets from IT monitoring and intensive care monitoring. Our results show how difference graph discovery can help localize causal mechanisms responsible for anomalous behavior.

</details>


### [215] [From Attention Masks to Inert Zero-Vector Tokens: OAttention and O-Closure for Token Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21174)
*Heyang Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OAttention，一种基于token活跃系数（p_i = ||h_i||²/(τ+||h_i||²)）的注意力机制，使零向量token成为注意力的零元素，实现精确的null-receiver/null-source插入、自注意力插入和空支持等性质。通过残差与组合闭包构建OTransformer。在TabPFN v3回归器的零微调改造中，性能变化仅+0.088%（OAttention）和+0.177%（Full-O），验证了精确性、主动路径兼容性和组合必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力掩码仅提供关系级控制（指定哪些query-source对可以交互），但缺乏表示层面的token状态——即token在注意力边界处可以"不参与"的机制。作者希望构造一种注意力实现，使零向量token成为真正的零元素（zero element），从而支持精确的插入、移除和空支持等代数性质，为Transformer提供更严格的组合结构。

Method: 为每个token定义活跃系数p_i = ||h_i||²/(τ+||h_i||²)，同一系数双重角色：(1) 门控token i发出的信息，(2) 决定token i参与共享计算的质量。OAttention在接收端用p_i门控输出，在源端用p_j加权注意力分子和配分函数，同时保留标准score、可见性关系、指数竞争和值聚合。由此构建局部O组件（OFFN、ONorm、OInject）、O-Closure定律M(H⊕0)=M(H)⊕0，并通过残差和组合闭包构建OTransformer。

Result: 通过契约测试和GPU评估验证。在TabPFN v3回归器的零微调改造中，OAttention和Full-O变体在18个匹配数据集-种子案例上分别改变平均RMSE +0.088%和+0.177%。两模块消融实验表明：OAttention单独无法通过普通宿主组件保持NULL状态，而OTransformer路径可以。

Conclusion: 这些测试是范围限定的——验证了精确性、主动路径兼容性和组合必要性的可行性；但并未建立通用无损失、任意宿主闭包、学习到的原点吸引或缺失值的一般语义。OAttention提供了一个表征携带的token级"存在/缺席"机制，使零向量成为注意力的真零元素，是对注意力机制代数结构的补充性贡献。

Abstract: Attention masks are relation-level controls: they specify which query--source pairs may interact. They do not provide a representation-carried token state that is non-participating at the attention boundary. We assign each token hidden carrier \(h_i\) an active-presence coefficient \(p_i=\lVert h_i\rVert^2/(τ+\lVert h_i\rVert^2)\). The same coefficient has two roles: it gates information emitted by token \(i\), and it determines the mass with which token \(i\) enters computations shared with other tokens.
  OAttention is the support-coupled attention realization of this rule. It gates the receiver output by \(p_i\) and weights source \(j\) by \(p_j\) in both the attention numerator and partition, while retaining the standard score, visibility relation, exponential competition, and value aggregation. This makes the zero-vector token a zero element and yields exact null-receiver, null-source insertion, self-attention insertion, and empty-support properties. The same token-level presence gives local O-components (OFFN, ONorm, and OInject), presence-weighted OStandardize, the O-Closure law \(M(H\oplus0)=M(H)\oplus0\), and an OTransformer by residual and compositional closure.
  The canonical operator is checked by contract tests and a GPU evaluation. In a zero-fine-tuning retrofit of a cloned pretrained TabPFN v3 regressor, calibrated hidden-carrier OAttention and Full-O variants change mean RMSE by $+0.088\%$ and $+0.177\%$, respectively, over 18 matched dataset--seed cases. A two-block ablation shows that OAttention alone does not preserve a NULL state through ordinary host components, whereas the OTransformer path does. These are scoped tests of exactness, active-path compatibility, and compositional necessity; they do not establish universal no-loss, arbitrary-host closure, learned attraction to the origin, or a general semantics for missing values.

</details>


### [216] [Personalized Privacy Control in LLMs via Attention Head Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21209)
*Junseok Kim,Nakyeong Yang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"个性化隐私"（personalized privacy）概念，将用户特定的披露偏好纳入隐私控制，并构建了P3Bench基准来扩展上下文隐私策略。研究发现基于提示的策略无法可靠地执行个性化隐私策略（Qwen2.5-7B和Gemma3-4B的策略忽略率分别达51.25%和74.28%），为此提出Repair方法——一种鲁棒的推理时注意力头干预方法，可显著提高对用户特定隐私偏好的遵从度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文隐私研究仅关注LLM是否根据情境规范调节信息披露，但忽视了即使在相同情境下，不同用户的可接受披露边界也可能不同。因此需要将用户特定的披露偏好引入隐私控制。

Method: 1) 提出个性化隐私概念，将用户特定披露偏好整合进隐私控制；2) 构建P3Bench基准，将上下文隐私策略扩展为个性化披露策略；3) 提出Repair方法，一种推理时的注意力头干预技术，通过调整模型披露行为使其朝向符合策略的响应。

Result: 实验表明基于提示的策略无法可靠执行个性化隐私策略，Qwen2.5-7B和Gemma3-4B的平均策略忽略率分别高达51.25%和74.28%。Repair方法能显著改善模型对用户特定隐私偏好的遵从度，降低模型未能遵循给定策略的情况。

Conclusion: 个性化隐私是超越上下文隐私的重要方向，而现有的提示策略在强制执行个性化隐私策略方面存在严重不足；Repair作为一种推理时干预方法，能有效改善模型对用户隐私偏好的遵循。

Abstract: The rise of agentic AI enables LLMs to access diverse user data, raising critical privacy concerns. Prior work on contextual privacy studies whether LLMs regulate information disclosure according to context-dependent norms. However, acceptable disclosure boundaries may vary across users even within the same context. To address this limitation, we introduce \textit{personalized privacy}, which incorporates user-specific disclosure preferences into privacy control. We further present P3Bench~(\textbf{P}ersonalized \textbf{P}rivacy \textbf{P}reservation \textbf{Bench}mark), a novel benchmark extending contextual privacy policies with personalized disclosure policies. Experiments show that prompt-based policies fail to reliably enforce personalized privacy policies, with Qwen2.5-7B and Gemma3-4B showing average policy ignorance ratios of 51.25\% and 74.28\%, respectively. Finally, to address this problem, we propose \textsc{Repair}, a robust inference-time attention head intervention method that adjusts disclosure behavior toward policy-consistent responses. Our method significantly improves adherence to user-specific privacy preferences by reducing cases where the model fails to follow the given policy.

</details>


### [217] [SENTRY: Deterministic, Intelligent Risk Assessment for IT Change Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21203)
*Daniel Arulpragasam,Christer Henrysson,Ella Ly,Deepika Anbalagan,Leo Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SENTRY，一个用于大型金融机构变更管理的风险评估平台，用基于XGBoost和混合RAG的确定性机器学习流程替代主观的问卷评分，在ROC AUC达0.87、准确率85%，高风险变更检出率约为现有流程的3.25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型金融机构的技术变更管理依赖风险评估，但现有实践多采用自报问卷，主观性强、易被操纵、难以区分常规变更与后续引发重大事故的变更，亟需更准确、一致且可审计的评估方法。

Method: 构建SENTRY平台，使用XGBoost梯度提升决策树，结合结构化运营元数据、应用依赖图和历史事故记录；通过混合语义与词法检索（混合RAG）从历史变更请求中提取非结构化文本的风险信号，将其压缩为单一标量特征再输入模型，保持模型确定性，并利用SHAP值提供逐预测可解释性。

Result: 在企业级变更数据上，SENTRY实现ROC AUC 0.87和85%整体准确率，高风险变更检出率约为现有流程的3.25倍。

Conclusion: 作者最后审视了该设计背后的架构权衡，并讨论其对受监管变更管理场景中使用机器学习的启示。

Abstract: Technology change management in large financial institutions depends on risk assessments that are accurate, consistent, and auditable. In practice, many institutions still rely on self-reported questionnaires. Those questionnaires are subjective, easy to game, and poor at separating routine changes from the ones that later trigger major incidents. This paper presents SENTRY, a risk assessment platform that replaces questionnaire-based scoring with a deterministic machine learning pipeline built from gradient-boosted decision trees (XGBoost) and hybrid retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The system combines structured operational metadata, application dependency graphs, and historical incident records with a hybrid semantic and lexical search over historical change requests. The retrieval step captures the risk signal in unstructured change request text, then compresses that signal into a single scalar feature before model inference. That design keeps the model deterministic and preserves per-prediction explainability via SHAP values. Evaluated on enterprise-scale change data, SENTRY achieves a ROC AUC of 0.87 and 85% overall accuracy, and it detects high-risk changes at roughly 3.25 times the rate of the existing process. We close by examining the architectural trade-offs behind this design and what they imply for the use of machine learning in regulated change management.

</details>


### [218] [Enhancing LLMs in Predictive Political QA with Semi-Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21218)
*Yinan Liu,Zihan Zhou,Zichun Jin,Xinyu Wang,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PSL框架，一种双视角方法，用于预测性政治问答任务。PSL从语义视角提取政治行动者的立场信号，并从向量视角学习行动者交互图中的高阶结构感知表示，将外部政治资源转化为面向推理的证据，超越传统事实查找和知识库增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM增强方法（如基于行动者画像的模拟和知识图谱证据注入）将外部政治资源仅视为基于知识的证据，未能充分建模预测相关的信号，如行动者立场和行动者间的间接依赖关系。

Method: PSL是一个双视角框架：(1) 语义视图：从与问题相关的行动者记录中提取立场信号；(2) 向量视图：从行动者交互图中学习结构感知的行动者表示。该框架将半结构化政治记录转化为面向推理的证据，供LLM使用。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集和多种LLM上，PSL一致优于基线方法。消融实验证实立场信号和结构信号具有互补性增益。

Conclusion: 立场信号与结构信号是预测性政治QA中互补且有效的两类信号，通过双视角框架整合可显著提升LLM的政治推理能力。

Abstract: Predictive political question answering (QA), such as predicting how a political actor will vote, goes beyond factual lookup. External political resources offer rich historical evidence, but rarely contain the answer itself. Existing LLM augmentation methods, including actor-profile-based simulation and knowledge graph evidence injection, improve political reasoning but largely treat external resources as knowledge-based evidence, leaving prediction-relevant signals under-modeled. We identify two complementary signals for predictive political QA: actor stances that capture issue-specific preferences, and high-order structure signals that capture indirect dependencies among political actors. We propose PSL, a dual-view framework that converts semi-structured political records into inference-oriented evidence for LLMs. PSL extracts stance signals from question-relevant actor records in a semantic view, and learns structure-aware actor representations from an actor interaction graph in a vector view. Across three real-world datasets and multiple LLMs, PSL consistently outperforms baselines, with ablations confirming the complementary gains of stance and structure signals.

</details>


### [219] [Ontology-supported AI Model and Dataset Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21224)
*Jan Novacek,Ali Ahari,Tobias Müller,Sebastian Reiter,Alexander Viehl,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个AI模型交换平台，该平台利用本体论（ontology）来促进AI模型和数据集的存储、交换、发现与管理，旨在解决工业场景中AI资产交换时存在的语义鸿沟问题，并通过实时关键系统的用例验证其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI方法的快速发展和广泛应用，各组织产生了大量需要追踪、管理和交换的AI资产（如模型、数据集等）。当前缺乏有效的机制来发现和交换相关资产，且在工业环境中存在语义鸿沟，导致对AI模型及相关资产的共同理解不足。因此，需要研究如何有效地交换和管理AI模型及相关资产。

Method: 作者构建了一个AI模型交换平台，该平台支持AI模型和数据集的存储、使用、交换和分析。平台核心创新在于集成了一套本体论（ontology），用以建立对AI资产管理的共同语义理解，从而帮助用户发现和交换相关资产，解决语义鸿沟问题。

Result: 通过一个实时关键系统场景的用例，展示了该平台在实践中的效用，验证了平台能够支持AI模型的有效交换和管理。

Conclusion: 该平台为工业场景中的AI模型交换提供了一个基于本体论的解决方案，有助于弥合语义鸿沟，促进AI资产的透明比较和深入理解，未来有望推动AI方法的持续进步。

Abstract: Recently, there has been a great deal of research into improving AI methods and their application. The main focus is on tracking progress, enabling transparent comparisons, and fostering a more profound understanding of AI. In that process, different organizations generate and use plenty of assets that need to be tracked, traced and managed. Moreover, it is important to discover assets relevant for the task at hand. This paper presents research aiming to contribute to answering the question of what is required to exchange and manage AI models and related assets effectively without semantic gaps in an industrial context. We introduce a platform for AI model exchange, which facilitates the usage, exchange, and analysis of AI models and datasets. The platform incorporates an ontology that can foster a more profound common understanding of what is required in these tasks and help tackle the issues mentioned above. Finally, we elucidate the utility of the platform through the illustration of a use case in the context of real-time critical systems.

</details>


### [220] [Fine-Grain GPU Parallelization of the Generalized Partition Crossover for Large-Scale Traveling Salesman Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21233)
*Swetha Varadarajan,Darrell Whitley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GPU的细粒度并行实现，用于大规模TSP问题中广义划分交叉（GPX）算子的分区阶段。通过将GPX分区重构为图并行问题（合并父代环游、分裂四度顶点、删除公共边、连通分量分析），在CUDA上实现并行化，在1万至200万城市实例上获得48x到625x的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于遗传算法（如EAX）的TSP求解器在标杆实例上表现优异，但在大规模并行架构上的可扩展性受限，因为交叉操作涉及不规则内存访问、图遍历和顺序依赖。现有GPU求解器主要利用种群级并行，且仅限于小规模问题。因此需要实现算子级并行以提升大规模TSP的可扩展性。

Method: 将GPX算子的分区阶段重构为图并行问题，采用合并内存布局（coalesced memory layouts）、虚拟节点变换（ghost-node transformations）和连通分量分析。在CUDA上并行实现四个核心步骤：合并父代环游、分裂四度顶点、删除公共边、识别重组分量。

Result: 在10,000到2,000,000个城市的实例上进行实验，相比朴素顺序CPU实现，GPU分区实现获得48x到625x的加速比，同时显著降低内存开销。

Conclusion: 算子级并行能够显著提升基于遗传算法的TSP求解器在现代众核架构上的可扩展性，证明了在GPU上实现细粒度TSP交叉算子并行化的可行性和高效性。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is one of the most extensively studied NP-hard optimization problems. Genetic Algorithm (GA)-based solvers, such as the Edge Assembly Crossover (EAX), achieve state-of-the-art performance on many benchmark instances. However, the scalability of these approaches in massively parallel architectures remains limited because crossover operations involve irregular memory access patterns, graph traversals, and sequential dependencies. Existing GPU-based TSP solvers primarily exploit population-level parallelism and are limited to relatively small problem sizes. This work presents a fine-grain GPU implementation of the partition phase of the Generalized Partition Crossover (GPX) operator for large-scale TSP instances. The proposed approach reformulates GPX partitioning as a graph-parallel problem using coalesced memory layouts, ghost-node transformations, and connected-component analysis. The im- plementation parallelizes the union of parent tours, the splitting of degree- four vertices, the deletion of common edges, and the identification of recombining components using CUDA. Experimental results on instances ranging from 10,000 to 2 million cities demonstrate substantial acceleration over a naive sequential CPU imple- mentation. The proposed GPU partitioning achieves speedups between 48x and 625x while significantly reducing memory overhead. The re- sults demonstrate that operator-level parallelism can substantially im- prove the scalability of GA-based TSP solvers on modern many-core architectures.

</details>


### [221] [CLEAR: Continuous Latent Adapter Routing for Utility-Preserving LLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21278)
*Chengxiao Wang,Enyi Jiang,Xiaojing Liao,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR框架，通过轻量级隐藏状态门控连续控制安全LoRA适配器的激活强度，在不显著损害模型utility的前提下降低有害输出，改善了LLM安全性与实用性之间的权衡。在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上，CLEAR将HarmBench ASR从32.3%降至0.5%，同时比全局SFT或LoRA在GSM8K上高出最多7.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局应用的安全微调（如SFT或LoRA）在提升LLM安全性时会对良性输入也产生负面影响，导致utility下降。现有方法无法在安全性和实用性之间取得良好平衡，因此需要一种条件性安全适配机制。

Method: CLEAR使用一个轻量级隐藏状态门控（hidden-state gate）来连续控制安全LoRA适配器的激活强度。该方法保持冻结主干网络不变，仅通过门控机制动态调节安全适配器对模型输出的影响程度，从而对有害输入施加更强约束，对良性输入尽可能不干预。

Result: 在HarmBench上CLEAR将ASR从32.3%降至0.5%；在utility方面，相较于全局SFT或LoRA，CLEAR在GSM8K上的准确率最高提升7.1个百分点，同时保留基础模型的大部分utility。

Conclusion: CLEAR通过条件性的安全适配路由机制有效改善了LLM对齐中的安全-效用权衡，是一种有前景的安全适配方案。

Abstract: Improving the safety of large language models (LLMs) often comes at the expense of utility, as globally applied safety tuning may affect model responses to both harmful and benign inputs. We propose \textbf{C}ontinuous \textbf{L}at\textbf{E}nt \textbf{A}dapter \textbf{R}outing (CLEAR), a conditional safety adaptation framework that uses a lightweight hidden-state gate to continuously control the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter. CLEAR aims to reduce harmful completions while avoiding unnecessary changes to the frozen backbone that could degrade performance on benign prompts. Experiments on widely used safety and utility benchmarks show that CLEAR improves robustness on HarmBench while reducing the utility degradation observed with globally applied safety tuning such as SFT or standard low-rank adaptation (LoRA). On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, CLEAR reduces HarmBench ASR from 32.3\% to 0.5\%, while retaining most of the base model's utility and achieving up to 7.1 percentage points higher GSM8K accuracy than globally applied SFT or LoRA. These results suggest that CLEAR is a promising mechanism for improving the safety--utility trade-off in LLM alignment.

</details>


### [222] [AUSO: Action-Level Unified Skill Optimization from Internalization to Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21292)
*Huizu Lin,Chengkai Huang,Tianqi Gao,Tao Huang,Daijiao Liu,Tongxin Li,Xiaoyan Sun,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AUSO提出了一种行动级别的统一技能优化框架，通过渐进式、行动感知的训练过程将技能学习与技能使用统一起来，使技能从外部监督逐步过渡为决策知识，同时保持强化学习作为共享主干，在ALFWorld、WebShop和SearchQA上取得了优于基线的一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未能建模技能在智能体策略演化过程中扮演的不同角色（可学习知识、能力形成、决策改进）。它们要么将技能保留在模型外部、要么完全内化、要么通过任务级别的噪声成功率在内部化与利用目标之间选择，导致训练碎片化，且对同一轨迹中的行动赋予统一重要性，忽略了技能可能对某些决策有帮助而对其他决策造成干扰的问题。

Method: AUSO通过三个渐进阶段实现技能学习与技能使用的统一：1) 训练初期联合学习教师引导与环境反馈，获取基础技能；2) 随后强调基于结果的策略优化以巩固自主问题解决能力；3) 策略成熟后，在每个采样行动上同时评估技能条件与无技能上下文，将行动级别的信息信号与轨迹结果优势耦合，使有益的技能敏感行动获得更强更新，有害行动被抑制。强化学习作为所有阶段的共享主干。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和SearchQA三个基准上，AUSO相比竞争基线持续提升了智能体性能以及分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 技能在智能体策略演化中应从外部监督源逐步转变为决策知识，其利用应当基于行动级别的收益进行自适应调整。AUSO通过行动级渐进式优化统一了技能学习与使用，验证了这一生命周期建模的有效性。

Abstract: Skills play different roles as an agent's policy evolves: they should first provide learnable knowledge, then support capability formation, and finally be invoked only when they improve individual decisions. Existing methods rarely model this lifecycle. They either keep skills outside the model, fully internalize them, or select among internalization and utilization objectives through noisy task-level success rates. Such designs fragment training and assign uniform importance to actions within the same trajectory, even though skill guidance may help some decisions while distracting others. To solve these problems, we introduce AUSO (Action-level Unified Skill Optimization), which unifies skill learning and skill use through a progressive, action-aware optimization process. At the beginning of training, AUSO jointly learns from teacher guidance and environmental outcomes, enabling the policy to acquire foundational skills without losing task-oriented feedback. It subsequently emphasizes outcome-based policy optimization to consolidate autonomous problem-solving ability. As the policy matures, AUSO evaluates each sampled action under both skill-conditioned and skill-free contexts. The resulting action-level information signal is coupled with the trajectory outcome advantage, allowing beneficial skill-sensitive actions to receive stronger updates and harmful ones to be suppressed. Therefore, skills gradually transition from an external source of supervision into decision knowledge whose utilization is adapted to its action-level benefit, while reinforcement learning remains the shared backbone across all stages. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA show that AUSO consistently improves agent performance and out-of-distribution generalization over competitive baselines.

</details>


### [223] [From Regulation to Implementation: A Critical Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Regulatory Compliance in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21317)
*Adriana Watson,Marco Bücheler,Grant Richards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在生成欧盟合规文档（数字产品护照DPP和数据保护影响评估DPIA）时，数据提取指令和监管模糊性对输出质量和一致性的影响，发现监管标准宽松的场景需要更多上下文提示才能保持一致性，而标准严格的数据格式虽一致但容易产生幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟ESPR和GDPR等法规要求企业创建合规文档（如DPP和DPIA），但这些文档的创建面临工业数据异构分散、DPIA无标准格式且需跨学科知识等挑战。虽然已有研究提出用LLM辅助生成这些文档，但数据提取问题和监管模糊性对LLM输出的影响尚未被充分研究。

Method: 通过实验研究了两种关键因素（数据提取指令和监管规则模糊性）对LLM生成合规文档质量的影响。使用基准模型与人工创建的真实模式（ground-truth schemas）进行对比评估，涵盖DPIA（宽松标准）和数字电池护照DBP（严格标准）两类合规文档。

Result: 实验发现：对于宽松指南（如DPIA格式），需要更丰富的上下文提示来维持LLM输出的一致性和完整性；对于严格指南（如DBP格式），无论提示上下文如何都能产生一致结果，但更可能导致输出中的幻觉（hallucinations）。

Conclusion: 研究填补了LLM生成合规文档领域中关于监管模糊性和数据提取指令影响的空白，为不同严格程度的合规文档提供了差异化的LLM提示策略建议。

Abstract: The European Union (EU) has emerged as a leading regulatory body in the development of sustainability and privacy regulations. While new regulation requirements vary, many include a documentation artifact to ensure compliance. Notably, the Ecodesign for Sustainable Products Regulation (ESPR) introduces Digital Product Passports (DPPs) for life cycle transparency, while the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) mandates Data Protection Impact Assessments (DPIAs) to mitigate privacy risks. Creating these compliance artifacts, however, is challenging. Industrial data, which often exists in heterogeneous formats and is scattered across company and supplier systems, is required for DPPs and can be difficult to extract into compliant DPP formatting. Furthermore, DPIA documents require interdisciplinary expertise and follow no standardized format, making development difficult for novel systems. To address the particular complexity of compliance artifact creation for both regulations, researchers have proposed the use of LLMs in the generation process; however, the impact of the aforementioned problems on the output of these systems is largely unaddressed. This work investigates the existing research gap by exploring how data extraction instructions and regulatory vagueness impact the quality and consistency of LLM-produced compliance artifacts. The resulting artifacts are evaluated by benchmarking different models against manually created ground-truth schemas. The results reveal that less strict guidelines, such as DPIA formatting, require higher context prompts to maintain consistency and completeness. Stricter guidelines, such as formatting for Digital Battery Passports (DBP), result in consistent results regardless of prompt context, but may lead to more hallucinations in the output

</details>


### [224] [Unified Branch-and-Bound Search for the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem on Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21319)
*Jingtao Tang,Hang Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了凸集图上的斯坦纳旅行商问题（Steiner-TSP on GCS），提出了一种统一的基于分支定界（branch-and-bound）的搜索方法，在无限解空间中求解最小代价闭合轨迹。通过加性下界图代价和割分离的连通流松弛分别约束已承诺路径与剩余成本，并在均匀正代价假设下保证了有限终止性和ε最优性。在移动机械臂检测任务中验证了联合的传感模式、访问顺序和连续轨迹选择，性能优于两个近期基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Steiner-TSP处理离散图结构，但许多实际应用（如移动机械臂检测）需要在连续凸集上进行轨迹规划，且解空间是无限的。现有方法难以同时处理连续轨迹、可选中转顶点、重访以及传感模式等联合优化问题，需要一种统一的、具有理论保证的求解框架。

Method: 提出统一的分支定界搜索，在根行走前缀（rooted walk prefixes）上搜索。下限由两部分组成：加性下界图代价（bound committed prefixes）和割分离的连通流松弛（lower-bound the residual cost）。采用最佳优先遍历和深度优先遍历两种策略。对于用户指定的因子ε≥1，利用全局下界认证当前解的ε最优性。

Result: 在移动机械臂检测任务中，两种遍历策略均在30秒内对所有基准实例找到可行解，平均认证最优性差距分别为28.1%和29.7%，而两个近期基线仅在约一半的实例上成功。

Conclusion: 该框架在理论保证（有限终止、ε最优性认证）和实际性能（全实例可行、更优的求解率与最优性差距）之间取得了良好平衡，为连续空间中的斯坦纳TSP类问题提供了统一的求解范式，可扩展至更广泛的高维轨迹规划与任务分配场景。

Abstract: We formalize the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem (Steiner-TSP) on Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS), which seeks a minimum-cost closed trajectory through required convex sets while allowing optional transit vertices and revisits. To explore the resulting infinite solution space, we propose a unified branch-and-bound search over rooted walk prefixes. Additive lower-bound-graph costs bound committed prefixes, while a cut-separated connected-flow relaxation lower-bounds the residual cost of visiting every remaining target and returning to the root. Under a uniform positive-cost assumption, best-first traversal terminates after finitely many expansions on every feasible instance without an initial incumbent, whereas depth-first traversal does so once a finite incumbent is available. For a user-specified factor $ε\geq1$, a global lower bound certifies that either strategy's incumbent cost is at most $ε$ times the global optimum. We further demonstrate joint sensing-mode, visitation-order, and continuous-trajectory selection for a mobile-manipulator inspection task, including action precedences expressed in linear temporal logic over finite traces (LTL$_f$). Both traversal strategies find feasible solutions on all benchmark instances within 30s with mean certified optimality gaps of 28.1% and 29.7%, respectively, whereas two recent baselines succeed on only about half of the instances

</details>


### [225] [Anatomy-Informed Neural Networks: Encoding Anatomic Priors in Loss and Architecture, with an SE(3) Formulation of Guidewire-Induced Aortoiliac Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21332)
*David P. Stonko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出解剖学-informed神经网络（AINN），将软解剖先验作为损失函数中的惩罚项、硬解剖先验嵌入网络架构，用于解决数据稀缺情况下解剖模型数值可信但解剖学不可能的问题。作者以内腔导线插入时主动脉-髂动脉树的变形这一临床案例进行验证，利用SE(3)李群中的曲线框架建模，通过Cosserat-rod导线与血管的耦合弹性模型及Wasserstein-2最优传输损失，实现从2D血管造影训练3D预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习解剖模型在数据稀缺时会出现数值上合理但解剖学上不可能的预测，且泛化能力差。现有方法缺乏将解剖学知识系统性融入模型的手段，需要一种既能编码软性解剖先验（如异常但可能的解剖结构）又能编码硬性解剖先验（如血管连续性）的框架。

Method: 提出AINN框架，包含：(1)软解剖先验作为损失函数的惩罚项（类似物理信息神经网络）；(2)硬解剖先验嵌入网络架构和状态表示，使无效预测在构造上不可能。具体实现：将血管中心线和导线路径从R^3提升到SE(3)李群的曲线框架，用Cosserat-rod模型耦合导线与血管，通过单侧管腔接触不等式约束，预测为耦合弹性能量的约束最小化解。监督使用C-arm几何投影下预测与观测血管造影间的Wasserstein-2最优传输损失。

Result: 运动学、损失和投影函数已对照已知真值验证；力学求解器仅对照自身最优性条件验证，预测位移尚未达到网格收敛。该测试案例中未训练任何网络。

Conclusion: AINN为数据稀缺的解剖建模提供了一种新的范式，将解剖先验系统性地融入模型。当前工作完成了在硅片模型上的综合验证，未来将转移到真实CT扫描，检验其是否能提高预测精度并减少训练数据需求。该工作对主动脉手术和自主腔内导航具有重要意义。

Abstract: Deep-learning models of anatomy can be numerically plausible yet anatomically impossible, and they generalize poorly when data are scarce. We introduce Anatomy-Informed Neural Networks (AINN), in which soft anatomic priors enter as penalty terms in the loss (e.g., a branching penalty that treats a renal transplant artery off the iliac instead of the aorta as unexpected rather than impossible), in direct analogy to a physics-informed neural network, and hard anatomic priors (e.g., continuity of the vessel) are built into the architecture and state representation, making such invalid predictions impossible by construction wherever the prior admits architectural enforcement.
  We develop it on a clinical test case with limited data: how the aortoiliac tree deforms when a stiff wire is introduced endoluminally. This is important to contemporary aortic surgery and will matter to autonomous endovascular navigation. We lift the vessel centerline and the wire path from R^3 to curves of frames in the Lie group SE(3), and couple a Cosserat-rod wire to a tortuosity-modulated, anatomically anchored vessel through a unilateral lumen-contact inequality. The prediction is a constrained minimizer of the coupled elastic energy, with contact forces as its Lagrange multipliers. Supervision is a Wasserstein-2 optimal-transport loss between the predicted projection through the C-arm geometry and the observed angiogram, so a 2D angiogram can train a 3D prediction. The kinematics, loss and projection are verified against known ground truth; the mechanics solver only against its own optimality conditions, and predicted displacement is not yet mesh-converged. Here, no network is trained. Future work will transfer this in silico model to real CT scans and test whether it improves predictive accuracy and reduces the training data required.

</details>


### [226] [VIALS: A Benchmark for Visual Interpretation of Artifacts in the Life Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21357)
*Elaine Lau,Thanuka Udumulla,Lee Izhaki-Tavor,Francisco Guzmán,Nicholas Magazine,Jonas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了VIALS，一个包含161个视觉问答任务的基准测试，涵盖生物技术行业实验工作流中常见的各种科学视觉工件（凝胶印迹、显微镜图像、质粒图谱、流式细胞术图、分子结构等）。研究发现前沿视觉语言模型无法准确解释这些科学图像，而具备领域专业知识的科学家却能轻松完成这些任务，表明现有模型在领域知识和专业视觉推理方面存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家在日常工作中经常需要解读各种科学视觉工件（如凝胶印迹、显微镜图像等）来指导研究决策，但目前缺乏针对这些真实工作流场景的视觉问答基准。现有评估多使用来自出版物和教科书的精美图片，与生物技术行业的实际工作场景存在差距。研究者希望评估前沿视觉语言模型在这类专业任务上的表现。

Method: 构建VIALS基准，包含161个视觉解释任务，覆盖生物技术行业实验工作流中遇到的各种类型的科学工件（而非出版物中的精美图形）。通过对比前沿视觉语言模型与具备领域专业知识的科学家在这些任务上的表现来进行评估。

Result: 尽管前沿视觉语言模型能够流畅描述自然图像，但它们无法准确解释这些科学图像，反映出模型在领域知识和领域特定视觉推理能力方面的局限性。相比之下，具备相关领域专业知识的科学家认为这些视觉解释任务很简单。

Conclusion: 无法解释此类科学图像的AI在专业生命科学工作流中的实用性将受到限制，因为这些工件是科学家推理、沟通和决策的核心。这一发现凸显了开发具备领域专业知识的AI系统以支持生命科学工作流的必要性。

Abstract: In professional life sciences workflows, scientists routinely interpret visual artifacts (gel blots, microscopy images, plasmid maps, flow cytometry plots, molecular structures, ...) to inform research decisions. We introduce VIALS, a visual question-answering benchmark with 161 such interpretation tasks, spanning the types of artifacts examined throughout experimental workflows in the biotech industry (rather than polished figures from publications and textbooks). While frontier vision-language models can now fluently describe natural images, we find that they are unable to accurately interpret these scientific images, reflecting limitations in domain knowledge and domain-specific visual reasoning capabilities. In contrast, scientists with relevant domain expertise find these visual interpretation tasks straightforward. AI that cannot similarly interpret such images will have limited utility in professional life sciences workflows, where such artifacts are central to how scientists reason, communicate, and make decisions.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [A Hybrid Edge Cloud Digital Twin for Welfare-Constrained Control in Poultry Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20367)
*Suresh Neethirajan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向家禽养殖设施的边云数字孪生框架，用于实时的、受福利约束的环境控制。该框架整合了分布式感知、设备端状态估计、混合物理-数据模型和模型预测控制，通过灰箱热力学与质量平衡模型结合学习残差来捕捉未建模的生物变异性，实现边缘端的实时估计与控制及云端的跨场学习。在肉鸡生产测试台上，温度预测误差从1.8°C降至0.4°C，氨气约束违规减少90%，通信需求降低约30倍，领域迁移得分达0.92，展现出强大的跨设施鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家禽生产处于紧密耦合的环境与生物动态之中，但商业气候控制仍主要依赖启发式方法，难以保障动物福利和运营效率。现有方法缺乏对生物变异性（如活动依赖性代谢热）的建模能力，且在实际农场有限连接条件下难以部署先进控制策略。

Method: 提出边云数字孪生框架：(1) 灰箱热力学与质量平衡模型结合学习残差捕捉生物变异性；(2) 混合模型嵌入状态空间表示，支持边缘端实时估计与控制；(3) 模型预测控制实现前瞻性自适应管理；(4) 云端协调支持跨农场学习与长时域优化；(5) 带宽感知处理与异步同步机制适应受限连接环境。

Result: 在高保真肉鸡生产测试台上，与规则控制和纯物理模型相比：温度预测误差从1.8°C降至0.4°C；氨气约束违规减少90%；通过边缘优先处理使通信需求降低约30倍；领域迁移得分0.92表明跨设施条件鲁棒性强。

Conclusion: 物理基础的数字孪生结合实时控制，能够实现可扩展且关注福利的生物生产系统管理，验证了混合建模与边缘计算在农业环境控制中的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Poultry production operates under tightly coupled environmental and biological dynamics, yet commercial climate control remains largely heuristic, limiting welfare assurance and operational efficiency. We introduce an edge-cloud digital twin framework for real-time, welfare-constrained environmental control in poultry facilities. The framework integrates distributed sensing, on-device state estimation, a hybrid physics-data model, and model predictive control to enable anticipatory and adaptive management under practical farm constraints. A grey-box thermodynamic and mass-balance formulation is augmented with a learned residual that captures unmodeled biological variability, including activity-dependent metabolic heat. This hybrid model is embedded within a state-space representation for real-time estimation and control at the edge, while cloud coordination supports cross-farm learning and long-horizon optimization. Bandwidth-aware processing and asynchronous synchronization enable deployment in connectivity-limited environments. Evaluation in a high-fidelity broiler production testbed demonstrates substantial gains over rule-based control and physics-only modeling. Temperature prediction error is reduced from 1.8 degrees Celsius to 0.4 degrees Celsius, ammonia constraint violations decrease by 90 percent, and communication requirements are lowered approximately 30-fold through edge-first processing. A Domain Transfer Score of 0.92 further indicates strong robustness across facility conditions. These results show that physically grounded digital twins, coupled with real-time control, enable scalable and welfare-aware management of biological production systems.

</details>


### [228] [Infrared Hotspot-Guided Early Warning of Lithium-Ion Battery Thermal Runaway Under Mechanical Abuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20383)
*Syed Sajid Ullah,Salman Khan,Muhammad Zunair Zamir*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段锂离子电池热失控早期预警方法：先从红外热点动态估计局部热不稳定性，再将此不稳定性评分与机械、电气、热、图像强度特征融合，实现20帧预警窗口。热点动态单独达到Stage-I ROC-AUC 0.945，两阶段分类器达到Stage-II ROC-AUC 0.908，热梯度上升平均比电压检测提前40帧（4秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械滥用可在传感器信号产生决定性变化之前，通过局部热生成触发锂离子电池热失控（TR），传统检测方法响应滞后，急需更早的预警机制。

Method: 提出两阶段预警方法：第一阶段从红外热点动态估计局部热不稳定性评分；第二阶段将该评分与机械、电气、热、图像强度特征进行多模态融合。采用重复实验式三折交叉验证，并在第二阶段训练中使用折叠外（out-of-fold）的第一阶段评分以防止堆叠模型乐观偏差。

Result: 热点动态单独获得Stage-I ROC-AUC 0.945；两阶段分类器达到Stage-II ROC-AUC 0.908，优于直接多模态融合。热梯度上升平均比基于电压的检测提前40帧（4秒）；在固定0.5阈值下，平均提前时间为14.8帧。

Conclusion: 两阶段方法不仅提升了预警性能，还保留了可解释的中间不稳定性信号，为电池管理系统提供更早干预窗口，显著优于直接融合方案。

Abstract: Mechanical abuse can trigger thermal runaway (TR) in lithium-ion batteries through localized heat generation before sensor signals become decisive. This paper proposes a two-stage early-warning approach that estimates localized thermal instability from infrared hotspot dynamics and then fuses this instability score with mechanical, electrical, thermal, and image-intensity features for a 20-frame warning horizon. Evaluation uses repeated experiment-wise three-fold validation, with out-of-fold Stage-I scores during Stage-II training to prevent stacked-model optimism. Hotspot dynamics alone achieve Stage-I ROC-AUC 0.945, and the two-stage classifier reaches Stage-II ROC-AUC 0.908, exceeding direct multimodal fusion while preserving an interpretable intermediate instability signal. Thermal gradient rise precedes voltage-based detection by 40 frames (4 seconds) on average, enabling earlier battery management system intervention. Lead-time analysis at a fixed 0.5 threshold yields a 14.8-frame mean lead time.

</details>


### [229] [Learning-Based Measurement-Robust Control Barrier Functions for Obstacle Avoidance under State Estimation Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20467)
*Nicholas Rober,Yixuan Jia,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对安全关键系统中安全滤波器依赖完美状态信息的问题，提出了两种新的控制屏障函数（CBF）公式：漂移测量鲁棒CBF（DMR-CBF）和神经测量鲁棒CBF（NMR-CBF），用于在状态估计误差存在的情况下实现避障。DMR-CBF通过对漂移动力学中最坏情况不确定性进行内部优化来增强标准CBF条件，而NMR-CBF通过可微轨迹展开微调以降低保守性和计算成本。在平面双积分器和12D四旋翼上的数值实验以及在Unitree Go2上的实际部署均验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全滤波器大多假设完美的状态信息，这在实际系统中很少成立。虽然已有工作开发了对状态估计误差鲁棒的滤波机制，但随着估计误差增大，这些方法仍可能出现安全违规或过于保守的行为。因此需要设计新的CBF方法来平衡鲁棒性、保守性和计算效率。

Method: 提出两种CBF公式：(1) DMR-CBF：在标准CBF条件中加入对漂移动力学最坏情况不确定性的内部优化，增强对估计误差的鲁棒性；(2) NMR-CBF：由DMR-CBF指导预训练，用学习项替代内部优化，再通过可微轨迹展开进行微调，实现经验安全性与DMR-CBF相当但更低的保守性和计算成本。同时提供DMR-CBF的理论分析。

Result: 在平面双积分器和12D四旋翼上，两种方法均能防止碰撞，而其他鲁棒方法要么失败要么过度保守。在Unitree Go2四足机器人上实际部署NMR-CBF，在导致标准CBF碰撞的里程计误差下仍能成功导航障碍物区域。

Conclusion: 所提出的DMR-CBF和NMR-CBF能够有效解决状态估计误差下的安全滤波问题，NMR-CBF在保持安全性的同时降低了保守性和计算负担，并已在真实机器人平台上验证了实用性。

Abstract: Safety filters are an effective tool for enforcing constraints in safety-critical systems, but most existing methods assume perfect state information, which is rarely available in practice. Recent work has begun to close this gap by developing filtering mechanisms that are robust to state estimation error, but these methods can still exhibit safety violations or overly conservative behavior as estimation error grows. Focusing on obstacle avoidance, we develop two new control barrier function (CBF) formulations: drift-measurement-robust (DMR)-CBFs and neural measurement-robust (NMR)-CBFs. The DMR-CBF augments the standard CBF condition with an inner optimization over the worst-case uncertainty in the drift dynamics, improving robustness to estimation error. This DMR-CBF then supervises a pretraining phase for the NMR-CBF, which replaces the inner optimization with a learned term. The NMR-CBF is subsequently finetuned through differentiable trajectory rollouts, yielding a filter that achieves empirical safety comparable to the DMR-CBF while reducing both conservativeness and computational cost. We provide theoretical analysis of the DMR-CBF along with numerical results on a planar double integrator and a 12D quadrotor, where both proposed approaches prevent collisions while other robust methods either fail or are overly conservative. Finally, we deployed the NMR-CBF on a Unitree Go2, enabling successful navigation of an obstacle field under odometry errors that caused a standard CBF to collide.

</details>


### [230] [AoI-Oriented Globally Optimal Joint Source and Update Scheduling in Fluid Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20545)
*Xiaopeng Yuan,Paul Zheng,Anke Schmeink*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究流体天线辅助的状态更新系统，通过联合优化源节点激活/分配与更新周期，以最小化用户的最大平均信息年龄（AoI）。提出了基于不动点理论的二分算法和过滤算法，获得全局最优联合解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体天线作为一种前景广阔的技术，能够通过可重构的流体端口选择实现自适应的无线电环境管理和干扰抑制。本文旨在探索流体天线在数据新鲜度（Age of Information）增强方面的潜力，解决多源节点状态更新系统中源调度与更新周期联合优化的问题。

Method: 1) 建立流体天线辅助的多源状态更新系统模型，所有用户采用基于最大SINR的流体端口选择；2) 将问题建模为最小化最大平均AoI的混合整数非线性规划；3) 给定源调度决策下，利用不动点理论设计高效的二分算法求解最优更新周期；4) 提出过滤算法剔除所有非最优的源调度决策，与最优更新调度结合得到全局最优解。

Result: 数值结果验证了所提方案的最优性，并展示了流体天线在提升数据新鲜度方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文通过联合优化源调度与更新周期，并结合流体天线的自适应端口选择能力，成功实现了系统最大平均AoI的最小化，为流体天线在时效性敏感通信系统中的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: As a promising technique, fluid antennas enable adaptive radio environment management and interference mitigation through reconfigurable fluid port selections. In this work, to explore the benefits of fluid antennas for data freshness enhancement, we consider a fluid-antenna assisted status update system supported by multiple source nodes monitoring the same environmental status. We assume a subset of the source nodes are activated to report status updates with different periods. Each user is assigned to one source node and equipped with an fluid antenna to adaptively enhance the channel gain to assigned source node while mitigating interference from other activated source nodes. With maximal signal-to-interference-noise ratio (SINR)-based fluid port selection at all users, we formulate an optimization problem to minimize the maximum average age of information (AoI), where source node activation and assignment, i.e., source scheduling, is jointly optimized with the update periods of all activated source nodes. To optimally solve the resulting mixed-integer nonlinear problem, we first consider given source scheduling decision and apply performance achievability analysis. Aided by fixpoint theory, we propose an efficient bisection algorithm for optimal update scheduling. Based on these characterizations, we further propose a filtering algorithm which efficiently eliminates all non-optimal source scheduling decisions. The globally optimal joint solution is then obtained by combining the resulting optimal source scheduling with its corresponding optimal update scheduling. Numerical results validate the optimality of proposed solution and demonstrate the effectiveness of fluid antennas in enhancing data freshness.

</details>


### [231] [Wireless Power Transfer in Titanium Implants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20550)
*R. W. Porto,L. Murliky,F. R. de Sousa,A. S. de Almeida,H. M. de Albuquerque,V. J. Brusamarello*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究导电块体内嵌入式设备的无线功率传输问题，重点分析了涡流效应如何降低耦合系数和传输效率。以钛植入物为案例，设计了输出1mA恒流的负载，并通过实验对比多种缓解涡流影响的方案，结果表明若不精心设计接收器的腔体几何结构，该系统在技术上可能不可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导电块体（如金属植入物）内部的无线功率传输面临涡流产生的反向磁场，严重削弱耦合系数和传输效率，亟需研究可行的解决方案。

Method: 以钛植入物为案例，设计恒流1mA的负载电路；搭建实验装置，对比接收器嵌入植入物后，采用多种缓解或避免涡流效应的方案，测量并比较电气参数差异。

Result: 实验表明，若不仔细设计接收器所在腔体的几何结构，系统在技术上可能不具备可行性；腔体几何对涡流效应和传输效率有决定性影响。

Conclusion: 腔体几何设计是导电块体内无线功率传输可行性的关键因素，需要特别注意涡流效应的缓解。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of applications that require wireless power transfer to devices embedded within conductive blocks. The emergence of eddy currents tends to generate a magnetic field opposing that of the transmitter, drastically reducing the coupling coefficient and, consequently, the efficiency of the process. A case study involving a titanium implant is presented and analyzed with a load designed to ensure a constant current of 1 mA. An experimental setup is assembled and evaluated to compare differences in the measured electrical parameters when the receiver is embedded in the implant, considering several solutions that mitigate or avoid the effects of eddy currents. The results show that such a system may be technically unfeasible if careful attention is not paid to the geometry of the cavity in which the receiver is positioned.

</details>


### [232] [A Study on THz Plasmonics in a CMOS Continuum Transistor Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20796)
*Hossein Naghavi,Shruti Chakraborty,Zainulabideen Khalifa,James Gruber,Aditya Varma Muppala,Chen Shen,Denil Sabu,Andreia Cathelin,Ehsan Afshari*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对CMOS在太赫兹（THz）频段的局限性，利用CMOS晶体管电子通道的等离子体特性，提出了一种基于连续晶体管阵列（CTA）的等离子体波放大方案。通过水动力学输运模型结合Pierce理论描述等离子体波传播，并在28nm FD-SOI工艺上设计制造了工作在700GHz的概念验证放大器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMOS在THz频段面临电荷渡越时间和寄生电容对最高工作频率（fmax）的根本限制，且随晶体管尺寸缩小，电流处理能力下降，传统电路设计方法难以满足THz频段需求，亟需新型放大机制。

Method: 利用28nm FD-SOI CMOS晶体管形成连续晶体管阵列（CTA），利用电子通道的等离子体波特性实现THz信号放大；采用水动力学输运模型结合Pierce理论建立等离子体波沿CTA传播的数学描述；通过门控放大器仿真验证先进工艺节点下的放大潜力，并设计制造700GHz的概念验证放大器。

Result: 仿真结果显示门控放大器在先进工艺节点下具备THz信号放大潜力；实际制造的概念验证等离子体波放大器工作在700GHz，并沿等离子体波传播路径表现出放大特性。

Conclusion: 基于等离体波效应的CTA方案为CMOS在THz频段的信号放大提供了一种可行的替代路径，突破了传统fmax限制，展现了CMOS技术在THz应用中的新可能性。

Abstract: This work addresses the limitations of CMOS at terahertz (THz) frequencies, where charge transit time and parasitic capacitances restrict the maximum operating frequency, fmax. As transistor dimensions shrink, reduced current handling capabilities further challenge CMOS, necessitating novel circuit design approaches for the THz domain. By leveraging the plasma characteristics of electron channels in CMOS transistors, this study explores a potential solution for THz signal amplification. Key mechanisms in plasma wave amplification within a continuum transistor array (CTA) formed by 28 nm fully depleted silicon-on-insulator (FD-SOI) CMOS transistors are investigated. A hydrodynamic transport model combined with Pierce's theory is presented to describe plasma wave propagation along the CTA. Simulations of gated amplifiers demonstrate the potential for THz signal amplification in advanced fabrication nodes. Finally, a proof-of-concept plasma wave amplifier operating at 700 GHz has been designed and fabricated, exhibiting amplification along the plasma wave propagation path.

</details>


### [233] [Sharing the Control Authority Between Deep Reinforcement Learning and Model Predictive Control: Application to Multi-Class Transportation Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20858)
*Giray Onur,Azita Dabiri,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多类交通网络的DRL-MPC分层控制框架，MPC在上层低频输出控制，DRL在下层高频输出控制，兼顾DRL的快速在线计算与模型无关性，以及MPC的优化与约束处理能力。在模型失配和噪声需求场景下，该框架优于混合状态反馈MPC控制器，相比分层MPC大幅降低在线计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多类交通网络（混合车型）是非线性、高维的复杂系统，现有DRL在大规模网络和时间受限训练下学习能力有限，MPC计算开销大且依赖精确系统模型，而实际复杂系统往往难以获得准确模型，因此需要一种兼顾两者优势的控制框架。

Method: 提出分层DRL-MPC框架：MPC位于上层，以低频更新率生成控制输入（适应其高计算时间）；DRL位于下层，利用快速在线推理生成高频控制输入。两者分工协作，共同完成多类交通网络的控制任务。

Result: 在多类高速路网上与分层MPC控制器和混合状态反馈MPC控制器对比，该框架表现优于混合状态反馈MPC控制器，相比分层MPC显著减少在线计算时间，且在模型失配情况下能更有效地实施约束。

Conclusion: DRL-MPC分层框架有效结合了DRL与MPC的互补优势，是在多类交通网络等复杂系统中实现实时控制且无需精确模型的一种可行方案。

Abstract: Transportation networks, in particular multi-class transportation networks (i.e., networks with mixed vehicle types), are complex systems that are challenging to control. Recently, Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL), which learns control policies from interactions with the environment, and Model Predictive Control (MPC), which uses a system model to optimize control inputs, have been increasingly utilized for transportation network control. However, nonlinear system dynamics and high-dimensional state spaces in large-scale networks limit DRL's learning capacity under time-constrained training and increase MPC's computation time, hindering real-time implementation with limited computational resources. Moreover, MPC depends on an accurate network model, which is often unavailable for complex systems such as multi-class transportation networks. This paper proposes a novel DRL-MPC framework for multi-class transportation networks that divides control authority between DRL and MPC, combining DRL's fast online computation and model independence with MPC's built-in optimization and constraint-handling capabilities. In the hierarchical framework, MPC operates at the higher level and determines low-frequency control inputs whose slower update rate accommodates its high computation time, while DRL operates at the lower level and determines high-frequency control inputs using its fast online deployment. The framework is evaluated on a multi-class freeway network against a hierarchical MPC controller and a hybrid state-feedback-MPC controller, including scenarios with model mismatch and noisy traffic demands. Results show that the proposed framework outperforms the hybrid state-feedback-MPC controller, substantially reduces online computation time compared with the hierarchical MPC controller, and provides more effective constraint enforcement under model mismatch.

</details>


### [234] [Parameters Overshadowed by Price Lags: Load, Climate, and Calendar Effects in ERCOT Day-Ahead Price Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20865)
*Yunus Bicen,Eman Hammad*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可解释人工智能(XAI)的体制敏感框架，用于揭示电价预测中被历史价格滞后项掩盖的物理驱动因素。作者使用直方图梯度提升回归器(HGBR)分析ERCOT日前市场2014-2024年数据，并引入"抑制比"(Suppression Ratio)量化价格滞后对物理参数预测能力的遮蔽效应。研究发现，滞后项在正常条件下提供短期记忆但对极端价格动态失效，甚至增加了价格尖峰时的预测误差；且价格尖峰并非单纯由温度持续上升导致，而是系统在特定温度范围内的剧烈反应所致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电价预测模型过度依赖历史价格滞后项，这些滞后项往往掩盖了市场波动背后的物理驱动因素（如负荷、气候、日历变量），导致对极端价格事件的预测能力不足。作者希望超越滞后驱动的范式，揭示极端价格事件的真实成因。

Method: 采用基于直方图的梯度提升回归器(HGBR)作为预测模型，引入"抑制比"(Suppression Ratio)量化价格滞后对物理参数预测贡献的遮蔽程度；通过体制敏感(regime-sensitive)的XAI框架，在正常与极端市场状态下分别分析负荷、气候和日历变量对价格的影响。

Result: 滞后项在正常市场条件下提供短期记忆能力，但在价格尖峰期间不仅无法捕捉极端动态，其纳入反而增大了MAE/RMSE预测误差。通过隔离滞后效应，研究发现价格尖峰的成因并非温度持续上升，而是系统在特定温度区间内发生剧烈反应所致。

Conclusion: 研究强调了超越基于滞后项模型的必要性，主张在电价预测中更多地关注物理驱动因素，以真正揭示极端价格事件的根本机制，从而提升智能电网的稳定性与预测可靠性。

Abstract: Accurate electricity price forecasting is critical for smart grid stability, yet the heavy reliance on historical price lags in modern predictive models often obscures the fundamental physical drivers of market volatility. This paper proposes a regime-sensitive, explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) framework to unmask the hidden roles of load, climate, and calendar variables in the ERCOT Day-Ahead Market (2014-2024). Utilizing a Histogram-based Gradient Boosting Regressor (HGBR), we introduce a Suppression Ratio to quantify how price lags overshadow physical parameters. Our analysis reveals that while lags provide short-term memory during normal conditions, they fail to capture extreme dynamics; notably, their inclusion increased forecasting error (MAE/RMSE) during price spikes. By isolating these effects, the study revealed that price spikes are not merely the result of continuously rising temperatures, but rather emerge from the system reacting sharply within a specific temperature range. These findings underscore the importance of moving beyond lag-based models to uncover the true drivers of extreme price events.

</details>


### [235] [A Safety-Driven Architectural Framework for Fail-Operational Drone Swarms in Critical Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20906)
*Luiz Giacomossi,Zafer Yigit,Marwan Shakarna,Shoaib Saleemi,Ivan Tomasic,Baran Çurüklü,Håkan Forsberg*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合关键性架构框架，将SAE ARP4754B适航认证方法应用于无人机集群重构。通过硬件隔离的安全监视器作为运行时保障（RTA）网关，将飞行关键核心与非确定性集群管理器解耦，并通过形式化安全契约和健康向量传播实现故障可操作任务重新分配。马尔可夫可靠性建模表明该方案理论上能达到每飞行小时10^-7次失效的危险目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机集群的安全关键操作认证需要可验证的设计保证，但适航标准要求确定性可靠性，而多智能体协调算法本质上是非确定性的，两者之间存在根本性矛盾。需要一种架构来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出混合关键性架构框架：(1) 硬件隔离的安全监视器充当RTA网关，解耦飞行关键核心与非确定性集群管理器；(2) 基于功能危险评估（FHA）系统化推导智能体健康向量，并以此执行形式化安全契约；(3) 将健康向量传播给集体规划器，触发故障可操作的任务重新分配。

Result: 马尔可夫可靠性建模证明，对于SAIL IV场景，只要安全监视器满足C_monitor>0.9991的可靠性（与DAL B CMD/MON实现一致），理论上可达到每飞行小时10^-7次失效的危险目标。

Conclusion: 该框架证明可以在保持飞行关键隔离的前提下，实现无人机集群的智能行为和可认证性，为满足适航标准提供了一条可行的架构路径。

Abstract: The certification of Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) swarms for safety-critical operations requires verifiable design assurance. Airworthiness standards demand deterministic reliability, whereas multi-agent coordination algorithms execute non-deterministic models. This paper proposes a mixed-criticality architectural framework that applies SAE ARP4754B methods to swarm reconfiguration. First, a hardware-isolated Safety Monitor functions as a Run-Time Assurance (RTA) gateway, decoupling the flight-critical core from the non-deterministic Swarm Manager. Second, the monitor enforces formal safety contracts based on agent Health Vectors derived systematically from a Functional Hazard Assessment (FHA). Third, the framework propagates these Health Vectors to the collective planner to trigger fail-operational task reallocation, enabling intelligent swarm behaviors without compromising flight-critical isolation. Markov reliability modeling demonstrates that the $10^{-7}$ failures per flight hour Hazardous target is theoretically achievable for our SAIL IV scenario, provided the Safety Monitor meets $C_{monitor}>0.9991$, consistent with DAL B CMD/MON implementations.
