</details>


### [236] [Multi-Objective Deep Reinforcement Learning for Secure and Stable Power System Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20914)
*Ioannis Papadopoulos,Georgios Tsaousoglou,Johanna Vorwerk*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一控制的深度强化学习智能体，在随机负荷波动下同时维持热安全并改善系统关键模式的阻尼，实现电力系统运行的多目标优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源转型给电力系统稳定运行带来挑战，需要快速不确定性决策。现有强化学习应用多聚焦单一运行准则（如热安全或小信号稳定），但电力系统运行本质上是多目标的，目标间可能存在权衡，缺乏统一多目标控制框架。

Method: 开发统一控制的深度强化学习智能体，在随机负荷变化下维持热安全的同时，引导系统向关键模式阻尼改善的运行点移动。

Result: 与仅考虑热安全的智能体和常规策略相比，所提智能体在各运行目标间取得更好平衡，显著改善阻尼且热安全违规可忽略。高阻尼运行点在小信号和大信号扰动下均能加快振荡衰减并改善临界切除时间。

Conclusion: 统一多目标强化学习框架能有效平衡电力系统运行中的热安全与动态稳定性目标，且改善阻尼具有实际运行价值。

Abstract: The ongoing energy transition challenges the stable operation of power systems and increases the need for rapid decision-making under uncertainty. While reinforcement learning has emerged as a promising framework for power system control and operation, existing applications typically focus on a single operational criterion, such as thermal security or small-signal stability. However, power system operation is inherently multi-objective and may involve trade-offs between objectives. This paper develops a unified-control deep reinforcement learning agent that maintains thermal security under stochastic load variations while steering the system toward operating points with improved damping of the most critical mode. Compared to a thermal-security-only agent and a business-as-usual policy, the proposed agent achieves a better balance among the operational objectives considered, with notably improved damping and negligible thermal-security violations. Finally, the operational value of increased critical damping is demonstrated under small- and large-signal disturbances, where operating points with higher damping lead to faster oscillation decay and improved critical clearing times.

</details>


### [237] [Robust lane-change intention anticipation under uncertainty based on a recursive Bayesian filtering approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21132)
*Dilara Kılınç,Hendrik Kleikamp,Bruno Viti*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于运动学特征和周围观测的高速公路驾驶员变道意图预测方法。该方法采用递归贝叶斯滤波策略（可解释为隐马尔可夫模型），提出两种似然模型以处理缺失观测，并重点关注概率校准以获得可靠预测。在高D数据集上进行了实际评估，并与多种基线方法对比，特别强调了鲁棒性、时间一致性和校准性能的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变道意图预测是自动驾驶和高级驾驶辅助系统的关键技术之一，准确及时的预测对于行车安全和交通流畅性至关重要。现有方法可能在处理缺失观测和概率校准方面存在不足，因此需要开发一种鲁棒且可靠的变道意图预测方法。

Method: 该方法基于递归贝叶斯滤波策略（隐马尔可夫模型框架），利用运动学特征和周围环境观测来预测变道意图。论文提出了两种似然模型用于处理缺失观测的场景，并针对获得的概率输出进行了校准处理，以提高预测的可靠性。

Result: 在高D数据集上进行了广泛的数值实验，与多种基线方法进行了比较。实验重点评估了算法的鲁棒性、时间一致性以及概率校准质量，并对不同方法的优势和局限性进行了详细讨论。

Conclusion: 通过广泛的数值实验，论文对提出的变道意图预测方法进行了细致的性能评估，分析了各方法的优缺点，验证了该方法在鲁棒性和校准方面的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper we discuss and analyze a method for lane-change intention anticipation of drivers on highways based on kinematic features and surrounding observations. The approach makes use of a recursive Bayesian filtering strategy, which can be interpreted as a hidden Markov model. We present two models for the likelihood term and show how they can handle missing observations. An additional focus of the work lies on calibrating the obtained probabilities in order to obtain reliable predictions. The introduced approach is evaluated in practice on the highD dataset and compared to several other baseline methods. Particular emphasize is put on evaluating the robustness and temporal consistency as well as calibration of the developed algorithm. Extensive numerical experiments allow for a careful performance assessment and detailed discussion of advantages and limitations of the different methodologies.

</details>


### [238] [Loadability Limits Under Periodic Load Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21256)
*Maitraya Avadhut Desai,Gustavo Valverde,Gabriela Hug*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文指出，当部分负荷（如数据中心负荷）随时间周期性变化时，系统的稳态是一个受迫周期轨道，其载荷极限与静态载荷极限不同。经典的优化论证从平衡点直接扩展到周期映射的不动点，在极限处单值矩阵获得+1的Floquet乘子，因此典型的失稳是轨道的环向折叠而非平衡点的鞍结分岔。该极限是强迫频率的函数，静态计算无法捕获。此外，当网络雅可比矩阵沿周期轨道奇异时，奇异诱导失稳被推广到轨道。在四母线测试系统中，静态裕度2.5 p.u.在摇摆频率附近缩小至0.53 p.u.，失稳机制从电压崩溃转变为转子角折叠，且冷启动在轨道仍然存在的振幅处失败。静态分析无法复现这些效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电网互联筛查基于静态载荷极限（平衡方程失去解时的需求），但数据中心等周期性变化负荷使系统稳态成为受迫周期轨道，其载荷极限与静态不同。现有静态分析方法无法捕捉强迫频率对极限的影响，需要新的理论框架。

Method: 将经典优化论证从平衡点扩展至周期映射的不动点，通过单值矩阵的Floquet乘子分析周期性轨道的载荷极限。在极限处Floquet乘子变为+1，表现为环向折叠。同时将奇异诱导失稳从平衡点推广到周期轨道。

Result: 在四母线测试系统上，静态裕度2.5 p.u.在摇摆频率附近缩小到0.53 p.u.；失稳机制从电压崩溃切换为转子角折叠；冷启动在轨道仍存在的振幅处失败。静态分析无法复现任何这些效应。

Conclusion: 周期性负荷下的系统载荷极限是强迫频率的函数，静态计算无法捕获。需要基于周期轨道的新分析框架来正确评估含周期性负荷的电网互联容量和大负荷接入安全性。

Abstract: The static loadability limit, defined as the demand at which the equilibrium equations lose their solution, is the standard basis for interconnection screening of large new loads. This letter shows that when part of the demand varies periodically, as for data-center loads, the steady state is a forced periodic orbit whose loadability limit differs from the static one. The classical optimization argument is extended directly from equilibria to fixed points of the period map. At the limit, the monodromy matrix acquires a Floquet multiplier at +1, so the generic instability is a cyclic fold of the orbit rather than a saddle-node of equilibria. The limit is therefore a function of the forcing frequency, which no static computation can capture. Additionally, with the network Jacobian turning singular along the cycle, singularity-induced instability is extended to orbits. On a four-bus test system, a static margin of 2.5 p.u. shrinks to 0.53 p.u. near the swing frequency, the instability mechanism switches from voltage collapse to a rotor-angle fold, and cold starts fail at amplitudes where the orbit still exists. A static analysis reproduces none of these effects.

</details>


### [239] [Fault Diagnosis of Dynamic Systems Under Unknown Operating Conditions: A Condition-Guided Selective Adaptation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21302)
*Jiaming Liu,Zeyi Liu,Hongshuo Zhao,Pengyu Han,Xiao He*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种条件引导的选择性适应方法用于工业动态系统在未知工况下的故障诊断。该方法在离线阶段采用连续工况对抗学习策略增强模型泛化能力，在线部署阶段利用残差工况响应识别可靠的无标签样本来更新诊断模型。在真实齿轮箱和电机数据集上的实验表明，该方法在诊断准确率上优于现有方法，同时保持较低测试时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业动态系统在未知工况下的故障诊断面临挑战，因为工况变化导致的数据分布偏移会显著降低诊断模型在实际应用中的性能。现有方法在应对这种分布偏移方面存在不足。

Method: 提出条件引导的选择性适应框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 离线阶段采用新型连续工况对抗学习策略配合渐进训练，增强诊断模型的泛化能力；(2) 在线部署阶段利用残差工况响应从流式数据中识别可靠的无标签样本，用于更新诊断模型。

Result: 在真实世界齿轮箱和电机数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的框架在诊断准确率上优于最先进的方法，同时保持相对较低的测试时间。

Conclusion: 该框架在诊断精度和测试效率方面均表现优异，展示了其在实际工业应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis under unknown operating conditions remains challenging for dynamic industrial systems, as the distribution shift caused by changing operating conditions can significantly degrade the performance of diagnostic models in real-world applications. To address the problem, a condition-guided selective adaptation approach is proposed. Specifically, a novel continuous operating-condition adversarial learning strategy with progressive training is developed in the offline stage to enhance the generalization ability of the diagnostic model. During online deployment, residual operating-condition responses are exploited to identify reliable unlabeled samples from streaming data, which are then used to update the diagnostic model. Extensive experiments on real-world gearbox and motor datasets have demonstrated that the proposed framework outperforms state-of-the-art methods in diagnostic accuracy while maintaining relatively low test-time, showing its potential for practical industrial applications.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [Strong growth and Goldstein subgradients in piecewise smooth optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20642)
*Adrian S. Lewis,Fahaar M. Pirani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类新的增长条件（growth condition），该条件针对Lipschitz函数在局部极小值附近的性质，与基于Goldstein次梯度的算法相关。研究发现对于分段二次连续可微的目标函数，该条件在强凸情形下成立；在非凸情形下，二次增长结合活动梯度的正则性性质足以保证该条件成立。计算实验验证了各假设的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Goldstein次梯度的算法在实践中常被观察到近似线性收敛，但其理论保证尚不完整。作者希望探究支撑这种良好实践表现的新增长条件，并在分段二次连续可微的函数类中给出其成立的充分条件。

Method: 首先定义并讨论新的增长条件；然后在分段C^2目标函数框架下，分别证明：(1) 强凸情形下该条件成立；(2) 非凸情形下，二次增长加活动梯度正则性性质足以保证该条件。最后通过计算实验验证假设的必要性。

Result: 在分段二次连续可微情形下，强凸性足以保证新增长条件成立；非凸情形下，二次增长与活动梯度的正则性性质相结合可保证该条件成立。计算实验展示了各假设在实际中的重要性。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于Goldstein次梯度算法在实践中的近似线性收敛行为提供了理论依据，明确了保证新增长条件成立的充分条件及其适用边界。

Abstract: We explore a new growth condition for Lipschitz functions around local minimizers, recently proposed in the context of Goldstein-subgradient-based algorithms and their behavior in practice. On generic examples, these algorithms are often observed to converge approximately linearly. We focus on objectives that are piecewise twice continuously differentiable. In that case, the new growth condition holds when the objective is, in addition, strongly convex. In the nonconvex case, we prove that quadratic growth in conjunction with a regularity property for the active gradients suffices. Computational experiments illustrate the importance of the assumptions.

</details>


### [241] [ResiliFlow: An Open Transport World Model for Infrastructure Perception and Disaster Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20709)
*Junxiang Xu,Vinayak Dixit,S. Travis Waller,Divya Jayakumar Nair,Qianwen,Guo,Sisi Jian,Xiao Wen,Ashutosh Ashutosh,Sunhyung Yoo,Julius Secadiningrat,Jingni Guo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出ResiliFlow，一个开放的交通世界模型概念及实现平台，用于基础设施韧性、响应和恢复。平台整合了两大工作空间：灾害交通韧性分析（提供六种地图中心功能）和AI交通基础设施感知与决策支持（检测道路、人行道和路缘状况），共享八步循环周期，并通过本地助手和LLM副驾将用户问题转化为可执行工具调用，使交通模型成为可检查、可复用、问题导向的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通韧性研究往往分散在独立的数据准备脚本、网络模型、仿真工具、图像检测系统和报告中，这种碎片化使得从观察到可测试、可审查的决策之间难以跨越。作者希望构建一个端到端整合的平台以弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: ResiliFlow平台连接两个工作空间：(1)灾害交通韧性分析，提供关键道路/区域识别、恢复优先级排序、中断路由、韧性测试和场景模拟等六种地图中心功能；(2)AI交通基础设施感知与决策支持，组织街景和卫星证据并检测可见的道路状况。两空间共享"感知-预测-模型开发-验证-执行-决策-反馈-记忆"八步循环，并配备研究验证记录、本地助手和可选多供应商LLM副驾，将用户问题转化为有界工具调用。

Result: 文档记录了平台架构、代表性数学模型、界面证据和计算机视觉学习结果。示例展示了8种可见状况类别的准确识别，紧凑的错误分析证明困难案例能引导持续学习。平台展示了交通模型如何变为可检查、可复用和问题导向的系统。

Conclusion: ResiliFlow证明交通模型可以从离散分析的集合转变为可检查、可复用、问题导向的集成系统。发布版本旨在支持研究协作、公众审查和机构审查下的扩展。

Abstract: Transport resilience work is often split across separate data preparation scripts, network models, simulation tools, image inspection systems and reports. This fragmentation makes it difficult to move from an observation to a tested and reviewable decision. We introduce ResiliFlow, an open transport world model concept and an implemented platform for infrastructure resilience, response and recovery. The platform connects two workspaces. Disaster Transport Resilience Analysis provides six map-centred functions for critical-road and critical-area identification, recovery prioritisation, disruption routing, resilience testing and scenario simulation. AI-based Transport Infrastructure Perception and Decision Support organises street-level and satellite evidence, detects visible road, footpath and kerb conditions, and prepares these observations for human-reviewed intervention planning. Both workspaces share an eight-step cycle of perception, prediction, model development, verification, execution, decision, feedback and memory. Research Validation records assumptions and checks, while a local Assistant and an optional multi-provider large language model Copilot translate user questions into bounded calls to executable tools. We document the platform architecture, representative mathematical models, interface evidence and computer-vision learning results. Examples show accurate recognition across eight visible-condition classes, while compact error analysis demonstrates how difficult cases guide continued learning. ResiliFlow shows how transport models can become an inspectable, reusable and question-led system rather than a collection of disconnected analyses. The accompanying release is intended to support research collaboration, public scrutiny and extension under institutional review.

</details>


### [242] [Improving Stability and Economic Operation in Transmission Systems through Volt/VAR Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20747)
*Shuaicheng Tong,Pascal Van Hentenryck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于同伦延续方法的离散控制无功/电压优化（VVO）方法，协调有载调压变压器和电容器组等开关设备，在调度后快速修正电压与无功越限问题，实验表明该方法能在实际运行时间内产生AC可行的设定点，并降低电压偏差、无功调度和发电成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输电系统运营商经常通过AC潮流计算来校正市场出清的DC调度结果，但校正后的设定点仍可能违反电压和/or无功限制。维持安全的电压分布和充足的无功支撑需要快速且可在运行中实施的校正决策，现有方法缺乏对离散控制设备的高效协调优化。

Method: 提出一种新颖的基于同伦的延续方法来解决离散控制的Volt/VAR优化（VVO）问题，协调可切换设备（如有载调压变压器OLTC和电容器组CB）。

Result: 在IEEE、PEGASE和RTE系统上的实验表明：所提出的VVO能在实际运行时间内产生AC可行的设定点，同时降低电压偏差、无功调度量和发电成本。VVO在使用ACPF校正的DC调度作为输入时，与使用AC可行调度相比表现相当。

Conclusion: VVO可以自然地集成到现有的输电调度实践中，以加强电网运行。该方法对使用DC调度结果的场景尤为适用，能够弥补DC调度与AC物理现实之间的差距。

Abstract: Transmission system operators often reconcile market-cleared DC dispatches with AC physics through power-flow solves (ACPF), yet the resulting setpoints can still violate voltage (Volt) and reactive-power (VAR) limits. Maintaining secure voltage profiles and adequate VAR support therefore requires fast corrective decisions that are implementable in operation. This paper presents a novel homotopy-based continuation method for discrete-control Volt/VAR Optimization (VVO) that coordinates switchable devices, such as on-load tap-changing transformers (OLTCs) and capacitor banks (CBs). Experiments on IEEE, PEGASE, and RTE systems show that the proposed VVO produces AC-feasible setpoints within practical runtime, while reducing voltage deviation, VAR dispatch, and generation cost. VVO also achieves comparable performance using ACPF-adjusted DC dispatches as inputs relative to AC-feasible dispatches, indicating that it can be integrated naturally into existing transmission dispatch practices to strengthen grid operation.

</details>


### [243] [Time-Inconsistent MDPs with Entropy Regularization: Equilibrium Existence and Policy Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20811)
*Fengyuan Cao,Zhenhua Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有可数无限状态空间和无界奖励函数的无限时域时间不一致马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）。作者基于熵正则化和加权泛函分析方法，通过引入两种不同作用的权重函数构造紧致不变集，利用Schauder-Tychonoff不动点定理建立正则化均衡的存在性，并通过λ→0+的极限过程获得原问题的松弛均衡。此外，论文还研究了策略迭代算法（PIA），在加权折现结构下证明了指数收敛性和均衡唯一性，并给出了显式的遗憾估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间不一致MDP在实际应用中广泛存在（如行为经济学中的双曲贴现），但现有研究多局限于有限状态空间或有界奖励函数。本文旨在突破这些限制，处理可数无限状态空间、无界奖励以及奖励显式依赖初始时间和初始状态的更一般情形，为时间不一致MDP提供严格的均衡存在性理论和可计算的算法框架。

Method: 本文方法包含两个核心环节：(1) 理论建立：利用熵正则化转化问题，引入两个不同角色的权重函数（一个控制奖励和值函数的增长，另一个定义环境加权空间），构造乘积拓扑下的紧致不变集，应用Schauder-Tychonoff不动点定理证明正则化均衡存在；随后令熵权重λ→0+，通过紧性、Gibbs策略浓度和一致可积性论证获得原问题的松弛均衡。(2) 算法分析：在加权折现结构和足够强折现条件下，证明策略迭代算法（PIA）在加权Banach空间中的指数收敛性和正则化均衡唯一性，并将PIA误差与soft-max近似界结合，给出原问题加权ε-均衡的遗憾估计。

Result: 论文证明了正则化均衡的存在性，且不变集可在λ∈(0,1]上一致选取；通过极限论证获得了原未正则化问题的松弛均衡。在算法层面，证明了PIA在强折现条件下的指数收敛性和均衡唯一性，并推导出迭代策略构成原问题加权ε-均衡的显式遗憾界。数值实验验证了强折现下PIA的收敛性，同时给出弱折现下算法失效的反例。

Conclusion: 本文为无限状态空间、无界奖励的一般时间不一致MDP提供了完整的均衡存在性理论和收敛算法，填补了该领域在一般情形下的理论空白。数值结果揭示了折现强度对算法收敛性的关键影响，为实际应用中的参数选择提供了指导。

Abstract: We study infinite-horizon time-inconsistent Markov decision processes with a countably infinite state space and unbounded reward functions. The reward is allowed to depend explicitly on the initial time and initial state, thereby accommodating general sources of time inconsistency. We seek relaxed feedback equilibria, and our approach is based on entropy regularization and weighted functional analytic methods. With entropy regularization, we characterize a regular relaxed equilibrium through a fixed-point operator. By introducing two weight functions with distinct roles, one controlling the growth of rewards and values and the other defining the ambient weighted space, we construct a compact invariant set under a product topology and apply the Schauder-Tychonoff fixed-point theorem to establish existence of regularized equilibria. Importantly, the invariant set can be chosen uniformly for small entropy weight $λ\in(0,1]$. We then let $λ\to0+$ and show, through compactness, concentration of Gibbs policies, and uniform-integrability arguments, that a subsequential limit is a relaxed equilibrium of the original unregularized problem.
  We further study a policy iteration algorithm (PIA) for the entropy-regularized equilibrium problem. Under a weighted-discounting structure and sufficiently strong discounting, we establish exponential convergence and uniqueness of the regularized equilibrium in a suitable weighted Banach space. Combining the policy-iteration error with a quantitative soft-max approximation bound, we show that the iterated policies constitute weighted $\varepsilon$-equilibria for the original unregularized problem and derive an explicit regret estimate. A numerical example illustrating the convergence of PIA under strong discounting and a counterexample demonstrating its failure under weak discounting are also provided.

</details>


### [244] [Failure of Whole-Sequence Convergence in Variable-Smoothing Full-Splitting Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20859)
*Min Tao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了平滑化全分裂近端次梯度方法（S-FSPS）在结构化非凸非光滑分式规划中的整序列收敛性问题。作者构造了反例，证明即使所有聚点都是精确的极限提升驻点，生成序列也未必整序列收敛，首次明确展示了不可和递减平滑化不能保证整序列收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的收敛理论仅证明了存在收敛到精确极限提升驻点的子序列，但整个生成序列是否必须收敛这一问题尚未解决。作者旨在回答这个开放问题，并检验可变平滑化单循环全分裂方法是否具备整序列收敛性。

Method: 通过构造两个可容许实例来否定整序列收敛性。第一个构造中线性算子A非零且所有对偶迭代非零；第二个构造中可行集是全维的、A满行秩，且f∘K和g∘A在可行集上均非常数。利用这些构造展示原序列的聚点集为{1}×S^1且轨迹无限长。

Result: 两个构造实例中，原序列的聚点集均为{1}×S^1，轨迹无限长，但每个聚点都是精确的极限提升驻点。第一个构造还产生了对应可变平滑化单循环全分裂方法的不收敛实例，尽管其子序列收敛到精确极限驻点。

Conclusion: 这是首个明确构造，证明即使结合精确极限驻点性，不可和的递减平滑化也不能保证可变平滑化全分裂方案的整序列收敛。该结果否定了先前理论中整序列收敛的潜在猜想。

Abstract: We investigate whole-sequence convergence of the smoothing-based full-splitting proximal subgradient method (S-FSPS) for structured nonconvex and nonsmooth fractional programs, introduced as Algorithm~4.1 by Boţ, Li, and Tao (\emph{SIAM J. Optim.}, 35(4):2623--2653, 2025). The existing convergence theory establishes only the existence of subsequences converging to exact limiting lifted stationary points, leaving open whether the entire generated sequence must converge. We answer this question in the negative by constructing two admissible instances whose primal sequences have cluster set \(\{1\}\times\mathbb S^1\) and infinite-length trajectories, although every cluster point is an exact limiting lifted stationary point. In the first construction, the linear operator \(A\) is nonzero and every generated dual iterate is nonzero. In the second, the feasible set is full-dimensional, \(A\) has full row rank, and both \(f\circ K\) and \(g\circ A\) are nonconstant on the feasible set. The first construction also yields a nonconvergent example for the corresponding variable-smoothing, single-loop, full-splitting method for nonconvex and nonsmooth composite optimization, although a subsequence converges to an exact limiting stationary point. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first explicit construction showing that vanishing nonsummable smoothing, even together with exact limiting stationarity, does not imply whole-sequence convergence for variable-smoothing full-splitting schemes.

</details>


### [245] [Maximum Covering Network Design on Graphs with Low Connectivity: Dynamic Programming and Block-Cut Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20894)
*Felix Rauh,Jannik Matuschke,Hande Yaman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究最大覆盖网络设计问题（Maximum Covering Network Design Problem），即在共享预算下同时决策设施选址与网络链路升级，以最大化目标出行距离内覆盖的人口。作者提出一种精确动态规划框架，利用道路网络低连通性（存在大量割点）的特性，在树上实现无需求解器的自包含递归，在一般低连通网络上则通过割点分解嵌入MILP求解子问题。该框架在306个测试用例中，80%以上实例匹配或优于直接求解；且单次运行即可获得全预算水平的覆盖-预算曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务的规划（如医疗、应急响应、学校）不仅需要选择设施位置，还需改善连接网络（如升级易涝道路），但传统选址模型将网络视为固定且预算给定。该问题即使在最简单网络上也是难的，且规划者希望看到覆盖随预算增长的完整曲线，而非单一方案。因此需要一个能同时处理选址和网络改进、并高效生成覆盖-预算曲线的精确方法。

Method: 提出精确动态规划框架，利用道路网络低连通性（割点众多）的特性：(1) 在树上，递归自包含，每个状态只需少量简单选址与升级选择，无需求解器即可快速计算，运行时间可预测；(2) 在一般低连通网络上，框架在割点处分解问题，嵌入给定MILP公式求解各子问题，通过界面处的覆盖条件协调各子问题。该框架可在单次运行中评估所有预算水平。

Result: 在树上，对于较大预算和出行距离，所提方法优于直接求解MILP；在一般低连通网络上，将某个MILP公式单独求解与其嵌入框架后的求解进行对比，306个测试用例中超过80%的实例框架匹配或优于直接求解。由于单次运行评估所有预算水平，可免费获得完整的覆盖-预算曲线，而直接求解需将时间分散到各个预算上。

Conclusion: 该动态规划框架通过利用现实道路网络的低连通性结构，有效解决了最大覆盖网络设计问题，在计算效率上优于直接求解MILP，并能同时提供完整的覆盖-预算投资曲线，为规划者提供更有价值的决策支持。

Abstract: Planning accessible public services such as health care, emergency response, and schools often requires not only choosing where to open facilities but also improving the network that connects people to them, for example upgrading flood-prone roads in vulnerable regions. Most location models, however, take the network as fixed and the budget as given. We study the Maximum Covering Network Design Problem, in which a single budget is shared between opening facilities and upgrading weak links to maximize the population within a target travel distance of an open facility. The problem is hard even on the simplest networks, and planners usually want to see how coverage grows with the budget, not a single plan.
  We develop an exact dynamic-programming framework that exploits a property common to real road networks: their low connectivity, with many cut points whose removal disconnects the network. On trees, the recursion is self-contained: each state reduces to a few simple facility and upgrade choices that are fast to compute without a solver, giving predictable running times; for larger budgets and travel distances it outperforms solving the MILP formulation directly. On general low-connectivity networks, the framework decomposes the problem at the cut points and embeds a given MILP formulation to solve the resulting pieces, coordinating them through coverage conditions at the interfaces. This lets us compare a formulation on its own against the same formulation inside the framework: across 306 test cases the framework matches or outperforms direct solving on more than 80% of instances. Because it evaluates all budget levels in a single run, it also yields the full coverage-versus-budget curve at no extra cost, whereas direct solving must split its time across individual budgets.

</details>


### [246] [Bridging Semantics and Behavior: An Agent-Based Evolutionary Model of Topic Competition with BERTopic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20996)
*Blekanov I.,Gubar E.,Fan X*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个语言无关的计算框架，将BERTopic语义主题建模与进化博弈论及基于智能体的仿真相结合，在NetLogo中模拟微博用户通过局部模仿和支付比较更新话题偏好，并在两个真实数据集上成功复现了话题扩散趋势，揭示了微观机制（模仿与噪声容忍选择）如何产生宏观层面的集体行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上信息传播和观点演化是计算社会科学的核��挑战，但传统模型往往将内容与行为分离，未能捕捉个体认知与社会互动如何共同驱动大规模话语。作者旨在弥合语义分析与行为模拟之间的鸿沟，从而更真实地反映用户有限理性下的决策过程。

Method: 该框架包含三个关键组成部分：（1）使用BERTopic从大规模微博数据集中提取连贯的话题；（2）基于点赞、评论和转发构建统一的参与度量化指标；（3）在NetLogo平台上，智能体通过局部模仿更新话题偏好，以支付比较和行为噪声模拟现实世界中的有限理性决策。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上的实验表明，该模型成功复现了观察到的主题扩散趋势，并揭示了社会学习频率和支付敏感性如何塑造公众注意力。微观层面的机制（模仿和噪声容忍选择）能够产生涌现的宏观层面模式。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将语义分析与行为仿真联系起来，为人机交互研究做出贡献，为在线集体行为的认知和社会基础提供了见解。该框架在错误信息韧性、舆论预测和自适应内容策展方面具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Understanding how information spreads and opinions evolve on social media is a core challenge in computational social science. Users constantly face competing topics, yet their decisions to engage are shaped by social learning, perceived utility, and cognitive limits. Traditional models often separate content from behavior, missing how individual cognition and social interaction jointly drive large-scale discourse.
  We propose a language-independent computational framework that bridges semantic topic modeling with evolutionary game theory and agent-based simulation. Using BERTopic, we extract coherent topics from a large Weibo dataset. We then quantify engagement through a unified metric based on likes, comments, and reposts. In NetLogo, agents update topic preferences via local imitation, guided by payoff comparisons and behavioral noise---mirroring real-world bounded rationality.
  Tests on two real-world datasets show that our model successfully replicates observed topic diffusion trends. It highlights how social learning frequency and payoff sensitivity shape public attention. Our results demonstrate that micro-level mechanisms---imitation and noise-tolerant choice---can produce emergent macro-level patterns. This work contributes to human-computer interaction by linking semantic analysis with behavioral simulation, offering insights into the cognitive and social foundations of online collective behavior. The framework also has practical potential for misinformation resilience, opinion forecasting, and adaptive content curation.

</details>


### [247] [Optimal Sensor Placement for Output Estimation Using an Artificial Bee Colony Algorithm with Pre-filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21042)
*R. P. P. F. Goetz,Y. Dwaraga,N. van de Wouw,T. Oomen,M. M. J. van de Wal,B. Sharif,H. J. Zwart*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对3D热弹性系统的卡尔曼滤波传感器放置问题（NP-hard），提出了一种结合Gramian预滤波的新型二进制人工蜂群（NBABC）算法，在降低计算复杂度的同时实现了高效且快速收敛的传感器优化布置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器放置问题本身是NP-hard优化问题，且可行解集合随着候选位置和传感器数量的增加呈组合爆炸式增长，使得传统方法难以高效求解。

Method: 将3D热弹性系统建模为离散时间线性随机模型，采用新型二进制人工蜂群（NBABC）算法进行传感器放置优化，并使用基于Gramian的预滤波器来降低计算复杂度。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法在计算效率和收敛速度方面均表现出优异的性能。

Conclusion: NBABC算法结合Gramian预滤波是解决3D热弹性系统传感器放置问题的有效方法，能够兼顾优化效果与计算效率。

Abstract: Sensor placement for maximizing the estimation performance of the Kalman filter is an NP-hard optimization problem. Furthermore, its feasible set grows combinatorially with the candidate locations and the number of sensors. In this paper, we study this sensor placement problem for a 3D thermoelastic system modelled as a discrete-time linear stochastic model. We use the Novel Binary Artificial Bee Colony (NBABC) algorithm with a Gramian-based pre-filter to reduce the computational complexity. Our results show the efficiency and the fast convergence of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [248] [Anchored Regularized Direct Least Squares (ARDLS): Integrating Established Prioritization Operators for Priority Elicitation in the Analytic Hierarchy Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21187)
*Kevin Kam Fung Yuen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出锚定正则化直接最小二乘（ARDLS）优化模型，通过将已有的优先级算子作为理论锚点集成到正则化惩罚项中，打破数学对称性，解决直接最小二乘（DLS）方法在层次分析法（AHP）中因非凸性导致的多全局最优解和优先级排序不稳定的问题，保证收敛到唯一全局最小解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLS方法虽然直观且无需复杂变换，但在高不一致性（如循环矛盾）条件下，其非凸性质会产生多个不同的全局最小值，导致优先级排序不稳定且严重依赖初始猜测，这是AHP决策模型的结构性缺陷。

Method: 提出ARDLS优化模型，将归一化技术、特征向量法（Eigenvector）、奇异值分解（SVD）、余弦最大化以及伪逆Gram矩阵（加权最小二乘的闭式解）等已有优先级算子作为理论锚点，集成到正则化惩罚项中，系统性地打破数学对称性，倾斜优化景观以确保收敛到唯一全局最小值。

Result: 综合数值实验和模拟验证表明，ARDLS框架在保证严格数学唯一性的同时，成功降低了各既有优先级算子之间的均方根误差（RMSE）。

Conclusion: ARDLS可能是AHP在众多应用领域中的理想替代方案，它结合了DLS的直观性与数学上的唯一收敛保证。

Abstract: Pairwise reciprocal matrices are fundamental to the Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP), a decision-making model. While the Direct Least Squares (DLS) method provides an intuitive mechanism for deriving priority vectors without complex transformations, the DLS provides multiple solutions. Under high levels of inconsistency, such as cyclic contradictions, this non-convexity yields multiple distinct global minima, resulting in unstable priority rankings that critically depend on initial algorithmic guesses. To overcome this structural deficiency, this paper introduces the Anchored Regularized Direct Least Squares (ARDLS) optimization model. ARDLS integrates uniquely determined established prioritization operators, such as normalization techniques, the Eigenvector method, Singular Value Decomposition, Cosine Maximization, and the Pseudo-Inverse Gram Matrix (the closed-form solution of Weighted Least Squares), as theoretical anchors within a regularization penalty. This integration systematically breaks mathematical symmetries, tilting the optimization landscape to guarantee convergence upon a single, unique global minimum. Comprehensive numerical experiments and simulations validate that the ARDLS framework successfully reduces root mean square error among established priority operators, while guaranteeing strict mathematical uniqueness. The proposed ARDLS may be the ideal alternative for the AHP applied to many application domains.

</details>


### [249] [Primal Acceleration of Newton's Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21359)
*Nikita Doikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的直接加速牛顿法，用于最小化具有Lipschitz连续Hessian的凸函数。该算法仅使用原始变量，每次迭代只需一次线性求解，通过简单的预定参数选择，实现了O(1/k^3)的全局收敛速率。这是该问题类别中首个在不求解辅助非线性正则化子问题、不进行非线性参数搜索或不使用对偶外梯度修正的情况下达到此速率并仅依赖一次线性系统求解的二阶方法。该方法还支持Hessian-free实现（使用非精确线性求解器）同时保持快速全局速率，并进一步推广到Bregman散度（任意几何）和复合优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的二阶方法（如三次正则化）虽能达到快速全局收敛，但通常需要求解昂贵的非线性正则化子问题或进行额外修正，计算开销大。作者希望设计一种更简单、更高效的方法——仅需一次线性求解即可获得最优收敛速率，降低每轮迭代的计算负担，同时保持理论上的最优全局收敛保证。

Method: 提出一种直接加速牛顿法，核心创新在于：(1) 仅使用原始变量（不引入对偶变量）；(2) 每次迭代仅需一次线性系统求解；(3) 采用简单的预定参数选择（无需非线性搜索）；(4) 通过适当的加速/动量机制与牛顿步结合获得O(1/k^3)速率；(5) 支持非精确线性求解（Hessian-free实现）；(6) 通过Bregman散度推广到任意几何，并扩展到复合优化问题。

Result: 在具有Lipschitz连续Hessian的凸函数最小化问题上，实现了O(1/k^3)的全局功能残差收敛速率，且每次迭代仅需一次线性系统求解。这是该问题类别中首个满足此条件的方法。数值实现上支持Hessian-free和不精确求解，并保持理论速率不变。方法成功推广到Bregman几何和复合优化场景。

Conclusion: 本文填补了二阶方法在全局最优收敛速率与计算效率之间的空白，证明了一种简单、可直接实现且仅需单次线性求解的加速牛顿方法即可达到O(1/k^3)的最优速率。该工作为大规模凸优化提供了更具实用价值的二阶算法框架，并展示了通过Bregman散度进行几何推广的通用性。

Abstract: We develop a new direct accelerated Newton method for minimizing convex functions with Lipschitz continuous Hessian. The algorithm uses only primal variables and performs just one linear solve per iteration. With a simple predetermined choice of parameters, it achieves the global convergence rate of $O(1/k^3)$ in terms of the functional residual. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first second-order method for this problem class attaining this rate while relying solely on one linear system solve per iteration (without solving auxiliary nonlinear regularized subproblems, such as cubic regularization, performing nonlinear parameter searches, or using dual extragradient corrections). Our method can be implemented in a Hessian-free way, using an inexact linear system solver, while preserving the fast global rate. We further extend our construction to arbitrary geometry through Bregman divergence, and to composite optimization problems.

</details>
